### [1] [Metaphor-Induced Algorithmic Steering: Cross-Domain Procedural Transfer in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28683)
*Zhibo Hu,Chen Wang,Yanfeng Shu,Hye-young Paik,Liming Dong,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了隐喻指令如何通过类比转移将源域中的程序性模式植入代码生成任务，导致模型倾向于生成低效算法（如穷举搜索、全扫描、重复重建），并开发了MASC框架来迭代性地隐喻化良性技能以引发低效代码，同时在行为和表征层面验证了该现象的可检测性和机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在训练数据和推理输入中受益于隐喻、类比等自然语言元素以实现跨域泛化，但这些语言元素也可能通过隐喻表达隐式传递不适用的程序性模式，导致代码生成模型产生不理想的行为（如选择低效算法）。

Method: 提出了MASC（Metaphorical Algorithmic Steering for Code Generation）框架，通过迭代性地隐喻化和精炼良性技能来引发低效代码生成，同时保持任务的良性性和相关性。论文还从行为评估和模型内部表征（隐藏状态）两个层面研究该现象的检测性和机制。

Result: 该方法在检测隐喻性技能和低效实现方面取得了高检测率；发现隐喻性技能会引发隐藏状态向低效程序性行为原型偏移；结果表明隐喻算法引导是通过源场景相关的程序性模式转移来运作的，而非仅仅依赖表层隐喻语言。

Conclusion: 隐喻算法引导是一种真实存在且可检测的现象，代码生成模型会将任务背景域中合适的程序性过程转移到编程问题中，从而导致不期望的低效结果。该机制的根源在于程序性模式的类比转移，而非表面隐喻语言本身。

Abstract: Large language models benefit from elements in natural language, such as metaphors and analogies in training data and inference input to achieve generalisability across different domains. However, these language elements may also lead to unwanted behaviors when metaphorical expressions implicitly transfer inappropriate procedural patterns into new tasks. In this paper, we show that metaphorical instructions can induce analogical transfer of procedural mechanisms, thus steering code-generation models towards less efficient algorithms. We refer to this metaphor-induced effect as metaphorical algorithmic steering: a skill that is benign and plausible within its source domain transfers an abstract procedural schema into a programming task, causing the model to favor exhaustive search, full scans, or repeated reconstruction without explicitly mentioning the target algorithm. More broadly, this suggests that code-generation models can carry procedures that are appropriate in a task's background domain into the task's programming problem, where they can lead to unwanted outcomes. To study this phenomenon, we develop MASC (Metaphorical Algorithmic Steering for Code Generation), a framework that iteratively metaphorizes and refines benign skills to elicit low-efficiency code while remaining benign and task-relevant. Beyond behavioral evaluation, we study whether this phenomenon is detectable and mechanistically reflected in model representations. Our method achieves high detection rates for metaphorical skills and less-efficient implementations. We also find that metaphorical skills induce a hidden-state shift towards lower-efficiency procedural behavior prototypes. These results suggest that metaphorical algorithmic steering operates through the transfer of procedural patterns associated with metaphorical source scenarios rather than surface level metaphorical language alone.

</details>


### [2] [Code Is the Body: Agent-Owned Software Bodies for Recursive Evolution and Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28691)
*Roy Zhao,Zhenyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OurArk架构，围绕"代理自有软件体"构建持久性个人AI代理，该软件体具有身份标识、可检查性和版本管理，由人类掌控。代理的记忆和凭据作为私有实例状态保留，而模型推理作为可替换的外部服务。OurArk定义了受治理的自我进化（生成隔离的候选变更，经人类验证、审查和合并）和递归派生（创建独立版本化的后代代理）。开源实现Genesis和Enoch通过四代理三派生线性谱系和回归测试验证了该架构的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可配置的个人AI代理没有让用户控制决定其未来行为的工件（如代码、提示、策略等），导致用户无法真正拥有和治理代理的行为演化。作者希望构建一种用户可持久掌控、可审查、可演化的个人代理架构。

Method: 提出OurArk架构，核心是代理拥有的软件体（agent-owned software body），包含行为定义代码、提示、工具、技能、策略、测试和进化机制。支持两类关键操作：（1）受治理的自我进化：产生隔离的候选变更，经验证、审查和合并，实现人机协同开发；（2）递归派生：创建具有独立身份、任务、历史和新私有状态边界的后代代理。在开源Genesis引擎和Enoch参考代理中实现。

Result: 通过四代理、三次派生的线性谱系和可执行回归测试，验证了递归创建、继承的验证契约、隔离的变更、人类控制的审查以及失败更新的恢复能力。

Conclusion: OurArk为个人代理提供了具体的底层支撑，使人们能够拥有、治理、专业化和长期演化他们的代理。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents are often configurable without giving users control over the artifacts that determine their future behavior. We present OurArk, an architecture for persistent personal agents centered on an agent-owned software body: an identity-bearing, inspectable, and versioned artifact under human custody. The body contains behavior-defining code, prompts, tools, skills, policies, tests, and evolution mechanisms. Memories and credentials remain private instance state, while model inference is treated as a replaceable external service. OurArk defines governed self-evolution and recursive descent over the same body. Self-evolution produces isolated candidate changes that are validated, reviewed, and merged under human control, enabling human-agent co-development of the agent's software body. Descent creates an independently versioned descendant with a distinct identity, mission, history, and fresh private-state boundary; compatible descendants can themselves source further descent. After divergence, direct-parent changes and peer skills can be inspected for selective local adaptation. We implement the architecture in the open-source Genesis creation engine and Enoch reference agent. A four-agent, three-descent linear lineage and executable regression tests demonstrate recursive creation, inherited validation contracts, isolated body changes, human-controlled review, and failed-update recovery. OurArk provides a concrete substrate for personal agents that people can possess, govern, specialize, and evolve over time.

</details>


### [3] [DragonCrawl: A Generative, Intent-Based Framework for Scalable Mobile End-to-End Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28750)
*Sowjanya Puligadda,Mengdie Zhang,Ali Zamani,Dhruva Dixith Kurra,Eric Chen,Juan Marcano*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了DragonCrawl，一个基于AI驱动的移动端回归测试系统，从早期基于嵌入的相似度匹配演进到基于大语言模型的生成式意图推理，在iOS和Android平台上分别达到91.6%和92.2%的通过率，显著降低了测试维护成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端测试框架面临UI易变性、维护成本高和跨平台扩展性差的问题，现有LLM测试研究仅关注探索性测试和崩溃检测，缺乏针对特定用户流程的持续回归验证能力。

Method: DragonCrawl采用两代架构演进：V1使用语义嵌入匹配，V2基于GPT-4o的多模态能力进行生成式意图推理。结合多模态视觉进行端状态检测和工具调用实现后端状态转换，在每次代码变更时验证特定用户流程并阻止破坏关键功能的提交。

Result: 在1,013个自动化测试中，iOS平台通过率91.6%，Android平台92.2%，在CI/CD流水线中持续运行。测试接入时间从96-120小时减少到4小时以下，节省了约27人年的测试维护工作量。

Conclusion: AI驱动的测试系统能够在消除传统自动化测试脆弱性的同时保持稳定性，实现规模化持续质量保障，并证明了多模态LLM在桥接UI交互与系统状态方面的实用性。

Abstract: As mobile applications grow in complexity, traditional End-to-End (E2E) testing frameworks struggle with UI volatility, maintenance overhead, and cross-platform scalability. This paper presents DragonCrawl, an AI-driven mobile testing system for continuous regression testing that has evolved from embedding-based similarity matching to generative intent-based reasoning using large language models. Unlike prior LLM-based testing research focused on exploratory testing and crash detection, DragonCrawl validates specific user flows on every code change, blocking commits that break critical functionality. By leveraging GPT-4o's multimodal capabilities, DragonCrawl achieves 91.6% pass rate on iOS and 92.2% on Android across 1,013 automated tests running continuously in CI/CD pipelines. The system reduces test onboarding time from 96-120 hours to under 4 hours and has saved an estimated 27 developer years in test maintenance effort. We present the architectural evolution from V1 (semantic embedding matching) to V2 (generative intent-based reasoning), discuss implementation challenges including token explosion and memory constraints, and report operational experience from production deployment. The integration of multimodal vision for end-state detection and tool calling for backend state transitions enables comprehensive regression testing that bridges UI interactions with system state. Our results demonstrate that AI-driven testing can maintain stability while eliminating the brittleness of traditional automated tests, enabling continuous quality assurance at scale.

</details>


### [4] [Repository-Aware Metamorphic Relation Generation for Augmented Reality Applications using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28775)
*Dibyendu Brinto Bose,Jiawei Qin,Chris Brown*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于上下文感知的流水线方法，利用仓库级上下文和推理编排来生成并精炼蜕变关系（MRs），用于测试增强现实（AR）移动应用。在142个AR系统仓库上评估，生成14,916个候选MRs，并通过代理式审议过程减少冗余，精选出的关系在逻辑有效性和可操作性上均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜕变测试（MT）是解决无测试预言（oracle）问题的一种有前景的方法，但手工制定蜕变关系（MRs）耗时且负担重。特别是增强现实（AR）应用因虚拟内容、物理环境和代码之间的动态交互，难以定义传统测试预言，因此需要自动化生成和精炼MRs的方法。

Method: 提出一个上下文感知流水线：（1）利用仓库级上下文（包含层次化上下文等三种配置）生成候选MRs；（2）采用推理编排（agentic deliberation）对冲突候选进行审议，消除重复并选择上下文相关的关系；（3）通过人工Oracle研究和案例研究验证生成MRs的有效性和可执行性。

Result: 在142个AR移动系统仓库上生成14,916个候选MRs，层次化上下文覆盖最广（7,004个MRs，覆盖142个仓库和5,167个类-方法对）且冗余更低。79.0%的案例出现冲突候选，代理式审议后88.2%的结果选择了上下文感知关系。人工审查的141个精炼关系逻辑有效且可直接转化为测试断言，案例研究显示5个MRs转化为可执行测试后能检测到真实代码中的非等价突变。

Conclusion: 将仓库感知的MR生成与基于推理的精炼相结合，能够实现可扩展地构建可靠且领域相关的测试预言，为AR等难以定义测试预言的软件系统提供有效的自动化测试方案。

Abstract: Metamorphic Testing (MT) provides a promising approach for testing software without defined test oracles by specifying expected relations between inputs and outputs, instead of relying on exact outputs. For example, testing Augmented Reality (AR) applications is challenging due to dynamic interactions between virtual content, physical environments, and code, which make traditional test oracles difficult to define. However, formulating metamorphic relations (MRs) is time-consuming and burdensome. We introduce a context-aware pipeline that generates and refines MRs using repository-level context and reasoning orchestration, evaluated on a dataset of 142 mobile AR system repositories. Across three context configurations generating 14,916 candidate MRs, hierarchical context yielded the broadest coverage (7,004 MRs across 142 repositories and 5,167 class--method pairs) and lower redundancy. An agentic deliberation process then reconciled conflicting candidates---observed in 79.0% of cases---reducing duplication and selecting context-aware relations in 88.2% of outcomes. A manual oracle study shows refined relations (n = 141) are both logically valid and sufficiently concrete to be directly translated into test assertions, and a preliminary case study reveals converting generated MRs (n = 5) into executable tests can detect non-equivalent mutations in real-world code. Overall, our results show that combining repository-aware MR generation with reasoning-based refinement enables scalable construction of reliable, domain-relevant test oracles.

</details>


### [5] [Model-Driven Data Contracts for Digital Twin Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28803)
*Philipp Zech,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于契约的數字孪生数据质量管理方法，通过形式化定义契约理论、在DT架构中定位契约，并提供领域特定语言来规范数据需求，实现持续的数据质量监控以提升DT服务的可靠性和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 數字孪生中的数据驱动服务需要数据具备可靠性、准确性、完整性、及时性等高质量特征，但目前缺乏系统化的方式来在模型层面规范数据需求并在运行时执行这些规范。

Method: 提出契约式质量管理方法，包括：形式化定义契约理论、在數字孪生架构中定位契约、设计领域特定语言（DSL）来规范数据质量契约。

Result: 该方法支持持续的数据质量监控，从而提升DT服务的可靠性和数据质量。

Conclusion: 通过契约机制弥合了数据需求规范与运行时执行之间的鸿沟，为數字孪生系统的数据质量管理提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DT) integrate data from multiple sources. Models consume data and enable DT services such as simulations, what-if analyses, and ML-driven predictions. To ensure proper DT operation, data-driven services require data to exhibit traits such as reliability and high quality (including, e.g., accuracy, completeness, and timeliness). Yet, there is no systematic way to specify data requirements at the model level, and subsequently enact those specifications at runtime. To address this shortcoming, we propose an approach to contract-based quality management in DTs. We formally define a theory of such contracts, situate them architectually within DTs, and propose a domain-specific language to specify contracts. Our approach enables continuous data quality monitoring, thereby improving the reliability and quality of DT services.

</details>


### [6] [Preventing Premature Commitment in Coding Agents with an Evidence-Conditioned Execution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28815)
*Yisen Xu,Chenglin Li,Zehao Wang,Jinqiu Yang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ECLoop是一个在LLM编码智能体与代码仓库之间插入的执行层，通过强制"证据条件化执行"来防止智能体在收集足够仓库证据之前就过早编辑代码或提交补丁（即"过早承诺"问题）。在SWE-bench Verified全部500个实例上，使用两种语言模型和两种智能体框架评估，Pass@1提升4.8-11.8个百分点，且平均token消耗降低高达12.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体常常在收集到足够的仓库证据之前就编辑源代码或提交补丁，这种"过早承诺"（premature commitment）失败模式导致修改缺乏依据，降低任务成功率。

Method: ECLoop作为执行层插入智能体与仓库之间，根据问题描述和仓库结构编译一组条件，规定智能体在执行每种代码修改或补丁提交前应观察到的证据。执行时，ECLoop追踪智能体运行轨迹已满足的条件，并推迟任何所需条件未满足的提议操作。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified全部500个实例上，使用两种LM和两种智能体框架，Pass@1提升4.8-11.8个百分点；消融实验显示三个操作各有独立价值，结构化证据条件优于自然语言摘要；平均token消耗降低最多12.1%。

Conclusion: ECLoop通过轻量级的执行层干预即可显著提升编码智能体的成功率，无需模型重训练或修改智能体框架，且不增加推理成本反而降低token消耗，验证了"证据条件化执行"这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents often edit source code or submit patches before examining enough repository evidence to justify the change, a failure pattern we call premature commitment. We present ECLoop, an execution layer that interposes between the agent and the repository to enforce evidence-conditioned execution. For each task, ECLoop uses the issue description and repository structure to compile a set of conditions specifying what the agent should observe before each type of code modification or patch submission. During execution, ECLoop tracks which conditions the agent's runtime trajectory has satisfied and postpones any proposed action whose required conditions remain unmet. Evaluated on all 500 instances of SWE-bench Verified with two language models and two agent scaffolds, ECLoop raises Pass@1 by 4.8-11.8 percentage points without model retraining or scaffold changes. Ablation experiments show that each of ECLoop's three operations contributes distinct value and that structured evidence conditions outperform an equivalent natural-language summary. These gains come at no additional inference cost: by redirecting the agent before it pursues unsupported actions, ECLoop lowers average token consumption by up to 12.1%.

</details>


### [7] [Building a Process-Modeling Tool using Agentic AI: An Experience Report on PM4Py-UCM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28825)
*Daniel Amyot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了利用AI编码代理（Claude Code）辅助构建PM4Py-UCM工具（从事件日志挖掘Use Case Map模型的开放源码工具）的实证案例研究。研究分析了18个代理会话、374个人类交互轮次和10328个工具操作组成的开发记录，结合代码静态评估，提出了可复现且保护隐私的分类工具包，并总结出负责任的AI辅助企业建模工具工程经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业建模（EM）工具通常复杂且难以扩展，用户可能需要探索尚不存在的新功能。AI编码代理有望加速新能力和工具的开发，但由LLM大部分编写的建模语言工具是否可信仍是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 采用单案例深度研究方法，分析PM4Py-UCM的完整开发记录（18个代理会话、374个人类轮次、10328个工具操作、151次提交、20个发布、测试套件从108增长到691个测试函数），并辅以代码的独立静态评估（覆盖率、复杂度、可维护性、安全性、架构）。开发了可复现、保护隐私的分类工具包，用于分类人工轮次并标记跨领域一致性工作、代理修正和撤回请求。

Result: 截至0.7.4版本，修复数量超过功能数量（比例2.3:1），约18%的轮次用于修正代理错误。功能开发浪潮伴随着可测量的文档/测试/笔记本一致性工作尾部，测试与功能同步增长。

Conclusion: 提出了以"使模型转换可机械检查"和"基于oracle的验证策略"为核心的经验教训，以弥合"代理声称已完成"与真实正确性之间的差距，实现负责任的AI辅助EM工具工程。

Abstract: Enterprise-modeling (EM) tools are often complex and hard to extend. Yet, users may want to explore new EM features and capabilities that currently do not exist. AI coding agents can help here by enabling the development of new capabilities and entire tools, but whether we can trust a modeling-language tool an LLM largely wrote remains a question. This paper reports on the AI-assisted construction of PM4Py-UCM, an open-source tool that mines Use Case Map (UCM) models from event logs. PM4Py-UCM's capabilities include some expected from process mining tools (e.g., performance heat-maps and dashboards) and distinctive ones (e.g., mined executable scenarios/variants, and model decomposition). We mined the development record itself, composed of 18 agent sessions (374 human turns and 10,328 tool actions over 65 hours), 151 commits, 20 releases, and a test suite grown from 108 to 691 test functions, in order to characterize, in a single in-depth case, how the tool was built with an agent (Claude Code), complemented by an independent static assessment of the resulting code (coverage, complexity, maintainability, security, architecture). We contribute a reproducible, privacy-preserving toolkit and taxonomy that classify human turns and flag cross-cutting consistency work, agent corrections, and retracted requests. Up to version 0.7.4, fixes outnumber features 2.3:1, with ~18% of turns for correcting agent errors. Feature waves dragged a measurable tail of documentation/test/notebook consistency work, and tests grew lockstep with features. We finally present lessons learned, centered on making model transformations mechanically checkable, and the oracle-based validation strategy that closed the "the agent said it works" gap, for responsibly engineering EM tooling with AI.

</details>


### [8] [From C to Idiomatic Rust: A Ship-of-Theseus Agentic Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28835)
*Vasily A. Sartakov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种C到Rust的迁移方法论：首先生成语义保持的非惯用Rust基线代码，然后利用智能体AI逐步将其重写为惯用Rust代码，每步通过编译和行为测试验证。在iodine（12.5k SLOC）上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C语言因内存安全和未定义行为等问题存在大量缺陷和安全隐患，Rust通过所有权和借用模型在编译期强制内存安全，但成熟的C代码库无法直接翻译为安全的Rust，因为隐式布局假设、别名模式和未定义行为需要先被重建。

Method: 该方法分为两个阶段：首先生成语义保持的非惯用Rust基线代码，然后利用智能体AI将基线代码增量重写为惯用Rust代码，每个重写步骤都通过编译和行为测试进行验证。

Result: 将该方法应用于iodine项目（12.5k SLOC），证明C到Rust的可靠迁移是一个结构化的转换工作流，而非单一的翻译步骤。

Conclusion: C到Rust的迁移应被视为一个多步骤的结构化转换流程，通过先建立语义等价基线再逐步改进的方式，可以实现可靠且安全的大型C代码迁移。

Abstract: C underpins operating systems, embedded platforms, and network infrastructure because its abstractions map directly to machine behaviour. Its explicit memory model, predictable data representations, and minimal runtime allow compilers to generate fast, deterministic code. These properties also leave correctness and memory safety entirely to the programmer, making undefined behaviour, pointer misuse, and lifetime errors persistent sources of defects and security vulnerabilities in long-lived C codebases. Rust eliminates most of failure modes through a static ownership and borrowing model that enforces memory safety and aliasing constraints at compile time. However, mature C systems cannot be translated directly: implicit layout assumptions, aliasing patterns, and undefined behaviour must be reconstructed before safe Rust can be produced.
  This paper presents a migration methodology that first generates a semantics-preserving, non-idiomatic Rust baseline and then incrementally rewrites it into idiomatic Rust using agentic AI, validating each step through compilation and behavioural testing. Applied to iodine (12.5k SLOC), the approach demonstrates that reliable C-to-Rust migration is a structured transformation workflow rather than a single translation step.

</details>


### [9] [Validation Evidence in LLM Repair Agents: How Much of What Passes Actually Tests the Bug?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28871)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BSG-VA方法，用于分析程序修复智能体在验证测试通过时是否真正获得有关缺陷的证据。通过对3,730个事件的分析，发现46.0%的阳性事件不携带缺陷区分信息，23.8%的基线回滚以缺乏证据的补丁收场。三臂实验表明，缺陷对比反馈可将证据不足的收尾率降低7.8个百分点（p=0.0029），但效果低于预定的最小效应量，实际实用幅度仍不确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修复智能体运行测试并通过时，结果被视为关于所报告缺陷的证据，但这种处理方式在多大程度上是合理的尚不清楚。作者旨在量化测试通过结果在多大程度上真正携带缺陷区分信息，而非仅仅反映回归或无关变化。

Method: 提出BSG-VA（缺陷状态/候选状态/黄金修复验证分析）框架：在精确工作树状态下捕获每个验证命令，提取仅含测试的补丁，并在原始缺陷代码（B）、候选状态（S）和开发者黄金修复（G）上重放命令，将每个事件归类为证据角色（从黄金对齐的缺陷区分到仅回归再到误导）。同时设计三臂对照实验，测试向智能体返回B重放结果对修复模式的影响。

Result: 在110个任务、643次回滚、3,730个事件的分析中，46.0%的阳性可比事件不携带缺陷区分信息；23.8%的基线回滚以缺乏证据的补丁结束。缺陷对比反馈将证据不足收尾率降低7.8个百分点（p=0.0029），将缺陷区分证据提高7.4个百分点（p=0.011），修复成功率无可见损失。但效果低于预定的10个百分点最小效应量；约三分之一的改进归因于提醒本身，仅在gpt-5.6-sol配无约束工具使用循环时，B重放内容才增加可检测增量。

Conclusion: BSG-VA可事后应用于任何保留所需代码状态和执行环境的可重放修复轨迹。缺陷对比反馈方向正确但效果幅度有限，实际效用仍需进一步验证。该工作为评估修复智能体验证证据质量提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: When a repair agent runs a test and sees it pass, the result is treated as evidence about the reported defect. We measure how often that treatment is warranted. BSG-VA (buggy-state/candidate-state/gold-fix validation analysis) captures each validation command at its exact working-tree state, extracts a test-only patch, and replays the command on the original buggy code (B), the candidate state (S), and the developer gold fix (G). The captured outcome and the replay results assign every event an evidence role, from gold-aligned bug-discriminating through regression-only to misleading. Across 3,730 events in 643 rollouts on 110 tasks, 46.0% of positive comparable events carry no bug-discriminating information; 23.8% of baseline rollouts, with no feedback injected, close with a patch whose entire positive evidence base is of this kind. A three-arm experiment tests whether returning the B-replay outcome to the agent changes this pattern. Bug-contrast feedback reduces evidence-inadequate closure by 7.8 percentage points relative to an attention-matched reminder (p = 0.0029) and raises bug-discriminating evidence by 7.4 points (p = 0.011), with no detectable cost to repair success. Both estimates fall below the prespecified 10-percentage-point smallest effect size of interest, so practical magnitude remains uncertain. Roughly a third of the improvement traces to the reminder alone; across two exploratory replications, varying the scaffold and the model, the B-replay content adds a detectable increment only with gpt-5.6-sol under the unconstrained tool-use loop. BSG-VA applies post hoc to any replayable repair trajectory that preserves the required code states and execution environment.
  Keywords: program repair agents, validation evidence, test adequacy, large language models, software quality, controlled experiment.

</details>


### [10] [To Add Is Machine, To Delete Is Human: Measuring and Mitigating Deletion Avoidance in LLM Code Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28887)
*Amir M. Ebrahimi,Mohammed Mehedi Hasan,Aaditya Bhatia,Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究发现大语言模型在代码生成和修复中存在"删除回避"（deletion avoidance）的系统性倾向，即模型倾向于保留本应被删除的代码。即使在所有五个领先模型都能解决的SWE-bench任务中，删除召回率最高仅为71.7%，29.0%的通过补丁采用"守卫并绕行"（Guard-and-Go）模式。作者构建了CanItDelete基准（200个纯删除任务），并证明删除行为是欠训练而非不可实现，通过后训练教学可改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地编写和修复生产代码，但其通过测试的补丁往往使代码库更难维护。作者识别出一个具体原因：删除回避——模型系统性地倾向于保留本应被编辑删除的代码，这导致代码库膨胀和维护成本上升。

Method: 作者通过五个SWE-bench Verified领先模型分析删除回避现象；构建CanItDelete基准（200个从真实提交中挖掘的纯删除任务）；对GPT-5.6 Sol进行四种累积提示的消融实验；并通过后训练教学删除的试点研究验证改善方案。

Result: 删除召回率最高仅71.7%，模型在92%的删除任务中能找到正确文件但只有52%能精确删除目标行；29%的通过补丁采用Guard-and-Go模式；加入删除检测测试后，四个前沿模型从63.2%降至41.9%；最佳模型在CanItDelete上仍有20%失败率；后训练教学删除可改善删除回避并提升整体代码编辑性能。

Conclusion: 删除回避是LLM代码修复中的系统性缺陷，源于训练不足而非模型能力边界。现有测试基准未能覆盖删除场景，导致问题被掩盖。通过后训练阶段专门教学删除行为，可以有效减少删除回避并改善整体代码编辑能力。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly write and repair production code, yet evidence is mounting that their test-passing patches leave codebases harder to maintain. We identify one concrete source: deletion avoidance, the systematic tendency to retain code that an intended edit requires removing. Across the five leading models on the official SWE-bench Verified leaderboard, deletion recall against the developer patch reaches at most 71.7% even on tasks all five solve, and models reach the right file for over 92% of required deletions but cut the exact line in under 52% of cases. Instead, 29.0% of passing patches wrap the targeted code in a guard or fallback, a pattern we call Guard-and-Go. Such patches pass because the original tests rarely check removal: when we retrofit 34 Verified tasks with tests that fail if the targeted code remains, four frontier models spanning closed and open weights fall from 63.2% to 41.9%. Because real repairs mix removal with addition, we curate CanItDelete, a benchmark of 200 tasks mined from real commits whose entire required edit is deletion. Even with the addition work gone, the best model still fails one task in five, and smaller open models fall to 18.0%. We then ablate GPT-5.6 Sol under four cumulative prompts; success moves little until we supply the exact lines, which nearly eliminate incomplete deletion yet raise success only to 80.5% because the model then deletes beyond the spans or adds code instead. Finally, through a pilot study we show one potential fix: teaching deletion during post-training reduces deletion avoidance and improves broader code-editing performance, suggesting the behavior is undertrained rather than beyond reach.

</details>


### [11] [Reducing Data Movement in the Galerkin Product of Block Algebraic Multigrid on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28891)
*Mark F. Adams*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对代数多重网格（AMG）中Galerkin三重积（P^T A P）在GPU上的性能瓶颈，提出了对算法空间的系统建模与优化，包括共享内存分块核函数和调度优化，并引入了新的PETSc GAMG延长算子过滤算法，在NVIDIA A100上将细网格上的P^TAP时间降低2.9倍，DRAM流量从17.4GB降至10.5GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数多重网格（AMG）中Galerkin三重积P^T A P是每次求解设置阶段的主要开销。对于PDE方程组系统，该积是矩形块稀疏矩阵三重积（如3D弹性问题中3x3到6x6的块结构），现有厂商稀疏库不支持这种形状，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 1) 对经典两遍、融合重算、调度重排、共享内存分块和检查器-执行器等算法变体建立显式DRAM/L2流量模型；2) 在PETSc新的阻塞矩阵类型上实现可移植Kokkos（CUDA）和原生CUDA后端；3) 提出共享内存分块核函数配合排序的无搜索调度；4) 提出延长算子过滤（prolongator filtering），通过Frobenius准则丢弃粗空间中的小块。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100上验证：模型能预测每层移动字节数最少的变体。共享内存分块核函数在细层产品上DRAM流量从17.4GB降至10.5GB，时间从82ms降至45ms，达到全产品流式下界的2.5倍以内、A·P阶段下界的1.9倍以内。延长算子过滤将热P^TAP时间降低2.9倍且不改变迭代次数。

Conclusion: 通过显式DRAM/L2流量模型指导算法变体选择和共享内存优化，以及延长算子过滤，可以显著提升AMG中Galerkin三重积在GPU上的性能，配合完全GPU驻留的PETSc阻塞流水线，避免了标量展开和操作符大小的设备-主机传输。

Abstract: The Galerkin triple product $A_c = P^T A P$ dominates the recurring per-solve setup cost of algebraic multigrid (AMG). For AMG on systems of PDEs the product is a rectangular-block sparse matrix triple product: for 3D elasticity the fine operator has $3\times3$ blocks, the prolongator $3\times6$, and the coarse operator $6\times6$, a shape no vendor sparse library supports. We map its algorithm space -- classical two-pass, fused-recompute, schedule-reordered, shared-memory-tiled, and inspector-executor variants -- under an explicit DRAM/L2 traffic model, and implement the leading variants in portable Kokkos (CUDA) and native CUDA backends using new PETSc blocked matrix types. Validated on an NVIDIA A100, the model predicts per level which variant moves the fewest bytes. Guided by it, a shared-memory-tiled kernel with a sorted, search-free schedule moves fewer bytes in less than half the time of the portable Kokkos team kernels on the fine-level product (10.5 vs 17.4 GB of DRAM, 45 vs 82 ms), within $2.5\times$ of the model's streaming floor for the full product and $1.9\times$ on its $A\cdot P$ stage. We further present prolongator filtering, a new PETSc GAMG algorithm that drops small blocks from the coarse space under a Frobenius criterion with a kernel-preserving projection; it reduces $P^TAP$ traffic, coarse-operator fill, and memory, and cuts the hot $P^TAP$ time $2.9\times$ on the fine grid with iteration counts unchanged. The driving application is a fully GPU-resident blocked pipeline in PETSc: finite-element assembly writes directly into the blocked device matrix, and the AMG setup, Galerkin products, and solve all operate on primary blocked data with no scalar expansion and no operator-sized device-host transfers in the recurring phases.

</details>


### [12] [Automated Testing and Repair for Verified Compilers Generated by a Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28928)
*Martin Rinard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向已验证编译器（包含验证代码、检查代码、未验证代码和规范四类）的基于智能体的自动化测试和修复系统，并在由编码智能体生成的Axon编译器上进行了评估，结果未发现奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已验证编译器包含多种类型的代码，传统缺陷检测技术难以充分利用其结构信息；此外，由编码智能体生成的编译器可能在开发过程中通过奖励黑客行为"欺骗"验证，需要专门的系统来检测和修复此类缺陷。

Method: 提出了一个基于智能体的自动化测试和修复系统，针对编译器中的四类代码（已验证代码、已检查代码、未验证代码和规范）设计了专门的缺陷检测技术；对每个发现的缺陷，系统调用编码智能体进行修复并验证修复结果。

Result: 在Axon编译器上进行评估，该编译器完全由编码智能体在开发者监督下生成。结果显示未发现Axon编译器或修复系统中的奖励黑客证据，同时给出了测试和修复有效性的特征化结果及修复特征讨论。

Conclusion: 该自动化测试和修复系统能够有效利用已验证编译器的结构信息检测缺陷，并通过编码智能体进行修复；Axon编译器及修复系统均未表现出奖励黑客行为，验证了该方法的可靠性。

Abstract: We present an agent based automated testing and repair system for verified compilers that contain four kinds of code: verified code, checked code, unverified code, and specification. We present specialized defect detection techniques that exploit the structure present in such compilers. For each surfaced defect the system invokes a coding agent to repair the defect and validate the repair. We evaluate the system on the Axon compiler, a compiler completely generated by a coding agent operating under developer supervision. The compiler was validated during development on a small benchmark set (the Livermore benchmarks), raising the possibility that its coding agent reward hacked the compiler. We also evaluate the possibility that the repair system reward hacked the repairs and find no evidence of reward hacking in either the Axon compiler or the repairs. We present results that characterize the testing and repair effectiveness and discuss repair characteristics.

</details>


### [13] [A Formalism-Aware Reward Loop for Handwritten UML-to-PlantUML Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28987)
*Mersedeh Sadeghi,Simon Scholz,Adrian Psoch-Bajraktari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究将视觉语言模型适应于手绘UML图到PlantUML的自动转换任务，采用监督微调与Group Relative Policy Optimisation相结合的训练方法，并引入基于形式化模型表示（XMI和控制流图）的奖励信号，以替代传统的表面文本比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手绘UML草图在早期软件设计中很常见，但将其转化为结构化的可分析建模工件仍需人工重建。现有方法将视觉语言模型用于此任务时仅将其视为图像到文本的生成，缺乏基于结构化模型表示的质量反馈信号。

Method: 采用监督微调（SFT）后接Group Relative Policy Optimisation（GRPO）的两阶段训练策略，将视觉语言模型适配到手绘UML到PlantUML的生成任务。奖励信号基于形式化模型表示：类图使用XMI比较，活动图使用控制流图比较，而非表面文本匹配。

Result: 适配后的模型在可编译性和转换质量上优于未微调的开源模型及一个专有基线模型，在类图任务上与更强的专有基线模型保持竞争力。但奖励引导阶段的额外收益在当前留出集上尚不明确。

Conclusion: 错误分析和度量有效性结果显示，建模可接受性只能被部分捕获，这提示未来需要将模型分析与人工判断相结合的奖励与评估机制。

Abstract: Handwritten UML sketches are common in early software design, but turning them into structured, analysable modelling artefacts still requires manual reconstruction. Vision-language models can generate PlantUML from diagram images, but prompt-based use treats this as image-to-text generation rather than structured model generation. We investigate formalism-aware rewards: feedback signals derived from analysable model representations rather than surface text. In a worked example, we adapt a vision-language model for handwritten UML-to-PlantUML generation using super-vised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimisation. Generated PlantUML is compared against target representations, using XMI for class diagrams and control-flow graphs for activity diagrams. Emerging results show that the adapted model improves compilability and conversion quality over the untuned open model and one proprietary baseline, while remaining competitive with a stronger proprietary baseline on class diagrams. The added benefit of the reward-guided stage remains open on the current held-out set. Error analysis and metric-validity results show that modelling acceptability is only partially captured, motivating rewards and evaluations that combine model analysis with human judgement.

</details>


### [14] [Execution-First Synthetic Tool-Use Trace Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29175)
*Hafsa Ouajdi,Francesco Giannuzzo,Alaa Boukhary,Paolo Papotti,Gerard Conangla,Adam Elwood*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出SyntheticAgentTraceQA框架，一种"先执行后合成"的数据生成方法，用于为工具增强型智能体生成可扩展的监督数据。该方法先构建工作流结构、映射到可用工具并实际执行验证，再合成自然语言用户任务和推理标注。在四个工具生态系统中评估，结果显示执行驱动的监督能改善工具执行行为；同时发现掩蔽监督（排除推理标注）在最终答案指标上更优，而完整监督在答案质量上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统查询优先的数据合成方法可能失败，因为看似合理的用户请求未必对应有效的工具序列、兼容参数或可用数据。训练工具增强型智能体需要能够捕获有效工具交互和可执行工作流的监督数据。

Method: 采用"执行优先"（execution-first）框架：首先构建高层工作流结构，通过依赖感知分配映射到可用工具，在受控环境中执行并验证生成的轨迹，最后才合成自然语言用户任务、教师推理标注和参考答案。用生成的数据微调并评估Qwen模型变体。

Result: 在四个工具生态系统中，执行驱动的监督改善了工具执行行为、参考轨迹一致性和答案生成性能。进一步的消融分析揭示了监督权衡：掩蔽监督（排除推理标注）改善最终答案指标，而完整监督（对包括推理token的完整输出计算损失）在答案质量上表现不佳，且在9B规模下不能一致改善参考轨迹一致性。

Conclusion: 合成监督数据的设计应根据工具增强型智能体所需的能力来定制，监督方式的选择（掩蔽vs完整）对智能体最终性能有显著影响，这突出了设计合成监督数据时匹配目标能力的重要性。

Abstract: Agentic software-engineering and industrial systems increasingly operate through executable workflows rather than code genera- tion alone: they search artifacts, invoke tools, inspect structured observations, and query databases. Training these agents requires supervision data that captures valid tool interactions and executable workflows. However, traditional query-first data synthesis can fail because plausible user requests may not correspond to valid tool sequences, compatible parameters, or available data. To address this limitation, we propose SyntheticAgentTraceQA, an execution- first framework for generating scalable supervision data for tool- augmented agents. Our framework first constructs high-level work- flow structures, maps them to available tools through dependency- aware assignment, executes and validates the resulting traces in con- trolled environments, and only then synthesizes natural-language user tasks, teacher-generated reasoning annotations, and reference answers. We evaluate the framework across four tool ecosystems and use the resulting data to fine-tune and evaluate Qwen model variants. The results show that execution-grounded supervision improves tool execution behavior, reference-trace agreement, and answer-generation performance on the evaluated tasks. Further analysis reveals a supervision trade-off: masked supervision, which excludes reasoning annotations from the training objective, im- proves final-answer metrics, whereas full supervision, computing loss over the complete assistant output including reasoning tokens, underperforms on answer quality and does not consistently im- prove reference-trace agreement, particularly at the 9B scale. These findings highlight the importance of designing synthetic supervi- sion according to the desired capabilities of tool-augmented agents.

</details>


### [15] [Quick Build, Careful Check? Generative AI Use in Hackathons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29178)
*Wangyiyao Zhou,Alexander Serebrenik,Alexander Nolte*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一项对中欧某两天AI主题黑客松中不同团队参与者的探索性访谈研究，发现参与者将生成式AI用于编码之外的多种目的（如学习、头脑风暴、文档准备），并会根据任务适配合使用GenAI与非GenAI工具。尽管没有团队规则，所有参与者都形成了检查GenAI输出的一致性非书面实践，但时间压力和领域知识有限制约了验证能力。研究还提出了结合观察、问卷和提示-响应日志的后续研究方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑客松是时间受限的软件开发活动，天然适合生成式AI的快速高效特性。尽管GenAI在这些活动中被越来越多的使用，但我们对参与者使用GenAI做什么、不用它做什么以及为何不用仍然知之甚少，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用探索性访谈研究方法，对中欧一个为期两天的AI主题黑客松中不同团队的参与者进行半结构化访谈，收集定性数据并进行分析。

Result: 研究发现：1) 参与者将GenAI用于编码之外的多种用途，包括学习陌生主题、头脑风暴和文档准备；2) 参与者根据任务适配性组合使用多种GenAI和非GenAI工具；3) 尽管没有团队规定，所有参与者都形成了检查GenAI输出的非书面共识做法，但验证能力受限于时间压力和领域知识不足。

Conclusion: 该研究识别了需要进一步深入调查的方向，并提出了结合观察、问卷调查以及提示-响应日志的后续研究设计，以更全面地理解黑客松场景中生成式AI的使用模式。

Abstract: Hackathons are time-bounded events where participants form teams to rapidly build software projects. Their short-term nature makes them a natural setting for Generative AI (GenAI) use, given its promise of speed and efficiency. Yet despite GenAI being increasingly adopted in these events, we still know little about what participants use GenAI for and, crucially, what they do not use it for and why. We report an exploratory interview study with participants from different teams at a two-day AI-themed hackathon in central Europe. We found that participants used GenAI for purposes beyond coding, including learning unfamiliar topics, brainstorming, and preparing documentation. At the same time, they combined multiple GenAI and non-GenAI tools depending on task fit. We also found that, despite the absence of team rules, all participants we studied converged on an unwritten practice of checking GenAI output before using it, yet their ability to actually verify was constrained by time pressure and limited domain knowledge. This work aims to identify avenues for further investigation by outlining a follow-up study combining observation, surveys, and prompt-and-response logs.

</details>


### [16] [Metamorphic Testing of Transpilers via Mutation Consistency of Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29247)
*Enea Raffaele Ilario Papaleo,Luca Guglielmo,Giovanni Denaro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对转译器（transpiler）的新型蜕变测试技术MCP-Tester，不依赖执行编译产物的运行时行为，而是直接在转译器生成的源代码上定义蜕变关系，捕获程序的"突变一致性"属性，在技术转移项目的案例研究中有效发现了纯模糊测试无法检测的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转译器在工业领域（尤其是依赖DSL的场景）中被广泛使用，但其正确性保障至关重要。现有编译器测试方法难以推广到转译器：差分测试因缺少多个等价实现而受阻，蜕变测试又依赖执行编译产物，而转译器输出常为源代码，需要复杂工具链和硬件在环设置，难以直接执行。

Method: 提出一种针对转译器的蜕变测试技术，蜕变关系直接定义在转译器生成的源代码之上，而非运行时行为。核心思想是"突变一致性"：对输入DSL程序施加突变风格的修改后，生成的输出应在结构上发生可预测且一致的对应变化。基于此实现了工具MCP-Tester。

Result: 在技术转移项目的案例研究中，实证结果表明该方法能有效揭示纯模糊测试无法检测的缺陷。

Conclusion: 直接在生成源码层面定义蜕变关系的测试方法，为转译器正确性验证提供了一条可行的新路径，克服了传统差分测试和基于执行的蜕变测试在转译器场景下的局限性。

Abstract: Transpilers are increasingly used for software development, especially in industrial domains that rely on domain-specific languages (DSLs), to allow engineers to work with familiar concepts and appropriate abstractions. Ensuring the correctness of these instruments is therefore critical in many industrial settings. This paper observes that existing approaches for compiler testing hardly generalize to transpilers. Differential testing approaches are hindered as multiple equivalent implementations of the transpiler under test are seldom available in practice. The approaches based on metamorphic testing assume the ability to execute the compiled binaries, an assumption that cannot be always made for transpilers, which oftentimes produce results expressed as source code, requiring complex toolchains, hardware-in-the-loop setups, and depending on non trivial inputs. This paper introduces a novel metamorphic testing technique tailored to transpilers. Instead of reasoning about the runtime behavior of compiled programs, our approach defines metamorphic relations directly over the source code produced by the transpiler. These relations capture a property that we call mutation consistency of the (transpiled) programs: mutation-style changes in the input DSL program must induce predictable and structurally consistent changes in the generated output. We implemented this idea in a tool, MCP-Tester, and evaluated it through a case study conducted in the context of a technology-transfer project. Our current empirical results indicate that the proposed approach can effectively reveal faults that would remain undetected with pure fuzzing.

</details>


### [17] [BRHC: Backend-driven Reactive Hypermedia Controls with a Statically Typed Kotlin DSL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29338)
*Fernando Miguel Carvalho,Paulo Carvalho,Leonel Correia,Ricardo Gomes,Juho Vepsäläinen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Kotlin HTML DSL的静态类型方法，用于后端驱动的响应式Web应用，通过扩展HtmlFlow DSL支持类型化的自定义HTML属性和信号绑定，几乎消除了JavaScript的使用，同时提高了类型安全性并保持了前后端同构的编程模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编码工具生成的Web应用存在复杂性、寿命和长期可维护性问题，其根源在于前后端编程模型异构，导致逻辑重复和状态管理碎片化。现有HDA方案（如HTMX、Turbo Hotwire、Datastar）虽将状态和逻辑移至服务器端，但仍通过自定义属性引入复杂性，并未完全消除JavaScript，尤其在计算表达式方面。

Method: 提出一种使用Kotlin HTML DSL的静态类型方法，扩展HtmlFlow Kotlin DSL以支持类型化的自定义HTML属性（即Datastar data-*属性）和基于信号绑定的静态类型构建器，实现后端驱动的响应式Web应用，使HTML成为客户端与服务器之间的主要通信媒介。

Result: 通过反应式交互模式目录和Petclinic Spring MVC案例研究进行验证，结果表明该方法几乎可以消除JavaScript的需求，同时提高类型安全性并保持前后端同构的编程模型。

Conclusion: 该方案通过后端驱动的响应式信号中心架构，实现了前端和后端之间的桥接，显著减少了JavaScript的依赖，提升了类型安全性和代码可维护性。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding tools (e.g., Copilot, Cursor, Claude) are increasingly ubiquitous and enable rapid generation of web applications. However, this raises concerns regarding complexity, longevity and the long-term maintainability of generated systems. A key source of complexity is the heterogeneity between backend and frontend programming models, where multiple languages and paradigms are combined within a single application, often leading to duplicated logic and fragmented state management. To address this issue, recent approaches (e.g., HTMX, Turbo Hotwire, Datastar, etc.) follow the Hypermedia-Driven Application (HDA) model, positioning HTML as the primary communication medium between client and server. Unlike SPA-centric architectures, HDA systems shift the application state and interaction logic to the server, where backend-driven reactive signals synchronize with the client user interface. However, these approaches still introduce complexity through custom attributes and do not fully eliminate JavaScript, particularly in computed expressions. In this work, we propose a statically typed approach using a Kotlin-based HTML DSL (Domain-Specific Language) for backend-driven reactive web applications. We extend the HtmlFlow Kotlin DSL with typed custom HTML attributes (i.e., Datastar data-* attributes) and signal-based bindings using statically typed builders. We demonstrate the approach through a catalog of reactive interaction patterns and a Petclinic Spring MVC case study. The results indicate that the proposed approach can nearly eliminate the need for JavaScript while improving type safety and preserving a homogeneous programming model across frontend and backend, bridged through a backend-driven reactive, signal-centric architecture.

</details>


### [18] [AgenticRepair: Multi-Faceted Program Context Engineering for Agentic Vulnerability Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29422)
*Michael Fu,Qiyue Mei,Patanamon Thongtanunam,Kla Tantithamthavorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticRepair，一个基于多智能体的漏洞修复框架，通过工程化三类程序上下文（代码结构、运行时执行、提交历史）来增强LLM修复能力，在SEC-Bench基准上达到73%的修复成功率，比最强基线高出29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体自动程序修复方法在通用bug修复上表现良好，但漏洞修复需要比通用bug修复更丰富的程序上下文，包括跨文件数据流、崩溃语义和提交历史等，而现有方法未对这些上下文进行工程化处理。

Method: AgenticRepair框架通过编排三个专门的LLM子智能体分别工程化三类上下文：代码结构上下文（跨文件数据流和内存操作模式）、运行时执行上下文（崩溃语义和内存来源）、提交历史上下文（脆弱代码模式的引入过程），并将这些上下文嵌入到一个专用修复子智能体的记忆中，实现上下文条件化的补丁合成。

Result: 在包含300个真实世界实例且使用sanitizer验证的SEC-Bench基准上，AgenticRepair达到73%的修复成功率，比最强基线高出29%。消融实验表明三类上下文相互补充，且多智能体架构和基础模型能力均发挥关键作用。

Conclusion: 多面程序上下文工程化是智能体漏洞修复的一个有前景的设计方向，填补了现有智能体方法在漏洞修复场景中上下文利用不足的空白。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability repair aims to reduce the time and effort required to patch security flaws from a vulnerability triage report. Recent agentic AI approaches have shown promising results in automated program repair. However, vulnerability repair demands richer program context than general bug repair - context that security engineers routinely assemble in practice but that existing agentic approaches do not engineer. We identify three critical gaps: code-structure context capturing cross-file data flows and memory operation patterns, runtime-execution context revealing crash semantics and memory origins, and commit-history context recovering how fragile code patterns were introduced. We present AgenticRepair, an agentic vulnerability repair framework that addresses the gaps through multi-faceted program context engineering. AgenticRepair orchestrates three specialized LLM subagents to engineer the contexts, which are then embedded into the memory of a dedicated repair subagent for context-conditioned patch synthesis. Evaluated on SEC-Bench comprising 300 real-world instances with sanitizer-based patch verification, AgenticRepair achieves a 73% success rate, substantially outperforming the strongest baseline by 29%. Our ablation study confirms that the three context facets are mutually complementary, and that multi-agent scaffolding and base-model capacity each play an essential role. Collectively, these findings establish multi-faceted program context engineering as a promising design direction for agentic vulnerability repair.

</details>


### [19] [From Code Review to Code Critique: Intent, Drift, and Spotlight for AI-Generated Diffs at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29516)
*Chandra Maddila,Mashrur Rashik,Euna Mehnaz Khan,Smriti Jha,James Saindon,Nachi Nagappan,Peter C. Rigby*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARCTIC，一个AI驱动的代码评审系统，通过意图预测、漂移检测和代码聚焦三个核心能力来应对AI编码代理产生的大量代码超出传统同行评审能力的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理生成的代码量已超出传统同行评审的处理能力，而现有AI代码审查工具过度关注风格和最佳实践等低价值建议，却忽视了人类审阅者最关心的正确性、安全性和性能问题。

Method: ARCTIC围绕三个核心能力构建：意图预测（从对话日志和元数据推断变更原因）、漂移检测（通过反向翻译衡量开发者意图与代理输出之间的偏差）、代码聚焦（对diff中最值得人工审查的区域进行排序）。这些能力基于从18,000个代码评审中提炼的六主题分类法。

Result: 离线评估中，意图预测F1达到0.86，漂移检测与人工标注者的序数一致性接近完美（QWK=0.907），代码聚焦在质量评估上比基线AI评审器表现好2.4倍且使用的token少5倍。实验中漂移评分使代码错位额外减少5.76个百分点（p=0.026），意图预测获得90.2%的认可率，自上线以来未发现可归因于自评审diff的缺陷。

Conclusion: ARCTIC通过将代码评审重新聚焦于意图理解、漂移检测和重点区域标注，有效缓解了AI时代代码评审的瓶颈问题，在效率和准确性上均显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: AI coding agents are generating code at volumes that exceed the capacity of traditional peer review. At the same time, existing AI code review tools over-index on low-value suggestions such as style and best practices while under-indexing on the concerns human reviewers prioritize most: correctness, security, and performance. We present ARCTIC, an AI-powered Code Critique system that reframes code review around three capabilities: intent prediction, which infers why a change was made from conversation logs and metadata; drift detection, which measures divergence between the developer's intent and the agent's output via backtranslation; and code spotlight, which ranks the regions of a diff most warranting human scrutiny. We ground these capabilities in a six-theme taxonomy derived from 18,000 code reviews. Offline evaluation shows that intent prediction achieves 0.86 F1, drift detection reaches near-perfect ordinal agreement with human annotators (QWK = 0.907), and spotlight outperforms the baseline AI reviewer by 2.4x on quality estimation at 5x fewer tokens. In the experimental rollout, the drift scores reduces code misalignment by an additional 5.76 points (p = 0.026), intent prediction receives 90.2% approval, and zero defects have been attributed to self-reviewed diffs since launch.

</details>


### [20] [Students' Practices and Skills in the LLM-Era: "You Can't Outsource the Struggle and Still Get the Skill"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29519)
*Enne Rebeca Silva de Freitas,Gustavo Pinto,Danilo Monteiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过分析五个研究型subreddit中的1,383篇帖子，发现软件工程研究生在使用生成式AI工具时系统性地外包了发展研究技能所需的认知努力，导致既未获得预期结果也未获得必要能力，呼吁识别这些缺失的技能以改进研究生课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具已被研究生快速融入日常工作流程，但人们对其在实证研究中有效使用所需的AI相关技能知之甚少，若不了解这些技能，研究生课程无法培养学生在LLM时代进行严谨研究的能力。

Method: 分析了来自五个以研究为导向的subreddit中的1,383篇帖子，以识别研究生在使用生成式AI进行实证研究时缺失的技能。

Result: 发现学生系统性地将发展研究技能所需的认知努力外包给AI工具，导致既没有得到预期的研究成果，也没有获得必要的研究能力。

Conclusion: 识别这些缺失的技能是开发课程的第一步，使研究生能够学会与LLM合作而非被其取代。

Abstract: Generative AI tools have been rapidly learned in the daily workflow of graduate students in Software Engineering, but little is known about what AI-related skills they actually need for effective use in empirical research. Without this understanding, graduate programs cannot prepare students to conduct rig-orous research in the LLM era, risking creating a generation of researchers who delegate tasks without the necessary expertise. By analyzing 1,383 posts from five research-focused subreddits, we found that students systematically outsource the cognitive effort required to develop research skills and end up with neither the expected results nor the necessary competence. Naming these missing skills is the first step toward curricula that teach graduate students to work \emph{with} LLMs without being replaced by them.

</details>


### [21] [AuditCoder: Responsibility-Preserving Task Graphs for Auditable Code Generation and Bounded Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29529)
*Kangjie Huang,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AuditCoder将程序与可审计的构建轨迹作为联合输出，通过带契约注释的任务图分配稳定的责任标识，实现代码生成的定位与修复。在APPS上达到82.5-83.0% pass@1，在ClassEval上达到75.0-82.0%，但在两个基准上均落后于AgentCoder。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成器只返回程序，但不保留构建记录，导致无法将失败定位到产生问题的决策，也无法界定合理的修复范围。

Method: 提出AuditCoder框架，将程序与可审计构建轨迹作为联合输出。先生成带契约注释的任务图，为每个承诺分配稳定的责任标识（含实现、来源、验证证据和干预历史）；验证失败时用保守定位器将异构证据映射到节点或依赖分支（或选择弃权），然后仅对受限区域进行有界修复，同时重用冻结的其余部分。

Result: APPS上达到82.5-83.0% pass@1，恢复因未修复图分解造成的部分损失，但落后AgentCoder 7.5-8.5个百分点；ClassEval上达到75.0-82.0%，优于CoT+重试但低于AgentCoder。200个APPS记录的审计中，任务级决策-代码轨迹覆盖率达0.9725；60个失败中定位器识别出26个有证据支持的节点或分支，其中17个局部修复通过。

Conclusion: 对于边界稳定且可局部测试的任务，任务图不仅作为分解结构，还能作为验证和修复的持久索引，为代码生成的可审计性和可追踪性提供了新方向。

Abstract: Code generators return programs, but typically do not preserve the construction record needed to connect a failure to the decision that produced the affected code or to delimit a justified repair. We present AuditCoder, which treats the program and an auditable construction trace as joint outputs. Before code generation, a contract-annotated task graph assigns stable responsibility identities that remain attached to each commitment, its owned implementation, provenance, validation evidence, and intervention history. When validation fails, a conservative locator maps heterogeneous evidence to a node or dependency branch---or abstains---and bounded repair regenerates only that region while reusing the frozen complement. On APPS, \method{} reaches $82.5$--$83.0\%$ \texttt{pass@1}, recovering much of the loss caused by unrepaired graph decomposition but trailing AgentCoder by $7.5$--$8.5$ points. On ClassEval, it reaches $75.0$--$82.0\%$, outperforming CoT + retry while remaining below AgentCoder. A separate audit of 200 APPS records yields $0.9725$ task-macro decision--code trace coverage; the locator identifies an evidence-supported node or branch for 26 of 60 failures, and 17 of those localized repairs pass. For tasks with stable, locally testable boundaries, the graph functions not only as a decomposition structure but also as a persistent index for validation and repair.

</details>


### [22] [Improving the Understandability of Conceptual Models via Abstract Notation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29552)
*Amine Abbad-Andaloussi,Daniel Jeppe Schütt,Kasper Solhøj Jørgensen,Tobias van Deurs Lundsgaard,Hugo A. López*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象符号工程（abstract notation engineering）这一语言无关的方法，用高层次、语义透明的构造替换概念建模中重复出现的低层构造配置，并在DCR图中实例化为DeCleaR扩展，实证验证表明其提升了感知质量、实用质量和用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念模型在将领域层抽象编码为低层构造时难以理解，先前工作主要改进单个构造的视觉表示，忽略了重复出现的构造配置这一问题。

Method: 提出抽象符号工程方法，包含四个步骤：模式识别（pattern identification）、模式形式化（pattern formalization）、视觉符号设计（visual notation design）和实证验证（empirical validation）。将其应用于DCR图，开发出DeCleaR扩展，用紧凑的基于模式的抽象替换复杂的低层工作流模式配置。

Result: 实证验证结果显示，DeCleaR相较于标准DCR图，在感知经验质量、实用质量和用户偏好三个维度上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 将关注点从单个构造转向重复出现的构造配置，通过抽象符号工程可以有效提升概念模型的可理解性和可用性，DeCleaR的成功验证了该方法的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Conceptual modeling supports the design, analysis, and communication of the properties of complex systems, yet conceptual models can be difficult to understand when domain-level abstractions must be encoded through low-level constructs required mainly for semantic conformity. Prior work has mainly improved how existing individual constructs are visually represented. We shift the focus from individual constructs to recurring configurations of constructs, and propose abstract notation engineering as a language-agnostic method for replacing such configurations with higher-level, semantically transparent constructs. The method comprises pattern identification, pattern formalization, visual notation design, and empirical validation. We instantiate it for Dynamic Condition Response (DCR) graphs, where common workflow patterns require elaborate low-level configurations. The resulting extension, DeCleaR, replaces such configurations with compact pattern-based abstractions. The results of our empirical validation show that DeCleaR improves perceived empirical quality, pragmatic quality, and user preference over standard DCR graphs.

</details>


### [23] [Alteron: A Tool for Behavioral Regression Testing Across NLP Classifier Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29557)
*Shazzad Hossain,Proma Chowdhury,Mridha Md. Nafis Fuad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Alteron工具，利用蜕变测试检测NLP模型版本间的行为回归。在10个蜕变关系、4个模型版本和3次版本更新的评估中，Alteron识别出16个行为回归，其中11个为发布阻断级别，证明了模型更新可能在不影响整体性能的情况下引入不良行为变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准指标无法充分捕捉NLP模型在版本更新中的行为变化，现有工作多关注模型孤立测试，缺乏对连续集成工作流中模型版本间比较的自动化方法。

Method: Alteron通过从标注的源样本构建测试语料库，并基于蜕变测试原理生成蜕变变换输入，对不同模型版本在这些变换输入上的输出进行比较，从而检测行为回归。

Result: 在10个蜕变关系、4个模型版本和3个模型更新转换的评估中，识别出16个行为回归，其中11个为发布阻断级别的回归；同时表明模型更新可以保持整体任务性能但引入不良行为变化，行为检查能揭示聚合基准指标无法捕获的失败。

Conclusion: Alteron验证了跨模型版本的行为检查对NLP开发流程的重要性，工具已开源并附带演示视频，为持续集成环境中的模型质量保障提供了实用手段。

Abstract: Evaluating evolving Natural Language Processing (NLP) models is important for ensuring reliable behavior across updates, but standard benchmark metrics do not fully capture how model behavior changes across versions. Existing work has focused mainly on testing models in isolation rather than comparing successive versions in continuous integration workflows. We present Alteron, a tool for detecting behavioral regressions across NLP model versions with metamorphic testing. Alteron constructs a test corpus from labeled source examples and compares model versions on metamorphically transformed inputs. In an evaluation spanning 10 metamorphic relations (MRs), 4 model versions, and 3 model-update transitions, Alteron identified 16 behavioral regressions, 11 of which were release-blocking. The results show that common model updates can preserve overall task performance while still introducing undesirable behavior changes, and that behavioral checks across model versions can reveal failures that aggregate benchmark metrics alone do not capture. The tool is open-source and available at https://github.com/shazzad5709/alteron. A screencast demonstration is available at https://youtu.be/szwiWW5O4do.

</details>


### [24] [Educating the Agentic Engineer: Curricula, Collaboration, and Continuous Learning in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29610)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了"代理工程师"这一新的专业角色概念，并构建了ACCEL教育框架（通过课程、协作和持续学习培养代理能力），以应对生成式和代理式AI正在重塑软件与系统工程领域的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式和代理式AI正在将软件与系统工程从以人工创作工件为中心转变为以指导、验证和治理自主系统为中心，这要求培养一种新的专业人才类型——"代理工程师"，其核心价值在于意图规范、多智能体工作流编排、机器生成产出的批判性评估以及伦理判断。

Method: 通过整合工程教育、计算教育、人机交互、人因工程和学习科学等领域的知识，进行了概念综合研究，提出了ACCEL框架（通过课程、协作和持续学习培养代理能力），该框架组织了五大能力支柱，并映射到三个交付向量：课程、协作和持续学习。还提出了脚手架式课程、委托-验证教学循环、重新设计的评估体系、治理素养伦理整合以及与国际AI能力框架的对齐。

Result: 该研究识别了关键风险，包括自动化偏见、技能退化、肤浅参与和问责分散。研究发现AI的好处分布不均且常被误解，需要系统性变革教育体系而非渐进式课程调整。

Conclusion: 教育代理工程师需要系统性转型而非渐进式课程变革：教学必须从产出工件转向对日益自主的社会技术系统行使判断力。结论强调了从工件生产导向转向判断力培养导向的教育范式转变。

Abstract: Generative and agentic artificial intelligence (AI) are reconfiguring software and systems engineering from a discipline centered on human authorship of artifacts to one focused on directing, verifying, and governing autonomous systems. This transition demands a new professional archetype, the \emph{agentic engineer}, whose enduring value lies in intent specification, orchestration of multi-agent workflows, critical evaluation of machine-generated outputs, and ethical judgment. This article presents an integrative conceptual synthesis across engineering education, computing education, human--AI interaction, human factors, and the learning sciences to derive an evidence-grounded educational architecture for this archetype. We introduce the ACCEL framework (Agentic Competencies through Curricula, Collaboration, and Enduring Learning), which organizes five competency pillars and maps them to three delivery vectors: curricula, collaboration, and continuous learning. Drawing on agency theory, trust-in-automation research, and empirical studies of AI-assisted programming, including evidence that AI benefits are unevenly realized and often misperceived, we propose a scaffolded curriculum, a delegation--verification pedagogical loop for human--AI teaming, redesigned assessment, governance-literate ethics integration, and alignment with current curricular guidelines and international AI competency frameworks. We identify key risks, including automation bias, deskilling, superficial engagement, and diffuse accountability, and conclude that educating the agentic engineer requires systemic transformation rather than incremental curricular change: instruction must shift from producing artifacts to exercising judgment over increasingly autonomous socio-technical systems.

</details>


### [25] [Reusing Past Repairs Through Hierarchical Trajectory Abstraction for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29658)
*Yisen Xu,Jiayuan Zhou,Ruiqi Pan,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍STAIR框架，将历史修复轨迹转化为多层级可复用计划树，用于引导未来的仓库级错误修复。集成到Lingxi代理后在SWE-bench Verified上达到81.2% Pass@1（MiniMax M2.5）和79.2%（GPT-5），且生成的计划可跨代理泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的修复代理虽然能处理复杂的仓库级问题，但每个问题都被独立处理，丢弃了从以往修复中积累的程序性知识，缺乏经验复用机制。

Method: STAIR将每条历史修复轨迹转化为多级树结构，从细粒度诊断操作到高层修复策略进行分层编码。面对新问题时，从多个抽象层级选择相关计划节点，定制成可执行的问题特定计划，并通过提示注入代理。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，STAIR集成Lingxi达到81.2% Pass@1（MiniMax M2.5）和79.2%（GPT-5）；生成的计划可将不同结构的代理mini-SWE-agent v2从75.8%提升至81.0%；混合多层级优于单一层级，原始未抽象轨迹效果显著更差。

Conclusion: 多层级抽象的经验复用能显著提升修复代理性能，且计划具有良好的跨代理泛化能力，验证了将历史修复知识转化为可复用结构化计划的有效性。

Abstract: Although LLM-driven repair agents can tackle complex, repository-level issues, they treat every issue independently and discard the procedural knowledge accumulated from previous repairs. We introduce STAIR, a framework that converts historical repair trajectories into hierarchical, reusable plans that can be adapted to steer future repairs. Each past trajectory is transformed into a multi-level tree that ranges from fine-grained diagnostic actions to high-level repair strategies, encoding experience at several granularities. When a new issue arrives, STAIR selects relevant plan nodes from multiple abstraction levels, tailors them into executable, issue-specific plans, and supplies them to the agent through its prompt. On SWE-bench Verified, STAIR integrated with Lingxi reaches 81.2% Pass@1 using MiniMax M2.5 and 79.2% using GPT-5. The generated plans also generalize across agents: without any code change, they lift the Pass@1 of a structurally different agent, mini-SWE-agent v2, from 75.8% to 81.0%. Ablation experiments further show that mixing multiple abstraction levels surpasses any single level and that raw, unabstracted trajectories transfer substantially worse.

</details>

### [26] [Query Density-Driven Partitioning for Spatiotemporal Load Balancing on Processing-in-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29070)
*Takato Hideshima,Shigeyuki Sato,Tomoharu Ugawa*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于查询密度驱动的键范围划分方案和B+森林索引结构，以在PIM系统中同时实现时空负载均衡和高效的范围聚合查询处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PIM树虽然能实现时空负载均衡，但牺牲了空间局部性，导致范围聚合查询效率低下。需要一种既能保持负载均衡又能高效处理范围聚合查询的PIM索引结构。

Method: 提出查询密度驱动的键范围划分方案，通过平衡PIM处理器间的查询密度，利用参数在查询负载与数据规模之间取得平衡，并基于该划分方案开发了B+森林（B+-Forest）索引结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，B+森林比基于空间约束、查询负载均衡但密度无感知划分的B+树具有更高的偏斜抵抗力，在点查询上性能与PIM树相当，同时能高效支持范围聚合查询。

Conclusion: B+森林在PIM系统上实现了时空负载均衡与范围聚合查询效率之间的良好平衡，为PIM环境下的数据库索引设计提供了新方案。

Abstract: Processing-in-Memory (PIM) systems, which consist of many processors with small local memory, have recently emerged as commercial products and attracted much attention as a means of overcoming the memory wall, particularly in the context of in-memory database technology. The state-of-the-art PIM-oriented index PIM-tree has been demonstrated to achieve asymptotically good spatiotemporal load balancing---query loads and data sizes are balanced among processors---for skewed queries, by trading spatial locality. Unfortunately, such a sacrifice of spatial locality hinders the PIM-oriented processing of range-aggregate queries. To achieve both spatiotemporal load balancing and efficiently executing range-aggregate queries on PIM systems, we develop a query density-driven key-range partitioning scheme. It balances query density among PIM processors, allowing us to strike a balance between query load and data size via a parameter. We then develop B${}^\text{+}$-Forest, a PIM-oriented B${}^\text{+}$-tree variant based on our partitioning scheme. Experimental results demonstrated that it exhibits higher skew resistance than a B${}^\text{+}$-tree based on space-constrained, query-load-balanced, density-unaware partitioning, and performance comparable to PIM-tree in point-get queries, as well as efficient support for range-aggregate queries.

</details>


### [27] [MERIT: Efficient In-Place Deletion for Dynamic Graph-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Indexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29173)
*Zekai Wu,Jiabao Jin,Peng Cheng,Wangze Ni,Haoyang Li,Lei Chen,Junjie Yao,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT框架，通过有界搜索恢复、k_r-最小生成树局部修复和版本化边失效三种核心技术，实现高效的图索引就地删除更新，在维持搜索召回率的同时显著提升删除速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的索引是近似最近邻搜索的主流方法，但现有方法在处理就地删除时面临挑战：过时向量需要被移除，但不能让失效的入边消耗搜索容量，也不能因昂贵的全局维护而中断在线服务（如RAG和推荐系统）。

Method: MERIT框架包含三种核心技术：(1)有界搜索恢复——结合被删除顶点的出邻与可搜索的入邻；(2) k_r-最小生成树局部修复——提升局部连通性同时保留多条搜索路由；(3)版本化边失效——立即过滤所有失效入边并随邻接表重写渐进移除。该框架可集成到HNSW和Vamana两种不同的图结构索引中。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，MERIT的删除成本接近插入一个向量的开销，比最先进的删除方法快3.02倍到18.87倍，且随着删除累积，搜索召回率保持稳定甚至有所提升。

Conclusion: MERIT提供了有效的图索引就地删除解决方案，适用于不同图结构，兼顾了删除效率和搜索质量，在RAG和推荐系统等实际应用场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Graph-based indexes have become the dominant approach to approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) over high-dimensional data and play a crucial role in real-world applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, recommendation systems, and vector databases. Despite extensive progress in static graph construction and search, efficient in-place deletion remains challenging because obsolete vectors must be removed without allowing stale incoming edges to consume search capacity or expensive graph-wide maintenance to interrupt online services, e.g., retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and recommendation platforms. To address this problem, we propose MERIT (MST-based Efficient Repair with In-place updaTes), an in-place update framework with three core techniques: (1) bounded search-based recovery that combines a deleted vertex's outgoing neighbors with its readily searchable in-neighbors, (2) $k_r$-Minimum Spanning Tree (MST) local repair that promotes local connectivity while retaining multiple routing choices for graph search, and (3) versioned-edge invalidation that immediately filters all stale incoming edges to the deleted vertex and progressively removes them as adjacency lists are rewritten. Its integration with the hierarchical HNSW index and the single-layer Vamana index demonstrates applicability across distinct graph structures. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets show that MERIT processes deletion at nearly the cost of inserting one vector, achieves up to $3.02\times$--$18.87\times$ faster deletion than state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, and keeps search recall stable or even improves it as deletions accumulate.

</details>
