### [28] [RareSense: Rarity-Aware Similarity Search for Anomaly Retrieval in Transactional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28879)
*Sidahmed Benabderrahmane,Talal Rahwan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RareSense是一个针对稀疏集合型异常数据的稀有性感知相似度框架，通过挖掘最小稀有项集和稀有关联规则，将对象映射为稀疏规则画像并使用加权Jaccard相似度比较，在多个基准数据集上取得了最优的检索和排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的相似度度量（如Jaccard、余弦、汉明距离）在比较稀疏集合数据时会被高频背景属性主导，IDF加权虽然部分缓解了这一问题，但仍停留在原子级别，无法显式表达有信息量的高阶共现模式。

Method: RareSense首先挖掘最小稀有项集作为中间结构，推导可靠的稀有关联规则，将对象映射为稀疏的稀有规则画像，然后使用加权Jaccard相似度进行比较。规则权重综合了逆支持度、置信度、提升度、结构复杂度和稳定性。从理论上证明了IDF加权Jaccard是RareSense的受限单例特例，且诱导的距离在原始对象上是伪度量、在等价类上是度量。

Result: 在涵盖网络安全和通用分类领域的四组基准数据集上，RareSense在查询条件下的宏平均检索性能达到最高；统计分析表明存在显著总体差异，配对比较中RareSense优于原子基线。在全球异常排序任务中，RareSense取得最高宏平均性能，且在统计上与多个强专用检测器相当。

Conclusion: RareSense通过显式建模稀有高阶共现结构，有效解决了经典相似度度量被高频背景属性主导的问题。其优势在异常共享可重复的稀有高阶结构时最为显著，但增益也依赖于具体工作负载。

Abstract: Similarity search over sparse set-valued data is often dominated by frequent background attributes because classical measures such as Jaccard, cosine, and Hamming compare objects through atomic overlap. IDF (Inverse document frequency) weighting partially reduces this effect but remains atom-wise and cannot explicitly represent informative higher-order co-occurrences. We introduce RareSense, a rarity-aware similarity framework for sparse transactional anomaly data. RareSense mines minimal rare itemsets as intermediate structures, derives reliable rare association rules, maps objects into sparse rare-rule profiles, and compares them using weighted Jaccard similarity. Rule weights combine inverse support, confidence, lift, structural complexity, and stability, so that neighborhoods are determined by shared rare evidence rather than uniform feature overlap. We show that IDF-weighted Jaccard is a restricted singleton case of RareSense, and that the induced distance is a pseudometric on the original objects and a metric over equivalence classes defined by identical rule profiles. Experiments across four benchmark families spanning cybersecurity and general categorical domains show that RareSense attains the highest observed macro-average query-conditioned retrieval performance among the evaluated similarity measures. The statistical analysis indicates significant overall differences, with corrected paired comparisons favoring RareSense over the atomic baselines. The gains remain workload-dependent and are strongest when anomalies share repeatable rare higher-order structure. For global anomaly ranking, RareSense achieves the highest observed macro-average performance while remaining statistically comparable to several strong dedicated detectors.

</details>


### [29] [LLM-Based Generative Retrieval for Snapchat Content Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28895)
*Liam Collins,Jiwen Ren,Donald Loveland,Bhuvesh Kumar,Clark Mingxuan Ju,Xuan Guo,Mo Li,Alvin Hou,Yi Cui,Peng Yang,Jian Wang,Saud Afzal Shafi,Nga Than,Ruiming Lu,Wenfeng Zhuo,Dongheng Li,Lili Zhang,Mingtao Zhang,Jinchao Ye,Vincent Xue,Chunhui Zhu,Neil Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SnapLGR，一个基于大语言模型的生成式检索系统，用于Snapchat的短视频推荐。系统通过三大核心设计——语义标识符(SIDs)结合PPR协同信号、持续预训练(CPT)来锚定新词表、以及基于TensorRT-LLM的高效服务架构——成功在生产环境中部署。A/B测试显示该系统相比现有TIGER风格基线在View Time、Time Spent等指标上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练大语言模型具备丰富的语义先验和序列建模能力，被视为有潜力的检索引擎，但将其转化为生产级生成式检索器面临两大挑战：模型需要学习预训练中不存在的新增物品词表，并且需在严格的延迟和成本约束下生成有效标识符。

Method: 1) 从多模态物品嵌入构建语义标识符(SIDs)，并通过基于Personalized PageRank(PPR)的共参与对比学习增强词表利用率并注入协同信号；2) 在监督微调(SFT)前使用持续预训练(CPT)将新增的SID token锚定到模型中；3) 通过TensorRT-LLM CUDA支持的beam search和去中心化worker-loop架构实现高效生产服务。

Result: 在线A/B测试中，SnapLGR相比现有TIGER风格生成式检索基线，View Time提升0.37%，Time Spent提升0.09%，Deep Sessions提升0.18%，Deep Sessions Unique User提升0.11%。同时通过固定tokenizer分解离线差距，量化了模型架构、规模和预训练各自带来的增益。

Conclusion: 成功的生产级SnapLGR部署需要在表示学习、词表锚定和高效训练与服务的联合设计。该工作表明，将LLM转化为生产级生成式检索器需要同时解决新增词表学习、标识符生成效率和部署可行性等多方面的系统设计挑战。

Abstract: Pretrained large language models (LLMs) are promising retrieval engines because they combine rich semantic priors, strong sequence modeling capabilities, and favorable scaling behavior. However, turning a pretrained LLM into a generative retriever in production deployment raises several challenges: the model must learn an internal item vocabulary that was absent from pretraining, and generate valid item identifiers under strict latency and cost constraints. We address these challenges through the design and launch of SnapLGR, an LLM-based generative retrieval system for short-video recommendation at Snapchat. The system is built around three main designs. First, we construct semantic identifiers (SIDs) from multimodal item embeddings and enhance them with Personalized PageRank (PPR)-based co-engagement contrastive learning, resulting in improved codebook utilization, reduced collisions, and infused collaborative signal. Second, we use continued pretraining (CPT) to ground the introduced SID tokens before supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on user interaction sequences. Third, we make SnapLGR serving practical through TensorRT-LLM CUDA-backed beam search and a decentralized worker-loop architecture. In a live A/B test, the launched system increased View Time by 0.37%, Time Spent by 0.09%, Deep Sessions by 0.18%, and Deep Sessions Unique User by 0.11% relative to the existing TIGER-style generative retrieval baseline. We then decompose this offline gap under a fixed tokenizer and quantify the gains due to model architecture, scaling, and pretraining. Overall, our deployment shows that successful production SnapLGR requires joint design across representation learning, vocabulary grounding, and efficient training and serving.

</details>


### [30] [TransX: Scaling Transformer-based Recommendation via Behavioral and Serving Stream Crossings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28940)
*Da Xu,Liyan Fang,Divya Venugopalan,Sunny Hsu,Xukai Wang,Rishav Roy Chowdhury,Cindy Liang,Nishant Satya Lakshmikanth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TransX是一种面向生产的编码器-解码器架构推荐系统，将推荐问题重构为序列到序列的动作转导任务，显式解耦行为流建模与服务事件建模，并通过增量行为编码与逐请求键值缓存实现低延迟部署，在LinkedIn推荐系统上CTR提升6.0%，转化提升4.4%，在线计算量减少约80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业推荐系统广泛采用Transformer序列模型，但将异构数据源（如长期用户行为和实时服务事件）压缩成单一token流会模糊其因果角色和时序特征的差异，导致建模效率低下以及训练和推理成本过高。

Method: 提出TransX编码器-解码器架构，将推荐重构为序列到序列的动作转导问题：显式解耦行为流建模与服务事件建模，通过可扩展的交叉注意力在近线行为编码与实时服务表示之间建立条件关系；同时设计摊销服务策略，结合增量行为编码与逐请求键值缓存，使服务延迟对行为序列长度不敏感。

Result: 在LinkedIn推荐系统上，TransX在离线实验与大规模在线A/B测试中一致优于最先进的DLRM和序列基线模型，CTR提升6.0%，转化增益4.4%，服务成本与现有生产模型相当，联合设计的服务策略将在线计算量减少约80%。

Conclusion: TransX证明了将推荐系统中的异构数据源解耦建模并采用编码器-解码器转导范式，能够在保持生产级服务成本的前提下显著提升推荐效果，为工业级推荐系统的架构设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems (RecSys) increasingly adopt Transformer-based sequence models, with an emerging paradigm that frames recommendation as next-token prediction over a unified monolithic user sequence. However, collapsing heterogeneous data sources -- such as long-term user behaviors and real-time serving events -- into a single monolithic token stream that obscures their distinct causal roles and temporal characteristics, leading to inefficient modeling and elevated training and serving costs. We propose TransX, a production-oriented encoder-decoder architecture that reformulates recommendation as a sequence-to-sequence action transduction problem. TransX explicitly decouples behavior-stream modeling from serving-event modeling and conditions next-action decoding on scalable cross-attention between nearline behavior encodings and real-time serving representations. To enable low-latency, high-QPS deployment, TransX is co-designed with an amortized serving strategy that combines incremental behavior encoding with per-request key-value caching, rendering serving latency insensitive to behavior sequence length. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests on LinkedIn's recommender systems show that TransX consistently outperforms state-of-the-art DLRMs and sequential baselines, and delivers substantial CTR lift (+6.0%) and conversion gain (+4.4%) while maintaining serving costs comparable to existing production models where our co-designed serving strategy reduces online computation by approximately 80%.

</details>


### [31] [Don't Contrast the Impossible: Region-Constrained Batching for Contrastive User Modeling on a Local Community Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28971)
*Seungho Han,Byeongchang Kim,Jin Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对本地社区平台中地理位置约束导致的"不可能负样本"问题，提出区域约束批次采样（RCBS）方法，通过构建同区域同质化的小批次，让用户主要与可行范围内可见的物品进行对比学习，从而提升用户表示质量和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习在推荐系统中广泛应用时，标准批次内负样本隐含"任何用户可看到任何物品"的假设。但在本地社区平台（如Karrot）中，曝光受地理约束，许多用户-物品组合在设计中就不可能存在，却被当作负样本参与训练，稀释了对比学习信号。

Method: 提出Region-Constrained Batch Sampling（RCBS），一种简单有效的批次构造方法。它构建区域同质的小批次，使得用户主要与在其可行可见范围内的物品进行对比，用可行负样本替代不可行负样本，从而在现实的曝光约束下引入更难、更具信息量的负样本。

Result: 通过离线评估和在线A/B测试，RCBS一致提升了用户表示质量，并显著改善了信息流排序、检索和展示广告排序等下游任务。训练出的用户嵌入已在多种应用场景中部署到生产环境。

Conclusion: RCBS通过区域约束的批次采样有效解决了本地社区平台中的不可能负样本问题，在不引入复杂模型改动的前提下提升了对比学习的质量，并在实际生产系统中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Contrastive learning is widely used for user modeling in large-scale recommender systems, where standard in-batch negatives implicitly assume universal exposure that any user can be shown any item. On local community platforms such as Karrot, however, exposure is geographically constrained; many user-item pairs are impossible by design yet still treated as negatives during training, diluting the contrastive learning signal. We address this impossible negatives problem and propose Region-Constrained Batch Sampling (RCBS), a simple yet effective batching method that constructs region-homogeneous mini-batches so that users are contrasted primarily against items they could feasibly see. By replacing impossible negatives with feasible ones, RCBS naturally introduces harder and more informative negatives under realistic exposure constraints. With offline evaluations and online A/B tests, we show that RCBS consistently improves user representation quality and consequently enhances home feed ranking, retrieval, and display ads ranking. The resulting user embeddings have been deployed in production across various applications.

</details>


### [32] [Think2Go: Generative Next POI Recommendation with LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28997)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Shanshan Feng,Hangwei Qian,Mingqi Yang,Heng Qi,Yanming Shen,Baocai Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Think2Go，一种生成式下一POI推荐框架，通过统一监督微调与强化学习推理，结合两种优势加权机制（基于核密度估计的提示认知不确定性校准和奖励归一化优势缩放）来增强语义ID理解与时空模式探索，实现记忆与推理的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有POI推荐方法存在双重局限：传统非推理模型受限于稀疏复杂移动模式，难以捕捉深层意图；而LLM因将语义ID（SIDs）单独训练而缺乏对SID表示的深入理解，表现欠佳。

Method: 提出Think2Go框架，核心包括：(1) 在同一架构中统一SFT与基于RL的推理，联合优化记忆与自适应推理；(2) 两种优势加权校准机制——基于核密度估计的提示认知不确定性评估（促进高不确定性下的探索）和奖励归一化优势缩放（提高训练稳定性）；这些机制形成隐式课程学习策略，实现细粒度实例感知策略更新。

Result: 通过测试时计算扩展增强模型对SID表示的理解，探索多样化时空模式；联合校准策略可防止熵坍缩并支持稳健探索。

Conclusion: Think2Go通过将记忆（SFT）与推理（RL）统一，并引入不确定性感知和奖励校准的优势加权机制，有效解决了LLM在POI推荐中的语义ID理解不足问题，为生成式推荐系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation task focuses on mining user behavioral preference patterns from historical check-ins to provide personalized suggestions for the next destination. Existing methods primarily rely on shallow contextual information and handcrafted feature interactions to predict the next POI. However, the inherent sparsity and complexity of user mobility patterns limit the computational capacity of non-reasoning models to capture deep intent, while large language models (LLMs) perform suboptimally because they lack a deep understanding of semantic IDs (SIDs) when SIDs are trained separately. To address these limitations, we propose Think2Go, a novel generative next POI recommendation framework, which enhances the model's comprehension of SID representations and explores diverse spatial-temporal patterns via test-time computational scaling. We unify supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL)-based reasoning within a single architecture, enabling joint optimization of memorization and adaptive reasoning to better retain user behavior patterns while exploring diverse user preferences. To further calibrate policy optimization in adaptive reasoning, we propose two advantage weighting mechanisms that integrate (1) prompt epistemic uncertainty, estimated via kernel density methods to assess the spatial-temporal periodic pattern alignment between queries and user history, promoting increased exploration under high epistemic uncertainty; and (2) reward-informed advantage scaling, captured by normalizing rewards against their maxima to adapt update magnitudes, thereby improving training stability and mitigating overfitting to noisy signals. This joint calibration forms an implicit curriculum learning strategy, delivering fine-grained, instance-aware policy updates that prevent entropy collapse and support robust exploration.

</details>


### [33] [PaletteID: Prototype-Composed Semantic Identifiers for Multimodal CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29000)
*Huanyu Liu,Baining Chen,Hui Liu,Zengyang Li,Ziyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PaletteID（PID），一种基于原型的语义标识符方法，通过调色板式的原型组合来桥接预训练多模态内容与推荐模型，在点击率预测任务上优于现有残差语义标识符方法，尤其对长尾物品效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义标识符（SID）的多模态推荐方法存在两大局限：一是码本分配未能保留语义相关性，且丢弃了原始嵌入空间中的细粒度连续信号；二是残差码路径高度依赖前缀码，限制了层次化标识符的表征可扩展性，导致增益有限。

Method: 提出PaletteID（PID），受调色板色彩合成启发：首先通过语义质量感知行列式点过程（SQ-DPP）构建原型调色板，同时考虑局部内容密度和全局语义多样性；然后对每个目标物品检索一组语义相关的原型并聚合成信息丰富的PID表示，实现丰富互补的语义建模。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，PID一致性地提升了CTR预测效果，对长尾物品获得更大增益；同时相比现有残差SID方法，PID产生更鲁棒的标识符分配和更可解释的令牌语义。

Conclusion: PaletteID通过原型组合的方式有效解决了现有SID方法中语义保留不足和残差路径扩展性受限的问题，为多模态推荐场景下的语义标识符设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal information can improve the accuracy of click-through rate (CTR) prediction and effectively alleviate item cold-start and long-tail problems. Recent studies commonly discretize pretrained multimodal embeddings into semantic identifiers (SIDs), allowing the model to learn task-specific semantic representations for recommendation. However, existing methods still provide limited gains due to two major limitations. First, codebook assignment fails to preserve semantic relevance and discards fine-grained continuous signals in the original embedding space. Second, the residual code paths are highly dependent on prefix codes, which limits the effective representational scalability of hierarchical identifiers. To address these issues, we propose PaletteID (PID), a prototype-based semantic identifier. Inspired by palette-based color composition, PID uses a compact set of representative prototype items as semantic anchors to bridge pretrained multimodal content space and recommendation models. Specifically, we first construct a prototype palette with Semantic Quality-Aware Determinantal Point Process (SQ-DPP), which jointly considers local content density and global semantic diversity. Then, for each target item, PID retrieves a sequence of semantically related prototypes and aggregates them into an informative PID representation, enabling rich and complementary semantic modeling. Extensive experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that PID consistently improves CTR prediction and yields larger gains for long-tail items. PID also produces more robust identifier assignments and provides more interpretable token semantics than existing residual SID methods.

</details>


### [34] [EvoReason: Self-Evolving Reasoning Primitive-Guided On-Policy Distillation for Latent Reasoning in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29010)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Zhipeng Wei,Rongfeng Guo,Shijie Li,Peng Zhao,Jie Chen,Fei Pan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoReason，一个自演进的潜在推理框架，通过原始引导的on-policy蒸馏，将显式推理监督与学生的潜在推理空间自适应对齐，解决推荐系统中直接蒸馏原始CoT轨迹带来的监督次优问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法依赖直接将原始链式思维(CoT)轨迹蒸馏为潜在表示，假设文本推理轨迹能提供充分监督。然而推荐推理轨迹包含多样的推理过程，存在冗余表达和不稳定的推理路径，使得原始CoT监督难以学习可迁移的潜在推理表示。

Method: EvoReason包含三个核心步骤：(1)从高质量agentic推荐轨迹中提取可复用的推理原语，每个原语捕捉关键的推理行为并作为教师结构化推理的伪工具；(2)基于这些原语赋予教师原语感知推理能力，生成结构化的CoT监督，减少冗余并提升一致性；(3)引入自演进的on-policy蒸馏机制，原语引导的推理过程根据学生的潜在推理结果不断演化，形成闭环共同进化。

Result: 通过闭环共同进化机制，策略更新持续根据潜在推理结果优化推理行为，实现渐进式更好的CoT监督对齐和更有效的推理迁移。

Conclusion: EvoReason通过原语引导的自演进蒸馏机制，有效解决了原始CoT监督在潜在推理表示学习中的次优问题，实现了推理监督与学生潜在推理空间的持续对齐，提升了推理迁移效果。

Abstract: Generative recommendation benefits from reasoning-enhanced inference, and latent reasoning offers an efficient paradigm by encoding intermediate reasoning processes into compact continuous representations for latency-sensitive deployment. Despite its efficiency, existing latent reasoning approaches typically rely on directly distilling raw chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories into latent representations, assuming that textual reasoning traces provide sufficient supervision. However, recommendation reasoning trajectories contain diverse reasoning processes with redundant expressions and unstable reasoning paths, making raw CoT supervision suboptimal for learning transferable latent reasoning representations. To address this challenge, we propose EvoReason, a self-evolving latent reasoning framework that adaptively aligns explicit reasoning supervision with the student's latent reasoning space through primitive-guided on-policy distillation. First, EvoReason extracts reusable reasoning primitives from high-quality agentic recommendation trajectories, where each primitive captures an essential reasoning behavior and serves as a pseudo-tool for structured teacher reasoning. Then, based on these primitives, we equip the teacher with primitive-aware reasoning capabilities, enabling it to generate structured CoT supervision with reduced redundancy and improved consistency. Finally, during latent reasoning optimization, EvoReason introduces a self-evolving on-policy distillation mechanism, where the primitive-guided reasoning process evolves according to the student's latent reasoning outcomes. Through this closed-loop co-evolution, policy updates continuously improve latent reasoning behaviors is refined according to the resulting latent reasoning outcomes, enabling progressively better-aligned CoT supervision and more effective reasoning transfer.

</details>


### [35] [Reproducing LightMem: Naive RAG Is Just as Good for Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29104)
*Yongjie Zhou,Shuai Wang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究复现了LightMem内存管理方法，并与直接检索原始用户轮次的Naive RAG进行对比。发现检索器选择是性能变化的主要来源（仅更换检索器使准确率从58.1%提升至75.5%），构建的内存并不总是优于原始轮次检索，LightMem主要在紧张的回答token预算下表现更好，且内存构建会丢失部分答案相关信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LightMem作为轻量级内存管理方法声称有效且构建成本低，但已有评估仅使用单一检索器，未充分考察检索器选择对性能的影响，也未验证内存构建是否会丢弃答案相关信息。

Method: 复现LightMem，并与Naive RAG（直接从原始用户轮次检索）进行对比。固定LightMem存储结构但更换不同检索器进行消融实验，同时在匹配检索深度和不同token预算条件下比较两种方法，并通过Oracle评估检验内存构建的信息丢失情况。

Result: 恢复了LightMem的主要配置趋势，但发现检索器是性能变化的主要来源（准确率在58.1%-75.5%之间波动）。Naive RAG在匹配检索深度下通常表现更好，LightMem仅在紧张token预算下占优，且Oracle评估确认内存构建移除了部分答案相关信息。

Conclusion: LightMem提供的是上下文效率上的权衡，而非对Naive RAG的全面优势。其价值取决于检索器选择和可用的token预算，未来应关注检索、重排序、查询构建及其与原始/构建内存表示的交互。

Abstract: Long-term conversational agents require access to information from earlier interactions, such as a user's preferences, past requests, or previously mentioned facts. Repeatedly providing the full dialogue history can be expensive as conversations grow, so many memory approaches instead transform past interactions into compact entries that can be retrieved when needed. LightMem is a recent lightweight memory-management approach that reports strong effectiveness while maintaining relatively low construction cost. However, it still relies on a separate constructed memory representation and is evaluated with only one retriever, leaving unclear how sensitive its results are to retriever choice and whether memory construction discards answer-relevant information.
  In this study, we reproduce LightMem and compare it with Naive RAG, which retrieves directly from raw user turns. We recover LightMem's main configuration trend, but find that retriever choice is a major source of performance variation: changing only the retriever over a fixed LightMem store shifts answer accuracy from 58.1% to 75.5%. Constructed memories also do not consistently outperform raw-turn retrieval. Naive RAG generally performs better at matched retrieval depths, whereas LightMem performs better mainly under tight answering-token budgets. Oracle evaluation further shows that memory construction removes some answer-relevant information.
  Overall, LightMem offers a context-efficiency trade-off rather than a general advantage over Naive RAG. Its value depends on the retriever and available token budget, motivating future work on retrieval, reranking, query formulation, and their interaction with raw and constructed memory representations.

</details>


### [36] [GALA: Generative Aligned Learning for Adaptive Multimodal Representation in the Taobao Shangou Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29213)
*Jiping Liu,Zhongmin Zhang,Zisen Sang,Zhijia Fang,Tao Ouyang,Ma Jiang,Shaopeng Liang,Zeyang Hou,Guodong Cao,Jia Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GALA，一个三阶段多模态推荐系统管线，通过引入"生成式RL对齐"中间阶段，利用GRPO奖励优化桥接预训练与微调之间的差距，已在淘宝闪购生产环境部署，服务超过2亿日活用户，AUC提升+0.12/+0.20，订单量提升0.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外卖推荐系统中多模态信号（图像、文本、用户交互历史）的融合仍然具有挑战性。主流两阶段方法将内容语义预训练与行为驱动的排序模型分离，导致语义理解与用户行为模式之间缺乏对齐，难以适应不断演变的用户意图。

Method: GALA包含三个阶段：(1) 行为感知的三元组预训练，利用搜索日志中的query-图像-文本对捕捉用户意图；(2) 核心创新——中间阶段通过GRPO（生成式RL）奖励优化动态细化多模态嵌入，桥接预训练与微调差距；(3) 通过自适应门控和混合损失整合多模态嵌入与ID嵌入，在长期ID主导训练下保持多模态贡献。

Result: 与SOTA方法相比，离线AUC提升+0.12/+0.20，PCOC指标更优；大规模在线A/B测试显示订单量提升0.55%，在工业规模下验证了有效性及对不同需求模式的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GALA通过创新的生成式RL对齐中间阶段有效解决了多模态预训练与行为排序之间的差距问题，已在淘宝闪购生产环境成功部署，验证了其在工业规模下的一致有效性。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems in food delivery increasingly leverage multimodal signals, including images, text, and user interaction histories, to enhance user experience, yet effective fusion of these heterogeneous modalities remains challenging, hindering both the joint modeling of multimodal signals and adaptation to evolving user intent. In mainstream two-stage approaches, the separation between content-semantic pretraining of image-text encoders and behavior-driven ranking models limits alignment between semantic understanding and user behavior patterns. To address these issues, we present GALA, a three-stage pipeline whose core innovation lies in an intermediate "generative RL alignment" stage that constructs multimodal pretraining data from user behavior and refines it via conversion-based rewards, effectively bridging the pretraining-fine-tuning gap to align with downstream objectives. GALA comprises three stages: first, behavior-aware triplet pretraining on query-image-text pairs from search logs to early capture user intent and content preferences; second, a novel intermediate stage that refines multimodal embeddings through reward-driven optimization (GRPO) to dynamically align them with user behavior and bridge the pretraining-fine-tuning gap; and finally, integration of multimodal and ID embeddings via adaptive gating with a hybrid loss, preserving multimodal contributions under long-term ID-dominant training. GALA has been deployed in the production environment at Taobao Shangou, serving over 200 million daily active users. Compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, it delivers consistent offline gains of +0.12/+0.20 AUC along with better PCOC metrics. Large-scale online A/B tests further report a 0.55 percent increase in order volume, confirming GALA's effectiveness at industrial scale and its robustness across diverse demand patterns.

</details>


### [37] [RecHarness: A Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for Self-Evolving Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29241)
*Haoran Ling,Yuecheng Li,Zeyu Song,Jing Yao,Shuwen Kang,Chi Lu,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RecHarness将推荐模型优化过程拆分为两个阶段：Bandit路由器根据历史验证反馈选择修改方向，LLM在选定方向内生成具体优化假设和可执行代码编辑，并通过跳盆地机制在局部编辑停滞时激活结构化跳跃，从而在有限实验预算下实现更稳定的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型优化仍高度依赖工程师手动迭代，LLM代理虽可自动化此过程，但让LLM同时选择修改方向并生成具体假设会导致搜索不稳定且实验预算有限。

Method: RecHarness采用两层分离的优化架构：1) Bandit路由器根据历史验证反馈选择下一个修改方向；2) LLM在选定方向内生成具体优化假设和可执行代码编辑；3) 引入跳盆地机制，当局部编辑停滞时激活结构化跳跃臂以维持长期探索。

Result: 在多个推荐任务、数据集和模型骨干上，RecHarness比LLM推理搜索更稳定且更有效地利用有限试验预算。在大型短视频广告平台的7天在线A/B测试中，ADVV提升2.084%，Revenue提升0.534%，Exposure提升0.559%。

Conclusion: 将方向选择（Bandit路由）与假设生成（LLM）解耦，配合跳盆地机制，能够显著提升自动化推荐模型优化的稳定性和效率，并在真实大规模广告平台上取得可观的业务指标提升。

Abstract: Optimizing modern recommender models still depends heavily on engineers manually iterating over architectural, objective, and training-strategy changes. While LLM-based agents can automate this trial-and-error process, allowing the LLM to both select modification directions and generate concrete hypotheses often leads to unstable search under limited experiment budgets. Inspired by the above challenge, we propose RecHarness, a Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for automated recommender model optimization. RecHarness separates the optimization process into two steps: a bandit router selects the next modification direction according to historical validation feedback, while the LLM generates a concrete optimization hypothesis and executable code edit within the selected direction. To sustain long-horizon exploration, RecHarness uses a jump-basin mechanism to activate a structural-jump arm when local edits stagnate. Across multiple recommendation tasks, datasets, and model backbones, RecHarness achieves more stable performance improvements and uses limited trial budgets more effectively than LLM-reasoning search. During a 7-day online A/B test on a large-scale short-video advertising platform, the selected candidate improves ADVV by 2.084%, Revenue by 0.534%, and Exposure by 0.559%. Code is available at https://github.com/6lyc/RecHarness.

</details>


### [38] [Language Models Agree With Each Other, Not With Readers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29274)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究使用2,523个读者标记集（来自120个网页文档，非为研究目的构建）作为人类参考，测量语言模型输出的一致性收敛。发现模型间的中位一致性（+0.093）远超人类基准（+0.040），99对模型完全高于人类区间，但没有任何模型与读者的一致性超过读者之间的一致性。效果与决定论、提示词、流程、供应商等无关，且具梯度性——最小模型与人类水平相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于语言模型输出同质化（homogenisation）的研究通常使用为研究专门收集的人类判断作为基准，这使人类侧成为设计的人工产物（众包者实际上在运行模型的提示词）。作者希望找到一个"没有人专门为此构建"的人类参考来测量模型收敛。

Method: 使用2,523个读者标记集（120个网页文档），来自默认关闭他人标记叠加的平台上按自身理由做高亮标注的真实读者。一致度定义为两个同等大小的句集重叠减去各自在深度和长度频带内重新采样时的预期重叠。随机基线的校准在0.006之内。测试18个模型臂（11个供应商、3个国家、两种权重制度），153对模型对与人类基准对比，并在四个后续发布的模型上进行样本外测试。

Result: 中位文档中，每方标注14/70句，两位读者共享4.1句，两个模型共享8.7句。153对模型对的中位一致度为+0.093（人类为+0.040），99对完全高于人类区间。两家竞争实验室的前沿模型达到+0.203——是GPT-4o自身两次调用一致度的两倍。效果与决定论、提示词、流程、供应商无关，且具梯度性。样本外测试中，四个后续模型均未超过人类区间。

Conclusion: 语言模型输出存在系统性的收敛效应，即使以"非为研究目的构建"的人类标记作为参照，模型间一致度仍显著高于人类间一致度。该效应具有梯度性（模型越大越收敛），且不依赖于程序性因素。但该倍数具有程序依赖性，且从多个模型模拟出的人群并非多个独立人群——模型收敛的同质化效应是真实且稳健的。

Abstract: Claims that language models homogenise are usually measured against human judgements collected for the study, which makes the human side an artifact of the design: a crowdworker given the model's instruction is running the model's prompt. We measure convergence against a human reference nobody built for the purpose -- 2,523 reader mark sets across 120 web documents, produced by people highlighting for their own reasons on a platform where the overlay of others' marks is off by default.
  Agreement is the overlap between two size-matched sentence sets minus the overlap expected when each is resampled within its own depth-and-length bands. The null's calibration is demonstrated, not asserted: every pair involving a random baseline lands within 0.006 of zero. On the median document each party names 14 sentences of 70; two readers share 4.1 and two models 8.7.
  Across 18 model arms spanning 11 vendors, 3 countries and both weight regimes, the median of 153 model pairs is +0.093 against a human yardstick of +0.040, and 99 sit entirely above the human interval. Two frontier models from rival labs reach +0.203, twice what GPT-4o agrees with itself on a second call. The effect is not determinism, prompt wording, procedure, vendor or routing, and it is graded: the smallest models agree at the human level. No model agrees with readers detectably more than a reader does, and at equal depth and length no surface feature separates their choices.
  The multiples are procedure-dependent and the ordering is not: models are cut to their sharpest set while a reader's is a random draw from what they marked, and blunting the models alike halves the gap without closing it. Tested out of sample on four models released after this analysis, against predictions fixed beforehand, none clears the human interval. A population simulated from several models is not several populations.

</details>


### [39] [Bridging the Question-Answer Gap in Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29402)
*Domen Vake,Jernej Vičič,Aleksandar Tošić*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings (HyPE)框架，将假设内容生成从查询阶段转移到索引阶段，通过为每个数据块预计算多个假设提示并嵌入数据块，将检索转变为问题-问题匹配任务，在六个数据集上相比标准方法将检索上下文精度提升最多42个百分点，声称召回率提升最多45个百分点，且不引入额外查询延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中用户查询与文档文本之间存在风格差距，现有解决方案（如HyDE）在查询时动态生成假设内容来改善对齐，但引入了额外的计算开销和延迟。

Method: HyPE框架将假设内容的生成从查询阶段转移到索引阶段：为每个数据块预计算多个假设提示，并用数据块的嵌入替代提示的嵌入，将检索任务转化为问题-问题匹配任务，从而消除查询时合成答案生成的需求。

Result: 在六个常见数据集上的实验表明，与标准方法相比，HyPE将检索上下文精度提升最多42个百分点，声称召回率提升最多45个百分点，同时兼容重新排序、多向量检索、查询分解等RAG先进技术。

Conclusion: HyPE通过将假设内容生成移至索引阶段，在不增加查询延迟的前提下有效增强了查询与相关上下文之间的对齐，为RAG系统提供了一种高效且兼容性强的检索优化方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems synergize retrieval mechanisms with generative language models to enhance the accuracy and relevance of responses. However, bridging the style gap between user queries and relevant information in document text remains a persistent challenge in retrieval-augmented systems, often addressed by runtime solutions (e.g., Hypothetical Document Embeddings (HyDE)) that attempt to improve alignment but introduce extra computational overhead at query time. To address these challenges, we propose Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings (HyPE), a framework that shifts the generation of hypothetical content from query time to the indexing phase. By precomputing multiple hypothetical prompts for each data chunk and embedding the chunk in place of the prompt, HyPE transforms retrieval into a question-question matching task, bypassing the need for runtime synthetic answer generation. This approach does not introduce latency but also strengthens the alignment between queries and relevant context. Our experimental results on six common datasets show that HyPE can improve retrieval context precision by up to 42 percentage points and claim recall by up to 45 percentage points, compared to standard approaches, while remaining compatible with re-ranking, multi-vector retrieval, query decomposition, and other RAG advancements

</details>


### [40] [QASP: Query-Adaptive Robust Vector Search Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29606)
*Hakan Ferhatosmanoglu,Kushal Kumar,Tal Wagner,Andy Warfield*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出QASP（查询自适应稳健向量搜索策略），通过一次性监督回归预测每条查询的完整召回率进展曲线，从而为任意召回目标推导搜索策略，避免搜索期间的迭代模型调用或为目标分别训练独立的预测器。该方法在减少80%数据访问的同时达到99%召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量搜索面临的核心挑战是在最小化计算成本的同时保持高召回率。固定搜索参数在不同查询间产生显著的性能差异，而传统的平均召回率评估会掩盖这些逐查询的不一致性，导致部分查询性能严重下降。

Method: QASP通过一次前置监督回归预测每条查询的完整召回进展曲线，再利用该曲线为任意召回目标推导搜索策略。它使用尺度不变特征和搜索前推理来预测归一化召回值，使其能够泛化到不同召回目标、索引配置和数据集。此外，其细粒度的进展预测支持一个轻量级反应式补充机制，根据预测与实际偏差调整搜索深度，无需额外推理。

Result: QASP显著降低了召回率方差和目标偏差，提高了查询满意度，并能扩展到大规模数据及分层索引而无需重新训练，在降低80%数据访问的同时实现了99%的召回率。

Conclusion: QASP在理论上证明了有限训练样本的可行性（与数据集大小和维度无关），其损失超过任何固定策略不可约下界的幅度趋于零，且相比固定探测的数据访问节省随内在维度呈指数增长，实验验证了其高效性和稳健性。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge of vector search is achieving consistently high recall while minimizing computational costs. Fixed search parameters cause significant performance variance across queries, and conventional evaluation on average recall masks these per-query disparities. We introduce QASP (Query-Adaptive robust vector Search Policy), which predicts the complete recall progression curve per query via a single upfront supervised regression, from which a search policy is derived for any recall target; this avoids iterative model invocations during search or separate predictors per target. By predicting normalized recall values with scale-invariant features and pre-search inference, QASP generalizes across recall targets, index configurations, and datasets. Its fine-grained progress predictions further enable a lightweight reactive complement that adjusts search depth based on predicted-versus-observed deviations without additional inference. We prove that QASP requires a finite training sample independent of dataset size and dimensionality, that its loss exceeds the irreducible lower bound of any fixed policy by a vanishing margin, and that its data access savings over fixed probing grow exponentially in intrinsic dimensionality. Experimentally, QASP achieves significantly lower recall variance and deviation from target, higher query satisfaction rate, and scales to large data and hierarchical indices without retraining, achieving 99% recall with 80% less data access.

</details>

### [41] [Curriculum Matters: Data-Efficient Relational PFN Pretraining with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29120)
*Mohammad Sadeq Abolhasani,Viswanath Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了关系型先验数据拟合网络（PFN）的三个核心问题：替代生成器PluRel能否替代RDB-PFN的先验、合成数据的呈现顺序对性能的影响、以及仅使用单表合成预训练能获得多少关系推理能力。研究发现渐进式课程学习（从7列到17列逐步拓宽）以约13,300个合成表达到0.703 ROC-AUC，相比全量一次性训练有明显优势；关系课程以约5,500个合成数据库恢复RDB-PFN 88%的性能；单表课程模型直接评估关系任务几乎匹配备用关系管道。结论是课程设计与数据多样性比特定生成器或原始合成规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探究关系型先验数据拟合网络（如RDB-PFN）的预训练范式中三个关键但尚未深入了解的问题：不同合成生成器是否可替代、合成数据呈现顺序的影响、以及单表预训练能获得多少关系推理能力，以验证课程设计和数据多样性在关系PFN预训练中的相对重要性。

Method: 使用PluRel作为唯一合成数据源设计多组对照实验：(i) 渐进式单表课程，将模式复杂度从7列逐步扩展到17列，总计约13,300个合成表；(ii) 从头开始的关系课程，仅使用约5,500个PluRel数据库；(iii) 直接评估单表课程模型在关系基准上的表现，无需关系适应。每个实验与RDB-PFN的报告结果及全量一次性训练进行对比。

Result: (i) 渐进式单表课程在23任务表格基准上达到0.703平均ROC-AUC，仅用约13,300个合成表（比RDB-PFN报告的启动配方少约45倍），而全量一次性训练仅达0.541；(ii) 关系课程从零开始仅用约5,500个PluRel数据库达到0.638平均ROC-AUC，恢复RDB-PFN报告性能的88%，且关系合成数据减少约220倍；(iii) 单表课程模型在关系基准上直接评估达到0.631，几乎匹配备用关系管道。

Conclusion: 课程设计与合成数据多样性对关系PFN预训练的影响可能比特定关系生成器或原始合成数据规模更为关键，这一发现挑战了传统上对先验生成器选择和大量合成数据的依赖假设。

Abstract: Relational Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) such as RDB-PFN approximate Bayesian inference over multi-table relational databases by pretraining on millions of synthetic tasks. We investigate three intertwined questions about this paradigm. First, can a structurally different synthetic generator PluRel substitute for RDB-PFN's prior? Second, how much does the order in which synthetic data is presented to the PFN affect downstream performance? Third, how much relational reasoning can a PFN acquire from single-table synthetic pretraining alone, before any relational data is introduced? Using PluRel as the sole synthetic data source across all experiments, we find: (i) a progressive single-table curriculum that gradually widens schema complexity from 7 to 17 columns reaches 0.703 average ROC-AUC on the 23-task tabular benchmark using only approximately 13,300 synthetic tables (approximately 45x fewer single-table datasets than RDB-PFN's reported warm-up recipe), while the same data trained all-at-once collapses to 0.541 ROC-AUC; (ii) a relational curriculum trained from scratch on only approximately 5,500 PluRel databases reaches 0.638 average ROC-AUC on the 19-task RelBench/4DBInfer benchmark, recovering 88% of RDB-PFN's reported performance with approximately 220x less relational synthetic data; and (iii) the single-table curriculum model, evaluated directly on the relational benchmark without any relational adaptation, achieves 0.631, nearly matching the dedicated relational pipeline. Together, these findings suggest that curriculum design and synthetic data diversity may matter more for relational PFN pretraining than the specific relational generator or raw synthetic scale alone.

</details>


### [42] [Topology-Aware Data Movement for Disaggregated GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28633)
*Sanjeev Rao Ganjihal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向去聚合LLM推理的拓扑感知传输编排器，通过感知互连层次结构并为每次传输选择最优传输方式，解决了KV缓存跨GPU池传输的网络瓶颈问题，相较于均匀RDMA方案可降低3-18倍的传输延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去聚合LLM推理中，prefill和decode运行在分离的GPU池上，需要在两者之间传输KV缓存（70B模型每请求2.6GB，生产规模下超过100GB/s聚合）。然而现有系统（DistServe、Splitwise、Mooncake）均使用均匀RDMA，完全忽略了不同GPU之间物理关系导致的72倍带宽差异（NVLink 900GB/s、InfiniBand 50GB/s、TCP 12.5GB/s）。

Method: 设计了一个拓扑感知传输编排器，在启动时发现互连层次结构并为每次传输选择最优传输方式。包含三种协同机制：(1) 流水线逐层传输，将传输与正在进行的prefill计算重叠，可隐藏60-85%的延迟；(2) 针对MoE模型的NVLink域感知放置，联合优化专家调度与KV缓存局部性；(3) 使用CXL 3.0内存扩展器作为共享溢出层，提供6倍容量且延迟比NVMe低86倍。

Result: 由于需要多节点异构互连集群和CXL 3.0硬件（超出学术资源且GPU云尚未提供），本文提供解析带宽模型、组件实现和三种架构下的投影分析，显示相较于均匀RDMA可降低3-18倍的传输延迟。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知是去聚合LLM推理传输优化的关键方向，现有均匀RDMA方案在异构互连环境中存在显著性能缺陷，但完整验证仍需等待硬件可用性。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM inference creates a datacenter networking problem that no existing system solves correctly. When prefill and decode run on separate GPU pools, the KV cache must be transferred between them. For a 70B model this is 2.6 GB per request, exceeding 100 GB/s aggregate at production scale. Yet DistServe, Splitwise, and Mooncake all use uniform RDMA, ignoring that bandwidth between two GPUs varies by 72x depending on their physical relationship: 900 GB/s via NVLink within a domain, 50 GB/s via InfiniBand across nodes, 12.5 GB/s via TCP across data centers. We design a topology-aware transfer orchestrator that discovers interconnect hierarchy at startup and selects optimal transport per transfer. Three mechanisms work together: (1) pipelined layer-by-layer transfer that overlaps transmission with ongoing prefill, hiding 60 to 85 percent of latency behind computation; (2) NVLink domain-aware placement for Mixture-of-Experts models that co-optimizes expert dispatch with KV cache locality; and (3) CXL 3.0 memory expanders as a shared overflow tier providing 6x capacity at 86x lower latency than NVMe. Full evaluation requires multi-node clusters with heterogeneous interconnects and CXL 3.0 hardware that is beyond academic resources and not yet available in GPU clouds. We present analytical bandwidth models, component implementations, and projected analysis across three architectures showing 3 to 18x transfer latency reduction over uniform RDMA.

</details>


### [43] [Sensitivity Analysis of GRU, LSTM and Transformer Encoder in Classification of Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28665)
*Bidhya Shrestha,Christos Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅使用车辆远程信息处理数据来识别Level 2自动驾驶系统（Comma Openpilot、Tesla Autopilot、Cadillac Super Cruise及手动驾驶）的三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer），并引入了一个模块化鲁棒性评估框架，模拟五种损坏类别、五个严重级别的遥测数据退化。结果显示干净数据下Macro F1达0.90-0.93，但时间抖动会严重损害性能（F1降至0.44-0.50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着软件定义汽车（SDVs）能够运行多个自动驾驶系统（包括原生和售后市场如Comma.ai的Openpilot），独立监控系统以验证哪个自动驾驶系统处于激活状态对于安全监控、法规合规、保险评估和异常检测至关重要。

Method: 1) 评估三种基于序列的分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer编码器）用于识别Level 2 ADS；2) 引入模块化鲁棒性评估框架，通过五种损坏类别（加性高斯白噪声+累积漂移、相关跨通道噪声、时间抖动、突发丢失、延迟转换/虚假切换/跨特征不一致）在五个严重级别（L1-L5）模拟真实的遥测数据退化；3) 使用Macro-F1作为鲁棒性度量指标。

Result: 三种模型在干净数据上均表现良好，Macro F1分别为0.92（GRU）、0.90（LSTM）、0.93（Transformer）；威胁匹配训练达到0.904-0.916 Macro F1且仅产生轻微干净数据惩罚。鲁棒性评估显示明显分化：事件级损坏仅略微降低F1（L5下≥0.87），而时间抖动使Macro F1骤降至0.44-0.50。

Conclusion: 序列模型在识别Level 2自动驾驶系统方面表现有效，但时间抖动是关键的鲁棒性弱点，需要在真实部署中重点解决；事件级损坏对模型影响较小，表明模型对通信类错误具有较好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated driving systems (ADSs) are becoming ubiquitous. Future Software Defined Vehicles (SDVs) may be able to run multiple ADSs, both native and aftermarket such as Comma.ai's Openpilot. Monitoring systems to independently verify which automated driving system is active are important for safety monitoring, regulatory compliance, insurance assessment, and anomaly detection. In this paper, we first evaluate the effectiveness of three sequence-based classification models: Gated Recurrent Units (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and a Transformer encoder model for identifying Level 2 automated driving systems using vehicle telematics data alone: Comma Openpilot, Tesla Autopilot, and Cadillac Super Cruise, along with manual driving. All three models achieve strong clean-data performance with macro F1-scores of 0.92 (GRU), 0.90 (LSTM), and 0.93 (Transformer encoder model) when trained on clean data; threat-matched training yields 0.904-0.916 macro F1 with only a modest clean-data penalty. Second, we introduce a modular robustness evaluation framework that simulates realistic telematics degradation through five corruption families at five severity levels (L1-L5). Continuous channels are perturbed using additive white Gaussian noise with cumulative drift, correlated cross-channel noise, and temporal jitter. Binary event signals are subjected to burst loss, delayed transitions, spurious toggles and cross-feature inconsistencies inspired by communication errors. Robustness is measured using macro-F1, which gives equal weight to each class and is suitable for imbalanced multiclass evaluation. Our evaluation reveals a sharp failure-mode split: event-level corruptions reduce macro-F1 only slightly (greater than equal to 0.87 at L5), while temporal jitter collapses macro-F1 to 0.44-0.50 across GRU, LSTM, and Transformer encoder model.

</details>


### [44] [Guarantees on Dynamical System Distinguishability for LLM Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28667)
*Mohamed Akrout,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将LLM响应分类形式化为两个随机线性动力系统之间的二元假设检验，从理论上解释了基于动力系统（DS）的LLM分类方法为何有效、如何随序列长度扩展以及何时在不同嵌入模型间迁移。研究发现忽略动力学的分类器存在精度下限，DS分类的误分类概率随序列长度指数衰减，并推导了跨嵌入泛化的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于动力系统的方法在区分LLM响应方面取得了经验上的成功，但缺乏理论理解：为什么有效、如何随token序列扩展、何时能跨嵌入模型迁移。

Method: 将分类任务形式化为两个随机线性动力系统之间的二元假设检验；以总变差距离度量两种DS稳态边缘分布差异；推导误分类概率随序列长度的指数衰减率；通过近似交织条件刻画嵌入模型间迁移性并使用交织映射最小奇异值建立可迁移判别性下界。

Result: （1）即使动力学差异显著，两个DS稳态边缘分布的总变差距离可以任意小，为忽略token动力学的分类器提供了基本精度下限；（2）DS分类的误分类概率随序列长度L指数衰减，衰减速率由动力判别量δ²决定；（3）建立了跨嵌入泛化能力与交织映射奇异值之间的定量关联。

Conclusion: 这些结果从理论上解释了DS分类方法的经验表现，并激励进一步利用动力系统理论分析AI系统，而非仅用AI建模动力系统。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that classifying large language models (LLMs)' responses can be distinguished by modeling token embeddings as trajectories of a black-box dynamical system (DS) and comparing prediction residuals of two DSs. Despite the empirical success of this dynamical approach, a theoretical understanding of why it works, how well it scales as a function of the token sequence, and when it transfers across embedding models remains lacking. We address these questions by formalizing the classification task as a binary hypothesis test between two stochastic linear DSs. We show that the total variation distance between the stationary marginal distributions of the two DSs can be arbitrarily small even when the dynamics differ substantially, which provides a fundamental accuracy floor for any classifier that ignores token dynamics. We then show that the misclassification probability of DS-based classification decays exponentially in the sequence length $L$, with the decay governed by a dynamical discriminability quantity $δ^2$ that captures the spectral distance between the two DSs. We also characterize cross-embedding generalization by introducing an approximate intertwining condition between embedding models and establishing a lower bound on the transferable discriminability in terms of the intertwining map's smallest singular value. Together, these results explain the empirical performance of DS-based classification and motivate further investigation into using DS theory to analyze AI systems, in contrast to the more common approach of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [45] [LARA: Lightweight Adapters in the Residual Stream for Composable Adaptation and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28669)
*Pascal Ekin,Hyosun Choi,Wei Jie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LARA（轻量级加性残差适配）方法，在冻结模型的残差流中而非权重中加入低秩校正，以极小的参数开销实现与LoRA相当的微调和偏好优化性能，并支持多个行为同时驻留、按token自动路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）在权重空间中添加低秩更新，无法提供插值控制，且多个行为需要分别加载模型，难以在单一设备上高效托管多个微调行为。

Method: LARA在冻结基础模型的少数层的残差流上读取隐藏状态并添加低秩校正，所有基座权重保持不变；引入推理时缩放因子γ，实现从基座到适配行为的平滑插值；每个行为作为独立的小型残差模块共享冻结基座，可按token自动路由。

Result: 在代码微调任务和DPO偏好优化上与LoRA在同等参数条件下表现相当；在一个冻结的1.5B模型上同时驻留7个行为（6个微调+1个偏好优化），仅需约33MB额外开销。

Conclusion: LARA通过在残差流中的加性适配实现了权重空间适配所不具备的分级控制和多行为共存能力，为在单设备上托管多个行为提供了高效方案。

Abstract: We present LARA (Lightweight Additive Residual Adaptation), a method for efficient adaptation that operates in the residual stream of a frozen model rather than in its weights. Where LoRA adds an update of low rank to weight matrices, LARA reads the hidden state at a small set of layers and adds a correction of low rank back to the residual stream, leaving all base weights untouched. On a code fine-tuning task and on preference optimization (DPO), LARA matches LoRA at equal parameter counts. Because adaptation is a frozen base plus a residual, LARA exposes a scale γ, applied at inference, that interpolates smoothly between base and adapted behavior, a form of graded control that adaptation in weight space does not offer. Finally, because each behavior is a small residual module over a shared frozen base, many behaviors can be held resident at once and routed automatically per token. We place seven behaviors, six fine-tuned and one optimized for preference, on one frozen 1.5B model for roughly 33 MB of overhead, against one full model for each behavior. Because the base is untouched, behaviors are trained separately and selected per token rather than loaded on demand, which suits hosting many behaviors, and adding new ones, on a single model on a device.

</details>


### [46] [Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-\texorpdfstring{$K$}{K} Routing: Two-Sided Dependence Control for Frozen Mixture-of-Experts at Fixed Per-Token Routing Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28670)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-K（CGA）的随机路由器构造方法，在保持每个token边际路由分布完全不变的前提下，通过高斯copula和反演构造来灵活调节不同token路由选择之间的正/负相关性，从而同时控制专家负载方差和专家集一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型的Gumbel-Top-K路由器对每个token定义了路由分布，但现有方法无法在不改变单个token路由规律的前提下，灵活控制不同token之间路由选择的联合分布。实际应用中既希望相关token（如同一组）选择相同的专家以提升一致性，又希望控制整体专家负载方差，这两者之间存在矛盾，需要一个可调的相关性控制机制。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-K（CGA）构造：1）在同一组token内，使用可交换的高斯copula对Gumbel扰动在各专家坐标上施加正相关，增强组内专家集一致性；2）在不相交的token对组之间，采用可调的反演（antithetic）构造引入可控的负依赖。两种操作均被证明不改变每个token的有序Top-K采样、混合权重和包含概率的边缘分布。通过一个基于冻结特征的轻量控制器，利用score-function梯度估计器进行训练，保持基础模型不动。

Result: 理论分析刻画了权衡关系：组内正耦合只会增大已实现专家负载的方差（相对独立路由），而组间非负反对称依赖只能降低方差（相对同组内强度的平坦耦合）。因此一致性和负载分散性由两个互补的依赖旋钮在不变约束面上控制。小规模实验验证了机制和训练路径的有效性，但未建立任务级微调增益。

Conclusion: 该工作为MoE路由器的联合分布控制提供了理论严谨的构造框架，将"一致性"与"负载均衡"分别映射为两个可独立调节的相关性维度，并通过冻结特征上的轻量控制器实现可训练的依赖管理，为大规模MoE模型的细粒度路由调控开辟了新途径，但仍需更大规模的实验验证其实际任务收益。

Abstract: A stochastic Gumbel-Top-$K$ router defines, for every token of a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model, a \emph{routing law}: a distribution over ordered expert lists and mixture weights. We ask which \emph{joint} distributions over the routing choices of different tokens are reachable while every individual token's complete routing law is held exactly fixed. We give a two-sided construction, \emph{Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-$K$} (\CGA{}). Within a group of related tokens, an exchangeable Gaussian copula positively correlates the Gumbel perturbations at each expert coordinate, which can increase within-group expert-set coherence. Across disjoint pairs of groups, a tunable antithetic construction introduces a selectable amount of negative dependence. We prove that both operations leave each token's ordered Top-$K$ sample, mixture weights, and inclusion probabilities identical in distribution to independent routing \emph{at a routing layer conditioned on its pre-routing logits}; conditional expected expert traffic is preserved as a consequence. We characterize the resulting trade-off: positive within-group coupling can only inflate the variance of realized expert loads relative to independent routing, while nonnegative cross-group opposition can only reduce it relative to flat coupling at the same within-group strength. Coherence and load dispersion are thus controlled by two complementary dependence dials on the invariance constraint surface. Because the base model is untouched, the dials can be driven by a small controller over frozen features, trainable with a score-function estimator: the frozen network is evaluated only in the forward direction, and gradients are confined to the controller. An initial small-scale pilot validates the mechanism and the training route, but does not establish task-level fine-tuning gains.

</details>


### [47] [LAWFUL: Law-Aligned Witness for Faithful Use of Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28672)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAWFUL框架，用于检验神经网络是否真正学习并在内部计算中使用物理定律（如多普勒频率公式），弥补了四个可解释性空白，并在Mocap2Radar transformer上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当神经网络能准确预测物理系统时，我们无法确定它是否真正学到了形式化的物理定律，以及其内部计算是否在整个定律有效域内使用该表示。现有可解释性方法在连续变量的物理定律上存在四个空白：缺乏覆盖感知的因果一致性度量、缺乏电路有效域测试、缺乏对定律不变量的验证、缺乏对派生物理量在电路中流动的量化。

Method: 开发LAWFUL框架，旨在弥合前两个可解释性空白（覆盖感知的因果一致性度量和电路有效域测试），并为后两个空白奠定基础。在Mocap2Radar transformer上进行验证，该模型中f(t)和v(t)均未直接出现在输入中，检验模型是否学会并内部使用多普勒频率定律。

Result: LAWFUL框架成功验证了Mocap2Radar transformer是否学习并在内部使用多普勒频率定律，并给出了从运动捕捉和雷达数据中推导该定律的因果一致性和有效域分析结果。

Conclusion: 这项工作为理解神经网络是否以及如何内化物理定律提供了系统化框架，填补了连续变量场景下可解释性方法的关键空白，为后续验证定律不变量和物理量流动路径的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: When a neural network predicts a physical system accurately, has it learned the governing law as formal, structured knowledge, and if so, does the network's internal computation actually use that representation throughout the law's domain of validity? We identify four interpretability gaps that limit answering these questions for {\em physics laws over continuous variables}: the absence of a coverage-aware causal-consistency measure over continuous counterfactuals; of a domain-of-validity test for the identified circuit; of a verification of the law's invariants and forbidden behaviors; and of a quantification of how a derived physical quantity flows through the circuit. We develop a foundational framework, LAWFUL, that closes the first two and lays groundwork for the remaining two, and illustrate it on the Mocap2Radar transformer, validating whether it learns and internally uses the Doppler frequency law $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}λ$ from motion-capture and radar data in which neither $f(t)$ nor $v(t)$ appears.

</details>


### [48] [MPP-GNN: Subject-Adaptive Community Detection for fMRI-Based Alzheimer's Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28681)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Jonathan Warrell,Avram Holmes,Xuan Zhang,Mark Gerstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MPP-GNN，一种元概率池化图神经网络，通过双层优化实现自适应分层图划分来发现受试者特异性的脑功能模块，并将发现的模块作为显式先验指导边细化和表示学习，在阿尔茨海默病分类中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的脑功能连接分析方法通常假设所有受试者具有预设数量的功能模块，忽略了个体间变异性；此外，发现的模块很少被直接用来指导学习到的连接模式。

Method: MPP-GNN将模型任务建模为耦合的双层优化，通过自适应分层图划分来发现受试者特异性的脑模块，然后将这些模块作为显式先验来指导边细化和表示学习过程。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上进行AD分类验证，MPP-GNN在两个数据集上均取得了最高的AUC，优于既有基线方法。

Conclusion: MPP-GNN发现的大脑模块与Yeo脑图谱定义的典型功能网络组织具有显著对齐性，并揭示了AD的网络级去分化模式，为脑疾病的图神经网络分析提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a widely used technique for studying the brain. Recent methods that utilize graph neural networks (GNNs) for analysis of brain functional connectivity have shown great potential for the classification of brain disorders, such as Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, these methods often assume a preset number of functional modules across all subjects, which overlooks inter-subject variability. In addition, the discovered modules are rarely used to directly guide the learned connectivity patterns. Here, to address these issues, we propose a Meta Probabilistic Pooling GNN (MPP-GNN). We frame the model's task as a coupled, bilevel optimization that performs adaptive graph partitioning hierarchically to discover subject-specific modules and then uses the discovered brain modules as an explicit prior to guide edge refinement and representation learning. We validate MPP-GNN on two public datasets for AD classification, achieving the highest AUC in comparison to established baselines for both datasets. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that MPP-GNN shows significant alignment with the canonical functional-network organization defined by the Yeo brain atlas and reveals a network-level dedifferentiation pattern for AD.

</details>

*Mohammad Asif,Azizuddin Khan,Mohd Azam,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了用于老年人轻度认知障碍（MCI）及痴呆早期检测与管理的多模态技术进展，涵盖EEG、神经影像、血液生物标志物和数字标记，并提出了跨学科分类体系、方法学严谨性评估框架及早期检测干预整合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人口老龄化，从MCI到痴呆的认知衰退成为未来数十年的重大健康挑战，但常规评估常漏掉最早期信号，亟需整合新兴技术以提升早期检测能力。

Method: 采用批判性综合综述方法，系统梳理神经电生理信号（EEG）、结构/分子神经影像（MRI及淀粉样蛋白/tau PET）、血液生物标志物和数字标记等检测手段，并整合AI/ML/DL技术进行多模态分析；同时提出跨学科分类体系、强调主体与部位无关验证的方法学视角，以及将分层筛查与干预相衔接的整合性早期检测框架。

Result: EEG标记物（alpha/theta变化、P300潜伏期）及深度学习模型（CNN、LSTM/BiLSTM、Transformer、自监督EEG基础模型）表现出较高准确率，但多基于小型单中心数据集。血浆p-tau217已达临床实用水平，2025年首个辅助阿尔茨海默病诊断的血液检测获批；抗淀粉样蛋白疗法（lecanemab、donanemab）已获批但疗效存在争议；多领域生活方式干预已趋成熟。可穿戴、远程、语音和虚拟现实工具支持持续生态化监测，多模态融合提升灵敏度和特异度。

Conclusion: 该领域近期发展前景在于构建可信赖、多模态、经纵向验证的系统，将早期检测与可操作、个体化的护理相衔接。当前主要障碍包括标准化不足、可解释性欠缺、数据隐私问题以及缺乏公平的外部验证部署。

Abstract: As populations age, cognitive decline from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to dementia is a defining health challenge of the coming decades, yet routine assessment often misses its earliest signs. This article critically synthesizes recent technological advances for detecting and managing cognitive impairment in older adults, spanning neurophysiological signals (chiefly electroencephalography, EEG), structural and molecular neuroimaging (MRI and amyloid/tau PET), blood-based biomarkers, and digital markers, integrated through artificial intelligence (AI), machine learning (ML), and deep learning (DL). Beyond summarizing, it contributes a cross-disciplinary taxonomy, a methodological-rigor lens foregrounding subject- and site-independent validation, an integrative early-detection framework linking tiered screening to intervention, and comparison tables of detection methods, interventions, and risk and protective factors. EEG markers (alpha/theta changes, P300 latency) and deep models (CNNs, LSTM/BiLSTM, transformers, self-supervised EEG foundation models) report strong accuracy, yet many rest on small, single-site datasets unlikely to survive rigorous external validation. Elsewhere, gains are tangible: plasma p-tau217 has reached clinical utility, with the first blood test cleared to aid Alzheimer's diagnosis in 2025; anti-amyloid therapies (lecanemab, donanemab) are approved despite modest, contested benefits; and multidomain lifestyle prevention has matured. Wearable, remote, speech, and virtual-reality tools enable continuous, ecologically valid monitoring, and multimodal fusion improves sensitivity and specificity. Barriers remain: standardization, explainability, data privacy, and equitable, externally validated deployment. The field's near-term promise lies in trustworthy, multimodal, longitudinally validated systems linking early detection to actionable, personalized care.

</details>

*WenYang Zhong,Tutut Herawan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM-BiLSTM混合深度学习模型的流式HDFS日志块异常检测工作流，结合并行计算处理历史日志和Kafka构建实时日志流水线，帮助运维人员快速准确地定位系统异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HDFS分布式文件系统会产生大量日志，且日志多为非结构化、不稳定的数据。传统的人工逐条检查日志来检测系统问题既复杂又枯燥，需要自动化方法来快速准确地检测HDFS块异常。

Method: 提出流式HDFS日志块异常检测工作流，包含三个核心部分：使用并行计算网络处理历史日志、构建LLM-BiLSTM混合深度学习模型检测异常块、基于Kafka构建流式日志管道实现实时异常检测。

Result: 利用机器学习和自然语言处理技术实现了HDFS块异常的自动检测，提供了历史日志处理和实时检测的完整解决方案，帮助运维人员快速准确定位和修复异常。

Conclusion: 该工作流为分布式文件系统维护提供了高效的自动化异常检测方案，结合深度学习和大数据流处理技术，能够提升系统运维效率和系统可用性。

Abstract: In recent years, with the development of big data technology, increasingly more companies use HDFS for data processing and storage. As a result, the maintenance of distributed file systems has become an extremely important part of data management. As the function of server systems is becoming increasingly diversified and their services are becoming complex, the logs, recording real-time events make it easier for system operators to locate the failures and errors that happened in the server systems to make server always available. HDFS, a distributed file system, which contains large data sets, will record a large number of logs. Moreover, the logs are not always structured data, they are not stable as well. However, to detect the problems that occur in the system by checking one log by one log, it's complicated and boring work for the system operators. Using machine learning techniques and natural language processing techniques to detect the HDFS block anomaly will help the system operators to locate and fix the anomaly rapidly and accurately. This paper proposes a streaming HDFS log block anomaly workflow. It helps maintenance practitioners to use parallel computing network in processing historical log, and construct LLM-BiLSTM hybrid deep learning model to detect anomaly block in HDFS, then build streaming log pipeline based on Kafka to give one real-time HDFS log block anomaly detection solution.

</details>


### [51] [SEDR-Seq2P: A Lightweight Dilated Residual Sequence-to-Point Network for Multi-Task Industrial NILM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28693)
*Hatem Haddad,Feres Jerbi,Issam Smaali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对工业NILM中测量噪声和并发设备运行导致的模型泛化问题，提出了一种轻量级的SEDR-Seq2P模型（基于Seq2Point的膨胀残差块+SE注意力扩展），在IMDELD统一评测协议下，相比Seq2Point基线降低MAE约7%、提升R²约1%、匹配率提升约0.8%，相比WaveNet推理延迟降低约58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景下的NILM（非侵入式负载监测）面临测量噪声大、多种机器并发运行等挑战，使基于住宅数据训练的模型泛化能力下降，亟需针对工业场景的高效且准确的负荷分解方案。

Method: 采用一对多（one-to-many）多任务分解框架，单网络同时估计多个工业机器负荷。在IMDELD数据集上统一评测Seq2Seq、Seq2SubSeq、Seq2Point、GRU、WaveNet五种基线；提出SEDR-Seq2P，即在Seq2Point基础上引入膨胀残差块和Squeeze-and-Excitation注意力机制。

Result: Seq2Point在准确率-延迟权衡上优于Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq；GRU和WaveNet准确率更高但计算代价显著更大。SEDR-Seq2P相比Seq2Point：MAE降低约7%，R²提升约1%，匹配率提升约0.8%；相比WaveNet推理延迟降低约58%，实现了更优的准确率-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: SEDR-Seq2P在保持高分解准确率的同时显著降低推理延迟，为可扩展的工业NILM部署提供了有利的准确率-延迟折中方案。

Abstract: Industrial NILM remains challenging because measurement noise and widespread concurrent machine operation reduce the generalization of models tuned on residential data. This work adopts a one-to-many, multi-task disaggregation setting, in which a single network estimates multiple industrial machine loads from aggregate power. Under a unified evaluation protocol on IMDELD, we benchmark Seq2Seq, Seq2SubSeq, Seq2Point, GRU, and WaveNet using energy-estimation metrics and the accuracy-delay criterion. While Seq2Point offers a stronger accuracy-delay balance than Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq, GRU and WaveNet achieve higher accuracy at markedly higher computational cost. To close this gap, we propose SEDR-Seq2P, a lightweight Seq2Point extension with dilated residual blocks and squeeze-and-excitation attention. Relative to the Seq2Point baseline, SEDR-Seq2P reduces MAE by approximately 7%, improves the coefficient of determination by approximately 1%, and increases the match rate by approximately 0.8%. In addition, compared to WaveNet, SEDR-Seq2P reduces inference latency by approximately 58%, yielding a favorable accuracy-delay trade-off for scalable industrial deployment.

</details>


### [52] [GQ-FSL: Green Quantized Federated Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29659)
*Idan Roth,Lutz Lampe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种绿色量化联邦分割学习（GQ-FSL）框架，通过随机量化同时应用于本地协作训练和无线传输，支持客户端与服务器端子模型采用非对称精度，在满足目标精度约束下最小化系统总能耗，实现资源受限设备上的大规模DNN高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线边缘部署深度神经网络受到移动设备严格能源和资源限制的严重制约。联邦分割学习虽可减轻设备端计算负担，但持续交换切割层数据和子模型仍带来显著能耗开销，需要一种更绿色的方案来平衡通信、计算与能耗。

Method: 提出GQ-FSL框架，包含三部分：(1) 对本地协作训练和无线传输均采用随机量化；(2) 支持客户端与服务器端非对称精度级别，解耦设备能耗与全局收敛退化；(3) 建立参数化能耗模型并推导统计异质数据下的理论收敛界，进而构建联合优化问题配置DNN分割点和精度级别，在满足严格目标精度约束下最小化系统总能耗。

Result: GQ-FSL在资源受限设备上实现了大规模DNN部署，相较于量化联邦学习和全精度联邦分割学习，取得了更优的能源效率。

Conclusion: 通过非对称精度量化和联合优化分割点与精度配置，GQ-FSL有效解耦了设备能耗约束与全局收敛性能，为边缘智能提供了绿色可持续的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying state-of-the-art deep neural networks (DNNs) at the wireless edge is severely bottlenecked by the strict energy and resource constraints of mobile devices. While federated split learning (FSL) mitigates on-device computation by offloading workloads to an edge server, this may introduce systemic overheads, while the continuous exchange of cut-layer data, and submodels still incurs significant energy consumption (EC). To address this, we propose a green quantized FSL (GQ-FSL) framework that incorporates stochastic quantization for both local collaborative training and wireless transmissions. Notably, GQ-FSL supports asymmetric precision levels for the client- and server-side submodels, effectively decoupling device energy constraints from global convergence degradation. To quantify these tradeoffs, we develop parameterized energy models for the split architecture and derive a theoretical convergence bound under statistically heterogeneous data. Building on that, we formulate a joint optimization problem to configure the DNN split point and precision levels, minimizing the total system EC while satisfying a strict target accuracy constraint. Ultimately, we demonstrate that GQ-FSL enables large-scale DNN deployment on resource-constrained devices, achieving superior energy efficiency compared to quantized federated learning and full-precision FSL.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: ResNet-50在合成显微图像基准上取得最优性能：R²=0.93，RMSE=0.18 log周期，macro-F1=0.91。GNLL损失将期望校准误差从0.089降至0.021（降幅76%）。Grad-CAM热图验证网络关注冶金学上意义的微观结构特征，单图像推理时间低于65毫秒。

Abstract: Here is the plain text version optimized for arXiv's submission form. Custom macros (like \CV and \SI) have been converted to standard text/math so they render correctly on the webpage: Evaluating the fatigue life of structural steels conventionally requires mechanical testing lasting tens to hundreds of hours, making it impractical for rapid quality control. We present CV, a computer vision framework that estimates the fatigue life ($\log N_f$) of lightweight alloy steels directly from optical micrographs without physical testing.The pipeline features a seven-stage OpenCV preprocessing routine to remove artifacts, a 28-dimensional physics-informed feature extractor (quantifying crack morphology, grain structure, porosity, and texture), and a CNN regression model trained with a Gaussian negative log-likelihood (GNLL) loss to jointly predict $\log N_f$ and sample-specific uncertainty $\hatσ$.Evaluating three architectures (SE-CNN, ResNet-50, VGG-16) on a synthetic micrograph benchmark, ResNet-50 achieves $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE = 0.18 log-cycles, and macro-F1 = 0.91. The GNLL objective reduces Expected Calibration Error by 76% compared to a mean-squared-error baseline (ECE: $0.089 \rightarrow 0.021$). Grad-CAM maps confirm the network attends to metallurgically meaningful microstructural features.Running in under 65 ms per image, the pipeline and synthetic dataset generator are open-sourced. Because validation relies entirely on synthetic micrographs, these results demonstrate methodological soundness under simulated conditions; a domain-transfer study on real field samples is the immediate next step.

</details>


### [54] [Mitigating Class-Tail Undercoverage in Medical Vision-Language Models under Clinical Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28696)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CALCoDe，一种为冻结的医学视觉语言模型设计的后验可靠性层，通过交叉验证识别高风险类别、利用独立校准分割估计类别条件尾部阈值，并结合局部化共形阈值与单向最大值操作，在八个临床迁移设置中实现了0.95的边缘覆盖率和最差类别覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在临床分布偏移后可能出现整体边缘覆盖率正常但某一疾病类别严重欠覆盖的问题，且受影响的类别随采集协议和骨干网络几何变化而变化，源域患病率无法可靠预测失败。现有局部化和尾部感知的共形方法分别适配测试邻域和源频率尾部，无法建模留出类别上的覆盖率失败。

Method: CALCoDe包含三个核心组件：(1) 交叉拟合验证预测识别存在欠覆盖风险的类别；(2) 独立的校准分割估计这些类别的条件尾部阈值；(3) 将受保护阈值与局部化共形阈值通过单向最大值结合，产生的集合包含局部化规则接受的每个标签。此外，独立校准的支持审计将支撑不足的案例延后处理。

Result: 在两种皮肤病学迁移（HAM10000到ISIC 2019和HAM10000到PAD-UFES-20）和四种冻结VLM骨干网络（BiomedCLIP、OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32、PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32和MedSigLIP-448）的八个设置中，CALCoDe是唯一同时达到0.95边际覆盖率和最差类别覆盖率的模型。在HAM10000到ISIC 2019上，其平均最差类别覆盖率为0.970，优于sTACP的0.926和LCP-VLM的0.864。

Conclusion: CALCoDe在受保护类别内可交换性条件下提供有限样本覆盖率保证，并包含相应的局部化共形集合。作为冻结医学VLM的后验可靠性层，它在不修改模型内部结构的条件下有效解决了类别级欠覆盖问题，在临床迁移场景下显著优于现有共形方法。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can retain high observed marginal coverage after clinical shift while substantially under-covering an individual disease class. The affected class varies with acquisition protocol and backbone geometry, so source prevalence does not reliably reveal the failure. Existing localized and tail-aware conformal methods respectively adapt to test neighborhoods and source-frequency tails, leaving held-out class-wise coverage failure unmodeled. We introduce Class-Tail Adaptive Localized Conformal Deferral (CALCoDe), a post-hoc reliability layer for frozen medical VLMs. Cross-fitted validation predictions identify classes at risk of undercoverage, and a disjoint calibration split estimates their class-conditional tail thresholds. CALCoDe combines each protected threshold with a localized conformal threshold using a one-sided maximum. The resulting set contains every label admitted by the localized rule, with additional protection confined to validation-identified classes. An independently calibrated support audit defers cases with insufficient inlier support. Under exchangeability among accepted examples within each protected class, CALCoDe provides finite-sample coverage at the prespecified guard level and contains the corresponding localized conformal sets; coverage on shifted external cohorts is evaluated empirically. Among standard conformal baselines and recent VLM-specific conformal methods evaluated across two dermatology shifts (HAM10000 to ISIC 2019 and HAM10000 to PAD-UFES-20) and four frozen VLM backbones (BiomedCLIP, OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32, PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32, and MedSigLIP-448), CALCoDe is the only approach whose observed marginal and worst-class accepted coverage both reach 0.95 in all eight settings. On HAM10000 to ISIC 2019, its average worst-class accepted coverage is 0.970, compared with 0.926 for sTACP and 0.864 for LCP-VLM.

</details>


### [55] [Flow Matching with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28698)
*Fairoz Nower Khan,Nabuat Zaman Nahim,Peizhong Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出缺失数据流匹配（Missing-Data Flow Matching），将训练样本的缺失坐标视为潜在变量，并对所有可能取值平均流匹配损失。理论证明该修正在MCAR条件下是精确的而非近似的，并通过有限样本分析回答了设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配假设训练数据完全观测，但现实应用中数据经常存在缺失。现有方法无法直接处理不完整数据，需要一种理论严谨且实用的在缺失数据下训练流匹配模型的方法。

Method: 将缺失坐标作为潜在变量处理，对可能取值平均流匹配损失。在MCAR假设下证明不完整数据目标等于完整数据目标；通过有限样本分析研究估计器方差、每样本补全数量与评估预算的最优权衡。

Result: 理论证明缺失只转移估计器方差而非增加方差；每个样本仅需一次补全即可与完整数据方差完全匹配；固定评估预算下一次补全最优。实验验证了理论预测，并发现确定性插补会导致生成分布坍塌，而学习式补全模型仅引入单一可约偏差。

Conclusion: 缺失数据流匹配提供了一种理论严谨的缺失数据生成建模方案，将困难从损失计算转移到补全模型上，并在实际表格数据上优于经典和深度插补基线。

Abstract: Flow matching assumes fully observed training data, which many real-world applications rarely provide. We propose Missing-Data Flow Matching, which treats the missing coordinates of training samples as latent variables and averages the flow matching loss over the values they could take. We first prove the correction is exact rather than approximate. Under missing completely at random with true completions, the incomplete-data objective equals the complete-data objective, so missingness changes nothing about what flow matching learns and the entire difficulty relocates to the completion model. Our finite-sample analysis then answers design questions that the algorithm leaves open, and the answers are not the ones intuition suggests. Missingness transfers estimator variance rather than adding it, one completion per example already matches complete-data variance exactly, and under a fixed evaluation budget one completion is optimal. A learned completion model contributes a single irreducible bias, which we bound by its expected conditional Wasserstein distance to the true completion law. Experiments numerically validate the theoretical predictions, show that deterministic rather than frozen imputation is what collapses the generated distribution, and place our method alongside strong classical and deep imputation baselines on real tabular data.

</details>


### [56] [MMFGU: Multimodal Federated Graph Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28708)
*Haodong Lu,Zekai Chen,Weiwei Ji,Shihao Li,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MMFGU框架，用于多模态联邦图学习中的细粒度遗忘（unlearning）。它将异构遗忘请求映射为统一目标载体，通过目标特定表示解耦、轻量探针修复传播残差、以及基于原型和响应信号的选择性清理，实现高效且破坏性最小的多模态遗忘。实验显示MMFGU在有效移除目标信息的同时，相比全量重训练实现41.5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习中，异构内容导致遗忘请求更频繁且更细粒度（如删除单个图像/文本、撤销模态间对应关系等）。现有联邦图遗忘方法仅处理实体/关系或客户端移除，无法满足这些细粒度多模态请求，且面临三个挑战：仅移除请求信息而不损害保留内容、防止目标通过剩余模态或图邻域被恢复、阻止聚合后其他客户端的相关痕迹重新进入全局模型。

Method: MMFGU框架围绕目标特定表示解耦构建，包含四个核心步骤：1）将异构请求映射为统一目标载体；2）在解耦请求表示的同时锚定保留语义；3）利用轻量探针暴露并修复传播残差；4）通过紧凑的原型和响应信号选择性清理受影响的客户端。

Result: 实验表明MMFGU能有效移除请求信息，保留保留的图数据效用，并相比全量重训练实现41.5倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: MMFGU有效解决了多模态联邦图学习中的细粒度遗忘问题，在满足多样化遗忘请求的同时兼顾了数据效用和效率，为联邦图学习中的隐私保护提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal federated graph learning enables clients to collaboratively train graph models over structural, textual, and visual signals without sharing private local data. However, the presence of heterogeneous multimodal content also makes unlearning requests more frequent and fine-grained: users may delete accounts or interactions, remove a particular image or text while retaining the associated entity, or revoke the learned correspondence between retained modalities or graph attributes. Existing federated graph unlearning mainly handles entity/relation or client removal and cannot directly satisfy these multimodal requests. They introduce three challenges: removing only the requested information without damaging retained content, preventing the target from being recovered through remaining modalities or graph neighborhoods, and stopping related traces on other clients from re-entering the global model after aggregation. To address them, we propose \textsc{\textbf{MMFGU}}, a multimodal federated graph unlearning framework built around target-specific representation decoupling. \textsc{MMFGU} maps heterogeneous requests into unified target carriers, decouples requested representations while anchoring retained semantics, exposes and repairs propagated residuals with lightweight probes, and selectively purges affected clients through compact prototype and response signals. Experiments show that \textsc{MMFGU} effectively removes requested information, preserves retained graph utility, and achieves a $\boldsymbol{41.5\times}$ speedup over full retraining.

</details>


### [57] [Mirror Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28737)
*Yunpeng Liu,Matthew Niedoba,Oluwanifemi A. Adekanye,Jason Yoo,Yingchen He,Berend Zwartsenberg,Frank Wood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"镜像学习"（Mirror Learning）框架，通过将第三人称被动观察转化为第一人称"镜像数据"来训练强化学习策略。方法结合微调的视频扩散模型（实现视角变换）和逆动力学模型（推断动作），无需遥操作即可实现可扩展的模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）在稠密且对齐良好的第一人称数据下表现优异，但无法利用人类和动物常使用的第三人称观察信号。传统方法依赖遥操作等昂贵的数据采集方式，缺乏可扩展且安全的替代方案。

Method: 该方法由两部分组成：(1) 利用微调的视频扩散模型学习视角变换，将学习者置于演示者的视角（"穿上演示者的鞋子"）；(2) 利用逆动力学模型在学习者的控制空间中推断动作轨迹。两者结合将从第三人称观察中合成"镜像数据"——即伪第一人称专家数据。

Result: 实验表明，仅使用镜像数据即可训练出有效的策略；将镜像数据与第一人BC训练数据结合，还能进一步提升下游策略性能。

Conclusion: 现代生成式世界模型隐式编码了足够的结构信息，能够提供一种可扩展且安全的替代方案，减少对遥操作密集型数据采集的依赖。

Abstract: We investigate imitation learning through the lens of third-person observation and propose a framework for mirror learning: acquiring actionable policies from passive observation. While behavior cloning (BC) excels under dense, well-aligned first-person data, it fundamentally fails to leverage the rich observational signals arising from third-person demonstrations that humans and animals routinely exploit. We introduce a method that composes (i) a learned perspective transformation that places learners in demonstrators' shoes using a fine-tuned video diffusion model and (ii) an inverse dynamics model that infers action trajectories in the learners' control space. This enables the synthesis of mirror data, pseudo first-person expert data generated from third-person observations of demonstrator behavior. Empirically, we show that mirror data alone can train effective policies, and that augmenting first-person BC training with mirror data further improves downstream policy performance. Our results suggest that modern generative world models implicitly encode sufficient structure to enable a scalable and safe alternative to teleoperation-heavy data collection.

</details>


### [58] [TAGTorch: A PyTorch Library for Geometry, Topology, and Symmetry-Aware Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28755)
*Brendan Kennedy,Tegan Emerson,Gregory Roek,Emilie Purvine,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TAGTorch，一个基于PyTorch的开源库，用于统一拓扑、代数和几何启发的工具，包括数据预处理、架构、训练技术和模型分析工具，以解决当前软件生态系统碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络已被应用于越来越多具有几何、拓扑或对称结构的复杂数据领域，但支持这些方法的软件生态系统碎片化严重，许多重要方法仅以研究原型存在于未维护的代码库中。

Method: 提出并实现TAGTorch，一个开源、基于PyTorch的统一库，整合了拓扑、代数和几何启发的工具，涵盖数据预处理方法、架构、训练技术及模型分析工具。

Result: 描述了TAGTorch的设计理念、当前架构和能力，并指出了其在现有软件生态中可以填补的空白领域。

Conclusion: 文章总结了TAGTorch的现状，并对该库未来的开发优先级进行了展望。

Abstract: Over the last decade, neural networks have been applied to an increasingly diverse range of applications, including data with rich geometric, topological, or symmetry-related structure. As a result, researchers have increasingly drawn inspiration from topology, algebra, and geometry. Despite this rich algorithmic development, the supporting software ecosystem remains fragmented. Many important methods exist only as research prototypes in unmaintained repositories. We address this by introducing Topology, Algebra, and Geometry Torch (TAGTorch), an open-source, PyTorch-based library that unifies tools inspired by topology, algebra, and geometry, including data-preprocessing methods, architectures, training techniques, and model analysis tools. We describe the design philosophy of TAGTorch and then discuss its current architecture and capabilities, highlighting areas where it can fill gaps in the current software ecosystem. We conclude with a discussion of our future development priorities for the library.

</details>


### [59] [Feature Interaction Modeling for Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28762)
*Quan Gu,Hongxia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本工作将因子分解机（FM）中的特征交互模块嵌入物理信息神经网络（PINN）和神经算子学习中，通过显式建模时空变量及物理参数的交叉交互，提升参数化偏微分方程解流形的表达力。提出了FM-PINN、FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet三种方法，在非线性守恒律和具有尖锐梯度或间断的问题上取得了显著的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化偏微分方程的解流形往往具有复杂的变量耦合关系，而传统的PINN和神经算子方法难以充分捕捉这些特征交互，导致模型表达能力受限，尤其是在处理具有强交叉场依赖的方程时精度不足。

Method: 受多元函数二阶泰勒展开中变量耦合项的启发，首先提出FM-PINN，显式捕获时空变量交互以提高光滑高阶PDE的逼近精度。进一步将空间坐标、时间、物理参数和初边值条件分为独立特征集，建模跨组交互，在此基础上开发FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的机制在具有挑战性的激波主导方程上取得了显著的精度提升，尤其在非线性守恒律和具有尖锐梯度或间断的问题上表现突出。然而在光滑算子学习基准上未显示出一致的优势。

Conclusion: 特征交互机制为物理一致性建模参数化PDE提供了有前景的方向，特别适用于具有强交叉场依赖和间断特性的问题，但需要进一步探索其在光滑问题中的适用性。

Abstract: This work embeds feature interaction modules derived from factorization machines (FMs) into physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and neural operator learning, to enhance model expressiveness for solution manifolds of parameterized partial differential equations (PDEs). Motivated by the second-order Taylor expansion of multivariate functions to characterize variable couplings, we first propose FM-PINN. It explicitly captures spatio-temporal variable interactions and improves the approximation accuracy for smooth high-order PDEs. We further group spatial coordinates, time, physical parameters, and initial and boundary conditions into independent feature sets and model their cross-group interactions. Based on this strategy, we develop FM-Operator and FM-DeepONet, which are particularly effective for nonlinear conservation laws and problems with sharp gradients or discontinuities, while offering no consistent advantage on smooth operator learning benchmarks. Numerical tests demonstrate that the proposed mechanism delivers substantial accuracy gains on challenging shock-dominated equations, indicating a promising direction for physics-consistent modeling of parameterized PDEs with strong cross-field dependencies.

</details>


### [60] [Representations from Pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials as Coarse Coordinates for Material Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28776)
*Paul Hagemann,Katharina Ueltzen,Simon Müller,Janine George,Philipp Benner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了预训练机器学习原子间势能模型（如MACE）的原子平均特征在无机晶体结构生成中的强大作用，提出了一种新的分布评估框架CFTD，既能评估生成质量又能检测记忆化问题，并可将粗粒度MACE特征用作生成模型的引导信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体结构生成模型及其评估方法依赖于简单的晶体结构表示形式，缺乏能同时捕捉生成质量和新颖性的统一评估框架，也未能充分利用预训练势能模型的特征表示能力。

Method: 1）提出Coarse-Fine Transport Distance（CFTD）距离度量，基于两种不同的特征化器，其中质量评估部分使用粗粒度MACE特征；2）将CFTD与连续SUN指标进行对比；3）利用粗粒度MACE特征作为材料生成模型的条件引导信号。

Result: CFTD能够有效捕捉晶体结构质量并检测模型的记忆化行为，验证了MACE预训练特征在材料生成评估及引导方面的多样用途。

Conclusion: 预训练MLIP特征（如MACE）为晶体结构生成提供了一种更强大的表示方法，可用于统一的质量-新颖性评估以及生成过程的引导。

Abstract: Generative machine learning is increasingly used for inorganic crystal structure generation. Most models and the corresponding evaluation approaches rely on simple forms of crystal structure representation. In this paper, we showcase the power of atom-averaged features from pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs), such as MACE, for such tasks. We first introduce a distance measure that assesses the output of material generative models by capturing both quality and novelty in a single distribution-based evaluation framework. In particular, we introduce the Coarse-Fine Transport Distance (CFTD) using two different featurizers, where the quality component is based on coarse MACE features. We showcase CFTD's versatility in capturing crystal-structure quality while also detecting memorization, and compare it with the recently introduced continuous SUN metrics. We further show that coarse MACE features can be used as guidance for a material generative model.

</details>


### [61] [Distilling Knowledge from Large Language Models into Lightweight Reinforcement Learning Agents for Autonomous Cyber Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28826)
*Konur Tholl,François Rivest,Mariam El Mezouar,Adrian Taylor,Ranwa Al Mallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大语言模型（LLM）通过提示工程而非微调的方式改进自主网络防御决策，并提出了在线策略蒸馏框架，将LLM防御策略迁移到仅含64,910参数的小型RL代理中，在CybORG环境中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网络作战（ACO）中常用的强化学习（RL）代理需要大量探索训练，导致决策不稳定且初期表现差。研究者希望利用LLM在前沿领域（如网络安全）的预训练知识，弥补RL代理在训练初期的不足，同时解决大型模型难以直接部署的问题。

Method: 通过提示工程（而非微调）使用80亿参数的网络安全预训练LLM在修改的CybORG CAGE Challenge 2环境中指导防御决策；提出在线策略蒸馏框架，将LLM防御策略迁移至仅64,910参数的轻量RL代理；在4至12个主机规模的CybORG场景中评估跨网络配置的迁移性，并测试教师引导的RL稳定化策略。

Result: 80亿参数的LLM在防御决策上优于基线RL代理；蒸馏后的轻量代理大幅缩减模型规模（几个数量级）的同时保持了有效防御能力；教师引导的RL稳定化策略中，没有任何一种能持续超越优化后的教师策略，表明奖励驱动的RL优化与教师引导的防御策略之间存在策略对齐限制。

Conclusion: 网络安全领域LLM可作为自主网络防御的专业知识来源，策略蒸馏为将前沿网络安全模型部署到轻量、可扩展代理中提供了实用路径，兼顾了模型效能与部署可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous Cyber Operations (ACO) are increasingly important for defending enterprise networks as cyber threats continue to evolve in sophistication. ACO applications commonly employ Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents to learn defensive behaviors through interaction with environments.
