Conclusion: FruBO作为可复现的计算感知贝叶斯优化基线，为有限计算和实验预算下的替代模型选择提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely adopted for data-efficient optimization in scientific and engineering applications, yet its computational cost is rarely evaluated alongside optimization performance. Here we present a systematic, compute-aware study of BO that evaluates surrogate models along two axes: optimization quality and computational frugality. Across eight benchmark functions and nine real-world datasets spanning materials science, mechanics, robotics, chemistry, and machine learning, we benchmark four surrogate models: Gaussian Processes, Random Forests, NGBoost, and Bayesian Adaptive Spline Surfaces. We show that Gaussian Process-based BO consistently incurs the highest time and memory overhead without delivering superior optimization or sample efficiency. In contrast, scalable alternatives achieve equal or better performance at a fraction of the computational cost. Motivated by these findings, we introduce a surrogate-recommendation framework that predicts the most suitable BO surrogate from inexpensive dataset characteristics. Together, these results establish FruBO as a reproducible, compute-aware baseline for Bayesian Optimization and provide practical guidance for surrogate selection under limited computational and experimental budgets.

</details>


### [96] [UniPolymer: A Unified Framework for Property Prediction, Structure Recommendation, and Evaluation in Polyimide Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29256)
*Junquan Hu,Zhihui Wang,Peng Xu,Xinru Guo,Xintong Li,Kun Lu,Ben Fei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UniPolymer，一个统一框架，用于聚酰亚胺设计中的性能预测、目标条件生成、候选评估和结构推荐，并构建了包含10066个去重聚酰亚胺重复单元及Tg标签的数据集PITg-Curated。该框架通过自监督化学语义学习、结构一致性增强和多尺度信息融合建立可靠的结构-性能映射，采用连续-离散联合Tg表示引导SELFIES自回归生成，并用冻结性能预测器和聚酰亚胺特定结构约束对候选进行评估排序，R²达0.93，候选评估通过率达73.79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚酰亚胺玻璃化转变温度(Tg)设计方法主要聚焦于目标条件生成，缺乏对生成结构与目标性能一致性的评估，导致偏离设计目标的低质量候选进入后续流程，增加无效实验并延长开发周期。

Method: UniPolymer采用统一框架，包含：(1)通过自监督化学语义学习、结构一致性增强和多尺度信息融合建立可靠的结构-性能关系映射；(2)使用连续-离散联合Tg表示引导SELFIES自回归生成候选结构；(3)利用冻结性能预测器和聚酰亚胺特定结构约束对生成候选进行评估，并按与目标Tg的偏差进行排序，过滤偏离目标的结构。

Result: UniPolymer在性能预测上达到R²=0.93，候选结构评估通过率为73.79%，分别比最佳基线高出2%和1.21%。推荐候选的预测Tg值与分子动力学模拟结果高度一致，减少了进入高成本实验阶段的候选数量。

Conclusion: UniPolymer通过将生成与评估统一在同一框架内，有效提升了对目标Tg的生成一致性，降低了无效实验比例，缩短了聚酰亚胺材料开发周期，为性能导向的聚合物逆向设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Designing polyimide structures with specific glass transition temperatures (Tg) is highly challenging. Existing methods primarily focus on target-conditioned generation, lacking an assessment of the consistency between the generated structure and the target properties. This leads to low-quality candidates deviating from the design objective entering subsequent processes, increasing invalid experiments and prolonging the development cycle. To address this issue, we propose UniPolymer, a unified framework for property prediction, target-conditioned generation, candidate evaluation, and structure recommendation in polyimide design and a dataset containing 10066 deduplicated polyimide repeating units with Tg tags (PITg-Curated) was constructed. To improve the consistency between generated candidate structures and the target Tg, UniPolymer first establishes a reliable structure-property relationship mapping through self-supervised chemical semantic learning, structural consistency enhancement, and multi-scale information fusion. Subsequently, the model employs a continuous-discrete joint Tg representation to guide the autoregressive generation of SELFIES. The generated candidate structures are further evaluated using a frozen property predictor and polyimide-specific structural constraints, and ranked according to their deviation from the target Tg, thereby preventing structures deviating from the target from entering the subsequent validation stage. Experimental results show that UniPolymer achieved a property prediction accuracy of R^2=0.93 and a candidate structure evaluation pass rate of 73.79%, which are 2% and 1.21% higher than the best baseline, respectively. Meanwhile, the predicted Tg values of the recommended candidates are in high agreement with the results of molecular dynamics simulations, thereby reducing the number of candidates that enter the high-cost experimental stage.

</details>


### [97] [Assessing the Generalization of Graph Neural Networks for Fault Location Across Increasing Distributed Energy Resource Penetration Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29293)
*Burak Karabulut,Olayiwola Arowolo,Carlo Manna,Chris Develder,Jochen L. Cremer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了时空图注意力网络（STGATv2）在分布式能源（DER）高渗透率背景下配电网故障定位的性能，并对比了纯时序（GRU）、纯空间（GATv2）及传统机器学习基线模型，揭示了渗透率跨层泛化的不对称性规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DER渗透率的增加改变了故障特征（间歇性发电和双向潮流使故障信号复杂化），而现有STGNN方法在DER渗透率变化下的表现缺乏系统性研究，需要严格评估其泛化能力。

Method: 在重构的IEEE 123节点馈线上，设置10%、25%、50%三种DER渗透率水平及多个注入点和中高阻抗故障场景，系统对比STGATv2与GRU、GATv2和传统机器学习基线的故障定位性能，并评估跨渗透率泛化能力及含噪条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: STGATv2在分布内测试中达到92-94%宏观F1分数，优于所有基线；跨渗透率泛化呈不对称性——在50%渗透率训练可保持较低渗透率下的近分布内性能，而从10%训练退化严重；STGATv2在剧烈漂移下保持81-84% F1，远高于GATv2（69-74%）和GRU（73-75%）；在含噪声条件下STGATv2保持>85% F1，而GRU最低降至33.5%。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知能力（空间建模）对主动配电网故障定位的鲁棒性至关重要，STGATv2在DER高渗透率和噪声环境下均展现出显著优势，为DER渗透场景下的故障定位提供了可靠的建模方案。

Abstract: Accurate fault location is critical for distribution network reliability. However, increasing distributed energy resource (DER) penetration complicates fault location due to intermittent generation and bidirectional power flows that reshape fault signatures. Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (STGNNs) have shown promise by jointly modeling spatial and temporal dependencies, but their behavior under increasing DER penetration has not been studied rigorously. In this paper, we (i) systematically benchmark spatio-temporal graph attention network (STGATv2) against purely temporal (gated recurrent unit, GRU), purely spatial (GATv2) and traditional machine learning baselines, and (ii) evaluate how well models generalize across increasing DER penetration levels (10%, 25%, 50%) on a reconfigured IEEE 123-bus feeder with multiple DER injection points and moderate-to-high impedance faults. Results show that STGATv2 consistently outperforms neural baselines, achieving 92-94% macro F1 in-distribution. Notably, generalization across penetration levels is asymmetric: training at 50% penetration retains near in-distribution F1 score at lower levels, whereas training at 10% degrades considerably at 50% - with STGATv2 retaining 81-84% F1 under these drastic shifts, substantially higher than GATv2 and GRU which drop to 69-74% F1 and 73-75% F1 respectively. Under realistic measurement noise, STGATv2 maintains > 85% F1, while GRU drops as low as 33.5% F1, highlighting the critical role of topological awareness for robust fault location in active distribution networks.

</details>


### [98] [Sample Efficient Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning via Best Policy Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29294)
*Anders Jonsson,Emilie Kaufmann,Gianmarco Tedeschi,Lorenzo Steccanella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HBPI-UCRL，一种并行学习高层与低层策略的基于模型的分层强化学习算法。该算法利用高层转移对应于低层多步转移这一特性，提出了两个低层动力学条件，在这些条件下证明了算法具有多项式样本复杂度，并且在稀疏奖励目标导向场景下，其样本复杂度上界严格低于非分层算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层强化学习（HRL）在实践中表现出色，但缺乏严格的理论保证，尤其是高、低层策略并行学习时的可学习性和样本复杂度尚无明确的理论分析。本文旨在为HRL的并行学习提供理论基础，解释其相对于非分层方法的经验优势。

Method: 提出HBPI-UCRL算法，基于模型（model-based）的分层强化学习方法，同时学习高层和低层策略。其核心思想是利用高层转移与低层多步转移之间的对应关系，并引入两个关于低层动力学的充分条件，在这些条件下进行并行学习。

Result: 证明了当所提出的两个低层动力学条件成立时，HBPI-UCRL具有多项式样本复杂度；在稀疏奖励、目标导向设定下，其样本复杂度上界严格优于非分层对应算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为分层强化学习的并行学习提供了首个严格的理论保证，从理论上验证了HRL在样本效率方面优于非分层方法的经验观察。

Abstract: We present HBPI-UCRL, a model-based algorithm for hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) that learns high-level and low-level policies in parallel. HBPI-UCRL exploits the fact that a high-level transition corresponds to a multi-step transition at the low level. We introduce two conditions on the low-level dynamics that are sufficient to make parallel HRL learnable. When these conditions hold, we prove that HBPI-UCRL has a polynomial sample complexity in the problem parameters. In the sparse-reward, goal-directed setting, our sample complexity upper bound for HBPI-UCRL is strictly lower than that of its non-hierarchical counterpart, providing theoretical justification for the empirical success of HRL.

</details>


### [99] [Analysing User Reviews to Identify User Concerns Around Permissions in AI Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29343)
*Babar Shah,Faheem Ullah,Myles Watkinson,Muhammad Moiz Khalid,Tehmina Karamat Khan,Muhammad Junaid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器学习模型，利用AI生成的评论来从大规模真实用户评论中识别权限相关评论，无需手动标注即可实现82%的分类准确率。研究发现用户更倾向于按对应用的情感而非具体权限类型来组织其关注点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI应用日益融入移动软件，但开发者未必精通安全与隐私最佳实践，而应用评论能反映用户真实体验。由于用户评论是非结构化的，难以构建传统标注训练集，需要一种无需人工标注的方法来分类AI应用评论中的权限相关问题。

Method: 提出一种机器学习模型，利用AI生成的权限相关评论作为种子，从大量人工撰写的评论中筛选相关训练样本，从而避免繁琐的手动标注过程，实现对AI应用评论的权限类别分类。

Result: 该模型在权限评论分类上达到82%的准确率。分析还发现用户倾向于根据对应用的情感态度来组织关注点，而非根据具体的权限类型。

Conclusion: 该研究对用户、开发者和平台管理者都有启示意义：用户关注点的组织形式与情感相关，这提示开发者在设计AI应用时应更关注用户情感体验，平台也应考虑如何更好地呈现权限相关信息。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly embedded in everyday software, making its integration into mobile apps inevitable. However, AI mobile app developers are not always versed in security and privacy best practices, leaving users to monitor their own security and understand how apps use their data. App reviews capture real user experiences, helping others make informed decisions before downloading. This paper presents a machine learning model for classifying AI app reviews into permission-related categories. Because user reviews are unstructured, assembling a conventional labeled training set is difficult. To address this, AI-generated security and permission reviews are used to identify relevant training examples from a large corpus of human-written reviews, eliminating the need for manual annotation. The proposed approach classified permission reviews with an accuracy of 82%. Analysis shows that users organise their concerns by sentiment toward the requesting app rather than specific permission types, with implications for users, developers, and platform administrators.

</details>


### [100] [Versatile On-device Adaptation at the Edge by Unifying Few-shot, Zero-shot, Continual, and In-context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29353)
*Douwe den Blanken,Martin Lefebvre,Charlotte Frenkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"嵌入器中心学习"（ECL）的统一框架，能够在资源受限的边缘设备上同时支持四种在线学习场景：少样本学习（FSL）、持续学习（CL）、零样本学习（ZSL）和上下文学习（ICL），并通过硅基实现验证了其在四个真实应用中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数边缘设备依赖固定推理算法，无法在设备端进行个性化学习。即使部分设备支持学习，通常也仅限于单一场景（如FSL），超出范围就需要依赖专用设备或云端重训练，这带来了显著的能耗和延迟开销、缺乏实时性以及隐私问题。因此需要一种统一的、能够在边缘端支持多种学习场景的框架。

Method: 提出嵌入器中心学习（ECL）框架，统一四种在线学习场景：FSL（即时定制）、CL（知识积累）、ZSL（语义数据利用）和ICL（超越分类的适应）。该框架在资源受限设备上进行硅基部署，涵盖四个真实世界用例。

Result: 在FSL字符识别任务上取得SOTA性能（Omniglot：5-way 1-shot达96.8%，32-way 1-shot达83.3%）；首次提供CL关键词点检测的硬件基线（NeuroBench keyword FSCIL：200-way 5-shot达71.8%）；首次展示ZSL语义数据硬件演示（5-way口语语句分类达60.6%）和ICL硬件演示（RegBench第500个token达46.2%），功耗仅为微瓦至毫瓦级别。

Conclusion: 通过统一多种学习场景，ECL为智能且多功能的边缘设备铺平了道路，使其能够在边缘端即席适应，无需依赖云端，在极低功耗下实现多种自适应学习能力。

Abstract: With the ever-increasing pervasiveness of smart edge devices, the demand is growing for applications that can be tailored to users (e.g., custom keyword spotting) or patients (e.g., adaptive health monitoring). Yet, most edge devices rely on fixed inference algorithms and thus cannot learn on-device to personalize predictions. When they can, devices typically support only a specific learning scenario, such as few-shot learning (FSL): going beyond this requires resorting either to another specialized device or to cloud-based retraining, which implies significant energy and latency overheads, a lack of real-time capabilities, and privacy concerns. In this work, we introduce embedder-centric learning (ECL), a framework that unifies four different online learning scenarios: FSL for on-the-fly customization, continual learning (CL) for knowledge accumulation, zero-shot learning (ZSL) for leveraging semantic data, and in-context learning (ICL) for adapting beyond classification. We demonstrate in silicon that ECL can be deployed on resource-constrained devices across four real-world use cases representative of the aforementioned learning scenarios. Our approach establishes a new state-of-the-art performance for FSL character recognition (Omniglot: 96.8% for 5-way 1-shot, 83.3% for 32-way 1-shot), and the first hardware baseline for CL in keyword spotting (NeuroBench keyword FSCIL: 71.8% for 200-way 5-shot). Moreover, we present the first hardware demonstrations of ZSL with semantic data (60.6% for 5-way spoken sentence classification) and ICL (46.2% at the 500th token of RegBench) operating at micro-to-milliwatt power budgets. Therefore, by unifying multiple learning scenarios, we pave the way for smart and versatile devices that can adapt right at the edge, without reliance on the cloud.

</details>


### [101] [Cross-Resolution Semantic Learning for Graph Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29365)
*Yingxu Wang,Haoze Huang,Zhongkai Zheng,Shangsong Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CReSL方法，通过跨分辨率语义学习解决图域适应中的"语义分辨率偏移"问题，即类判别性信息在不同源/目标邻域范围内分布的跨域变化。CReSL构建多分辨率表示库、引入跨分辨率原型传输与目标嫁接机制，在多种图域适应基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图域适应方法仅对齐表示或正则化图结构，未显式建模类判别性知识在不同源邻域范围（传播分辨率）与目标范围之间的对应关系。固定同分辨率配对可能次优，并增加负迁移风险。

Method: CReSL包含三个核心组件：(1) 多分辨率表示库：基于共享GNN和可学习分辨率嵌入构建，每个源分辨率配备一个索引专家；(2) 跨分辨率原型传输：从源标签和软目标后验构建类-分辨率原型，将跨域原型差异转化为目标分辨率上的专家特定路由；(3) 跨分辨率目标嫁接：构建后验加权的目标到源原型位移，并强制执行对应加权的预测一致性以实现实例级适应。

Result: 在多种图基准和多样的域偏移设置下进行的大量实验中，CReSL在大多数设置下优于强代表性基线。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨域传播分辨率的语义对应关系可以更有效地实现图域适应，降低负迁移风险，为处理图数据分布偏移提供了一种新的思路。

Abstract: Graph Domain Adaptation (GDA) transfers predictive knowledge from labeled source graphs to unlabeled target graphs under distribution shift. Existing methods align representations or regularize graph structures, but do not explicitly model how class-discriminative knowledge learned at different source neighborhood ranges should be routed across target ranges. We call the neighborhood range encoded by a graph representation its propagation resolution and define semantic resolution shift as a cross-domain change in the propagation resolutions at which class-discriminative evidence is strongest. Such shifts can make fixed same-resolution pairing suboptimal and increase the risk of negative transfer. To address this issue, we propose Cross-Resolution Semantic Learning (CReSL), a GDA method that learns soft sourceto-target resolution correspondence from cross-domain class structure. First, CReSL constructs a multi-resolution representation bank using a shared Graph Neural Network and learnable resolution embeddings, with a resolution-indexed expert for each source resolution. Second, CReSL introduces Cross-Resolution Prototype Transport, which constructs class-resolution prototypes from source labels and soft target posteriors and converts cross-domain prototype discrepancies into expert-specific routing over target resolutions. Third, CReSL introduces Cross-Resolution Target Grafting, which constructs posterior-weighted target-to-source prototype displacements and enforces correspondence-weighted prediction consistency for instance-level adaptation under class uncertainty. Extensive experiments on graph benchmarks under diverse domain shifts show that CReSL outperforms strong representative baselines across most settings.

</details>


### [102] [OnlineCache: Learning Dynamic Caching Policies with Error Correction for Efficient Diffusion Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29398)
*Zhikang Xie,Xichen Ye,Yifan Wu,Haoshen Yu,Li chenan,Peizhu Gong,Weizhong Zhang,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OnlineCache，一个动态缓存框架，通过策略梯度训练的轻量级网络自适应决定何时缓存和如何纠正近似误差，实现跨样本和时间步的计算资源动态分配，在FLUX.1-dev上实现近3倍加速且保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于缓存的扩散模型加速策略依赖静态的、与样本无关的调度方案，忽视了生成难度因提示而异以及误差敏感度随时间步波动这两个关键事实，导致无法自适应分配计算资源。

Method: 提出OnlineCache动态缓存框架：(1)使用策略梯度训练轻量级网络实现自适应缓存决策；(2)引入可学习的纠正器来缓解缓存引起的近似误差；(3)通过双层优化框架联合优化策略与纠正器，策略面向全局生成质量，纠正器面向局部误差最小化。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev上实现近3倍加速并保持生成保真度；在DiT和CogVideoX上也获得具有竞争力的加速效果而不损害生成质量；在所有场景中均优于现有基于缓存的加速基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过动态、自适应的缓存策略和可学习误差纠正机制，OnlineCache能够自动地在样本和时间步两个维度上分配计算资源，从而在保持生成质量的同时显著提升扩散模型的推理速度。

Abstract: Diffusion models have revolutionized generative tasks but incur high latency due to iterative denoising. While cache-based strategies accelerate inference by reusing intermediate features, they largely rely on static, sample-agnostic schedules. We argue that this rigidity overlooks two facts empirically validated in this paper: (i) generation difficulty varies across prompts, requiring adaptive resource allocation--complex inputs demand more computation while simpler ones require less; (ii) error sensitivity fluctuates across timesteps, where static policies may cache high-error steps or waste computation on low-error ones. We therefore propose OnlineCache, a dynamic caching framework that jointly learns when to cache and how to correct approximation errors. We leverage policy gradient to train a lightweight network for adaptive speed-quality trade-offs, and incorporate a learnable corrector to mitigate caching-induced errors. Both modules are jointly optimized under a bilevel optimization framework, with the policy targeting global generation quality and the corrector minimizing local errors. Our method automatically allocates computational resources across both samples and timesteps, improving overall generation quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate clear superiority. On FLUX.1-dev model, OnlineCache achieves nearly 3 speedup while preserving generation fidelity. On DiT and CogVideoX, it similarly delivers competitive acceleration without compromising quality; across all scenarios, it consistently outperforms existing cache-based acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [103] [ALIVE: Warnings Before Exclusion in Budgeted Multi-Source Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29400)
*Xiyang Zhang,Hongzhi Wang,Yuanhe Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ALIVE（基于证据的行动分层干预）框架，研究在有限总体审计与学习共享预算的条件下，哪些证据应授权不同持久性的路由决策修订与源排除操作。系统采用随机无放回前缀缓存证据、启发式警告驱动非锁定的下限约束路由，仅两次同时新鲜证书分离可锁定排除请求。在理想均匀审计排列条件下，任何保持该接口的可预测控制器都能继承对未通过预固定绝对或相对严格多数分歧谓词来源的任意族级δon界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路由决策可在下一次交易时修订，但锁定的源排除会跨决策持续存在。作者质疑在有限总体审计与学习共享预算的约束下，应依据何种证据授权这些持久性不同的操作，以平衡统计严格性与实用性。

Method: ALIVE是一种可审计的控制层：采用随机无放回前缀提供缓存证据；启发式警告驱动非锁定的下限约束路由；仅两次同时新鲜证书分离可锁定排除请求（受容量可行性激活约束）。通过条件化固定支持与标签，在理想均匀审计排列下分析任意保持接口的可预测控制器。

Result: 在发布的已知规模全严格多数PPR引擎中，中位证据数从e40的304降至96个身份，从e60的171降至62个，e80两者均用48。匹配的CIFAR控制器中，持久行动层在全部十个配对的种子簇中较仅路由增加了+0.1935准确率-AUBC百分点。全系统对CBR的+0.1954点对比为正但未达到预先声明的多重性校正标准。在固定自然面板上，探索性PPR使用中位闭合前缀95而非105，但暴露了88.0%的面板。

Conclusion: 研究绘制了约束-效能-成本-效用边界：行动契约控制定义好的持久决策，而净价值取决于证据余量、审计成本与预算制度。系统在非严格多数场景下表现良好，但在最严格的多重性校正标准下未达显著性门槛。

Abstract: A routing decision can be revised at the next transaction, but a latched source exclusion persists across later decisions. We ask what evidence should authorize these unequal-persistence actions when finite-population auditing and learning share a budget. ALIVE (Action-Layered Intervention via Evidence) is an auditable control layer: one randomized without-replacement prefix supplies cached evidence, heuristic warnings drive non-latching floor-bounded routing, and only two fresh simultaneous certificate separations may latch an exclusion request subject to capacity-feasible activation. Conditional on fixed support and labels under an ideal uniform audit permutation, any predictable controller preserving this interface inherits an anytime familywise bound of δon acting against a source that fails the pre-fixed absolute or relative strict-majority-disagreement predicate. With a published known-size, all-strict-majority PPR engine, median evidence count fell from 304 to 96 identities in e40 and from 171 to 62 in e60, while both engines used 48 in e80. In the matched CIFAR controller, the persistent-action layer added +0.1935 accuracy-AUBC percentage points over routing-only in all ten paired seed clusters. The +0.1954-point full-system contrast against CBR was also positive but did not meet the predeclared multiplicity-adjusted criterion (conditional Holm-adjusted sign-flip reference value =.097656). On a fixed natural panel, exploratory PPR used a median closure prefix of 95 rather than 105 for exploratory Serfling/FPC, but still exposed 88.0% of the panel and had no downstream task. Together these results map a restraint--power--cost--utility boundary: the action contract controls a defined persistent decision, while net value depends on evidence margin, audit cost, and budget regime.

</details>


### [104] [Explore Beyond the Boundary Using Entropic Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29419)
*Bumgeun Park,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ENTINEX方法，通过利用熵信息识别状态分布边界并赋予内在奖励，激励智能体探索分布边界之外的区域，从而有效解决强化学习中稀疏和延迟奖励带来的探索难题。实验表明该方法在多种稀疏和延迟奖励环境中优于现有探索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中稀疏和延迟奖励环境下，有限的反馈信号使学习过程难以引导，需要在状态空间中进行广泛探索才能发现有价值的奖励信号，这是当前面临的重要挑战。

Method: 提出ENTINEX（Entropic Information for Exploration）方法，通过熵信息识别状态分布的边界区域，并为这些边界区域分配内在奖励，从而激励智能体探索超出当前状态分布范围的新区域。

Result: 大量实验表明ENTINEX在稀疏和延迟奖励环境中持续提升探索性能，并且优于现有的探索方法。

Conclusion: ENTINEX通过熵信息驱动的边界探索机制，在稀疏和延迟奖励场景下有效提升了强化学习智能体的探索效率，验证了该方法相对于现有方法的优越性。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning, exploration with sparse and delayed rewards presents a significant challenge due to the limited feedback available for guiding the learning process. Addressing this issue requires extensive exploration in the state space to discover valuable reward signals. In this paper, we propose Entropic Information for Exploration (ENTINEX), a novel method that enhances exploration by incentivizing agents to explore beyond the boundaries of the state distribution. ENTINEX achieves this by assigning intrinsic rewards to these boundaries, leveraging entropic information to identify them effectively. Through extensive experimentation, we demonstrate that ENTINEX consistently improves exploration performance in environments characterized by sparse and delayed rewards. Our experimental results show that ENTINEX outperforms existing exploration methods, highlighting its effectiveness in both sparse and delayed reward scenarios.

</details>


### [105] [End-to-End Fairness Optimization with Fair Decision-Focused Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29441)
*Yu Wang,Violet,Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了端到端公平性优化（E2EFO）框架，将公平性从预测阶段贯穿到决策阶段，并提出了公平决策焦点学习（FDFL）训练范式，通过多任务学习联合优化预测准确率、预测公平性和决策遗憾，同时推导了决策雅可比矩阵的闭式解，并建立了有限样本泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多系统依赖预测模型进行决策，公平性问题同时存在于预测和决策两个阶段，但现有方法往往只关注其中一个阶段，缺乏将公平性端到端整合到整个预测-决策流水线中的统一框架。

Method: 提出E2EFO统一框架，整合预测与决策阶段的公平性；提出FDFL训练范式，通过多任务学习联合优化预测准确率、预测公平性和决策遗憾；对可处理的公平分配问题推导决策雅可比矩阵的精确闭式解，一般情形下使用可微优化层；并建立标量化FDFL目标的有限样本泛化界。

Result: 在医疗保健单资源分配和合成多资源分配上的数值实验表明，在预测驱动的决策中联合考虑预测公平性和决策公平性具有实际价值。

Conclusion: FDFL能够有效联合优化预测准确率、预测公平性和决策公平性，为预测驱动型决策中的端到端公平性优化提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Many real-world systems rely on predictive models to inform decisions, and fairness concerns arise in both the prediction and decision stages. We introduce end-to-end fairness optimization (E2EFO) as a unifying framework that integrates fairness across the prediction-to-decision pipeline. We focus on resource allocation with group-based fairness: the prediction task estimates allocation impacts while limiting accuracy disparity across groups, and the decision task distributes those impacts equitably by optimizing a group-based alpha-fairness measure. Within this framework, we propose fair decision-focused learning (FDFL), a training paradigm that jointly accounts for prediction accuracy, prediction fairness, and decision regret -- the loss in decision fairness due to imperfect predictions. FDFL trains the predictor by gradient descent, combining the objective gradients through multi-task learning techniques. The core computational challenge is the decision Jacobian with respect to the predictor parameters: we derive exact closed-form formulas for a tractable class of fair allocation and apply a differentiable optimization layer in the general case. We further establish a finite-sample generalization bound for the scalarized FDFL objective. Numerical experiments on a healthcare-based single resource allocation and a synthetic multiple resource allocation illustrate the value of jointly accounting for prediction fairness and decision fairness in prediction-informed decision-making.

</details>


### [106] [TFGformer: Multivariate Time Series Forecasting via Time-Frequency Graph Learning and Covariate Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29459)
*Yu Sun,Yuan Chang,Xiaohou Shi,Yan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CrossRAG框架，将检索增强生成(RAG)应用于多变量时间序列预测，通过形状感知记忆、未来一致性对比学习和跨注意力时间融合，有效解决异质IoT传感器数据的长期预测问题，在七个公开基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列基础模型依赖静态参数知识，推理时无法动态访问外部历史模式。虽然RAG有望解决这一问题，但直接应用于时间序列预测面临两大挑战：异质来源间的幅度差异，以及历史相似性与未来一致性之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出CrossRAG框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Shape-Aware Memory (SAM)结合RevIN归一化实现幅度鲁棒的形状级检索；(2) Future-Consistent Contrastive (FCC)学习用于区分具有相似历史但未来发散的信息性参考与困难负样本；(3) Cross-Attention Temporal Fusion (CATF)在表示层面将检索到的历史-未来参考对融合进基础模型的表征中。

Result: 在七个公开基准数据集上的实验表明，CrossRAG一致性地优于仅参数化的基线模型和现有的检索增强预测方法。

Conclusion: 通过将检索增强机制与时间序列预测深度结合，CrossRAG有效解决了异质IoT多变量时间序列的长期预测难题，验证了动态外部知识访问对提升预测性能的价值。

Abstract: Large-scale multivariate time series from heterogeneous IoT sensors demand accurate long-term forecasting for resource scheduling and predictive maintenance. While recent time series foundation models exhibit strong generalization, they rely on static parametric knowledge and lack dynamic access to external historical patterns during inference. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) offers a potential remedy, yet its application to time series forecasting is challenged by magnitude variations across heterogeneous sources and the mismatch between historical similarity and future consistency. We propose CrossRAG, a retrieval-augmented forecasting framework that integrates Shape-Aware Memory (SAM) with RevIN normalization for magnitude-robust shape-level retrieval, Future-Consistent Contrastive (FCC) learning to distinguish informative references from hard negatives with similar history but divergent futures, and Cross-Attention Temporal Fusion (CATF) to fuse retrieved historical--future reference pairs into the backbone's representations at the representation level. Experiments on seven public benchmarks show that CrossRAG consistently outperforms both parametric-only baselines and existing retrieval-augmented forecasting methods.

</details>


### [107] [Parameter-Free Heavy-Tailed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29460)
*Gianmarco Genalti,Alberto Maria Metelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了重尾赌博机中尾部参数（矩上界u和尾部指数ε）未知时的自适应遗憾最小化问题，提出了一个无需知道这些参数的分阶段探索算法，在已知ε但未知u时达到最优自适应边界，且该算法可通过校准到ε=1在完全未知ε时实现次线性遗憾，同时证明了对所有ε∈(0,1]统一实现次线性遗憾是不可能的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重尾赌博机算法大多要求预先知道尾部参数ε和u，但这些参数控制着罕见事件的频率和幅度，恰恰是最难从有限观测中可靠推断的量。这一假设在实践中过于严格，因此需要解决参数未知时的自适应问题。

Method: 作者首先证明了对u不可知的算法必须在分布依赖和分布无关遗憾保证之间满足一个尖锐的权衡；然后提出一种分阶段探索（scheduled-exploration）算法，不需要u的先验知识即可匹配自适应边界（至多相差对数因子）；最后通过将探索计划校准到ε=1的端点，使同一算法在ε未知时也能工作。

Result: 该算法在已知ε但未知u时达到自适应边界；在ε和u都未知时，对任意固定的ε>0实现次线性遗憾；同时证明了不存在任何算法能对所有ε∈(0,1]统一保证次线性遗憾。这些结果完整地解决了COLT 2025的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文给出了重尾赌博机中适应未知尾部参数的统计代价的精确刻画，在没有额外分布假设的情况下完整解决了COLT开放问题，为实践中更具挑战性的重尾序列决策问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed distributions arise naturally in sequential decision-making problems such as financial investment, online advertising, and network management, where rare but extreme outcomes can dominate performance. Heavy-tailed bandits model online decision-making in these settings by assuming only that rewards $X$ satisfy $\mathbb{E}[|X|^{1+ε}]\leq u$, for some tail exponent $ε\in(0,1]$ and moment bound $u<+\infty$. However, most existing regret minimization algorithms require these parameters to be known. This assumption is particularly restrictive in practice: $ε$ and $u$ govern the frequency and magnitude of rare events and are therefore precisely the quantities that are hardest to infer reliably from limited observations.
  Motivated by an open problem posed by Genalti and Metelli at COLT 2025, we resolve the assumption-free adaptation problem for heavy-tailed bandits and characterize the price in the regret of not knowing the tail parameters. We first study adaptation to the moment bound $u$ for a fixed tail exponent $ε$. We prove that every algorithm unaware of $u$, or of any upper bound on it, must obey a sharp trade-off between its distribution-dependent and distribution-free regret guarantees. We then introduce a scheduled-exploration algorithm that requires no knowledge of $u$ and matches the resulting adaptation frontier up to logarithmic factors. Finally, we show that the same algorithm can be instanced without knowing $ε$ by calibrating its exploration schedule to the endpoint $ε=1$. It achieves sublinear regret for every fixed $ε>0$, while no algorithm can guarantee sublinear regret uniformly over all $ε\in(0,1]$. Altogether, our results resolve the COLT open problem without additional distributional assumptions and provide a sharp characterization of the statistical cost of adapting to unknown heavy tails.

</details>


### [108] [MolGVR: A Chemistry-Grounded Framework for Text-to-Molecule Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29479)
*Qian Tan,Xuanyu Zhu,Lei Jiang,Zhonghang Yuan,Chen Zhang,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MolGVR框架，通过生成器-验证器-优化器三层架构改善文本到分子的生成任务。生成器推断结构证据并生成候选分子，验证器将描述转化为化学约束并进行检查，优化器对未通过验证的候选进行修正。在ChEBI-20和PCDes数据集上的实验表明该方法提升了精确匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的文本到分子生成被建模为一次性的序列生成问题，但分子描述中常包含结构约束信息，违反这些约束会改变分子的化学身份。现有方法缺乏化学验证和错误修正机制，这是一个未被充分探索的关键问题。

Method: MolGVR框架包含三个核心组件：1) Generator：推断结构证据并生成候选分子；2) Verifier：将描述转化为化学约束并对候选分子进行验证；3) Refiner：对未通过验证的候选分子进行修订。三者协同形成可执行验证与反馈引导的闭环。

Result: 在ChEBI-20和PCDes数据集上的实验表明，MolGVR相比现有方法显著提升了精确匹配（exact-match）性能。

Conclusion: 将生成与可执行的化学验证及反馈引导的优化相结合，是提升文本到分子生成任务效果的有效途径。

Abstract: Text-to-molecule generation is typically formulated as a one-shot sequence generation problem, where a model directly maps target descriptions to molecular representations. However, molecular descriptions often contain informative structural constraints, and violating such constraints can change the molecular identity. This makes chemical verification and error correction important but underexplored. To fill this gap, we propose MolGVR, a chemistry-grounded Generator--Verifier--Refiner framework. The Generator infers structural evidence and generates candidate molecules. The Verifier addresses the lack of chemical validation by converting descriptions into chemical constraints and checking candidates against them. The Refiner addresses generation failures by revising candidates rejected by the Verifier. Experiments on ChEBI-20 and PCDes show that MolGVR improves exact-match performance. These results suggest that coupling generation with executable verification and feedback-guided refinement is an effective way to improve text-to-molecule generation.

</details>


### [109] [DreamQAS: Learning a Decision-Useful World Model for VQE-Efficient Quantum Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29491)
*Jiayang Niu,Yan Wang,Jie Li,Ke Deng,Azadeh Alavi,Muhammad Usman,Yongli Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DreamQAS，一种基于模型的强化学习框架用于量子架构搜索（QAS），通过精确建模电路动力学并仅学习VQE后的昂贵反馈，配合循环随机先验集成和想象策略学习，在多个分子任务上以更少的真实VQE调用达到更低的能量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的量子架构搜索（RL-QAS）在每次扩展电路后都反复优化变分量子本征求解器（VQE），然而电路构建和动作合法性是确定且已知的，这种反复优化造成了不必要的计算浪费。作者旨在引入模型化RL框架，避免对昂贵VQE反馈的重复计算。

Method: DreamQAS采用基于模型的强化学习框架：(1) 精确保留电路动力学，仅学习VQE后的昂贵反馈；(2) 使用循环随机先验集成（recurrent randomized-prior ensemble）预测相对经验能量前沿的无神谕分数；(3) 支持在显式合法电路上进行多步想象策略学习；(4) 结合排序激活、不确定性感知悲观主义和截断、选择性真实VQE验证，形成可靠性控制的循环学习机制。

Result: 在15,000回合预算和冻结评估条件下，DreamQAS在五个分子任务中的四个取得最低平均冻结策略能量误差，一个任务取得第二低。在精细误差目标下，四个任务减少1.6到2.0倍的真实VQE调用，BeH2-8q减少10.6倍。反事实动作排序效用平均提升0.346（95%置信区间[0.185, 0.507]），集成分歧在所有三个探测任务上优于随机拒绝的风险覆盖。

Conclusion: DreamQAS证明了量子架构搜索的世界模型设计价值在于提供决策有用的反馈而非精确的能量预测，通过模型化学习大幅减少昂贵的真实VQE调用，同时提升或持平性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning-based quantum architecture search (RL-QAS) repeatedly optimizes a variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) after extending a circuit, although circuit construction and action legality are deterministic and known. We introduce DreamQAS, a model-based RL framework that preserves these exact circuit dynamics and learns only the expensive post-VQE feedback. A recurrent randomized-prior ensemble predicts an oracle-free score relative to an empirical energy frontier and supports multi-step imagined policy learning over explicit legal circuits. Ranking-based activation, uncertainty-aware pessimism and truncation, and selective real-VQE verification form a reliability-controlled learning loop. Under a common 15,000-episode budget and frozen evaluation for the RL methods, DreamQAS has the lowest mean frozen-policy energy error on four of five molecular tasks and the second-lowest on one. At fine-error targets reached by all seeds of both methods, it uses 1.6x to 2.0x fewer real VQE calls on four tasks and 10.6x fewer on BeH2-8q. Counterfactual action-ranking utility increases across all five tasks, with a mean increase of 0.346 and a 95 percent confidence interval of [0.185, 0.507], while direct greedy and beam use of the same model does not recover the gains of imagined policy learning. Ensemble disagreement also improves risk-coverage over random rejection on all three probed tasks. These results establish a world-model design for QAS whose value lies in decision-useful feedback rather than exact energy prediction.

</details>


### [110] [Adaptive FastOPD: Progress-Aware Rollout Horizon Expansion for Efficient On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29494)
*Qian Tan,Huaifei Liang,Xuanyu Zhu,Lei Jiang,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive FastOPD，一种自适应的进度感知策略，用于加速在线策略蒸馏（OPD），通过仅在边界区域学习停滞且当前horizon充分利用时扩展rollout长度，在不牺牲性能的情况下大幅减少训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD虽能提供密集的教师监督，但在线rollout计算开销大，尤其是少数长响应会延迟批次完成。现有加速方法使用固定预算或绝对阈值控制rollout长度，无法反映不同模型和训练阶段的学习进度差异。

Method: 提出进度感知的自适应扩展机制：通过四个教师-学生信号（相对于进入每个horizon时的基线值）判断当前边界区域的学习是否停滞；同时检查当前horizon是否被充分利用，以防止少数长响应触发不必要的rollout成本增加。

Result: 在两组教师-学生配对实验中，Adaptive FastOPD获得了最高的平均性能，同时相对OPD 15K将训练时间减少了49.1%-71.2%，并在多种超参数设置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于相对信号而非绝对阈值的自适应rollout扩展策略，能够根据阶段特定的学习进度动态调整计算成本，在保持高性能的同时显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher supervision along student-generated trajectories, but its online rollout process incurs substantial computational cost, particularly when a few long responses delay batch completion. Existing acceleration methods typically control rollout length using fixed budgets or absolute teacher--student agreement thresholds, which may not reflect learning progress across different models and training stages. We propose Adaptive FastOPD, a progress-aware strategy that expands the rollout horizon only when learning near the current boundary region has plateaued and the current horizon is sufficiently utilized. The former is determined from four teacher--student signals measured relative to their values upon entering each horizon, making expansion responsive to stage-specific progress rather than a predefined step interval or an absolute threshold on the raw agreement signals, while the latter prevents a small number of long responses from triggering increases in rollout cost. Across two teacher--student pairs, Adaptive FastOPD achieves the highest average performance while reducing training time by 49.1--71.2\% relative to OPD 15K, and remains robust across a range of hyperparameter settings.

</details>


### [111] [Transcript-Managed Transformers: Monotone Multi-Agent Collapse and Universality with Two Pop-Enabled Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29496)
*Sergey Salishev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究固定有限精度因果Transformer的转录管理（transcript management）机制。转录被划分为带边界块的通道，每次转换操作咨询固定可见后缀并可追加一个块。定义了Transcript-Managed Transducer（TMT）模型，包含单控制器、k个通道、以及stay/push/pop三类操作。无pop的受限模型（RTMT）等价于确定性有限状态 transduction；而引入pop后，单通道等价于DCFL，双通道及以上达到RE完全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限精度因果Transformer在固定架构（模型、权重、token协议不变）下的计算能力边界，特别是通过转录（transcript）管理机制——即上下文窗口的可视性和块级操作——来刻画其表达能力，并建立与经典自动机层级之间的联系。

Method: 提出TMTk模型：一个有限控制器、k个通道、每轮可从stay/push/pop中选择动作，受调用者驱动状态图约束。固定可见窗口编码为有限符号；通过PopContext(c)操作删除通道c上最新块。将多通道模型编译到Hopcroft-Ullman表示，借用经典层级结果；并对比聚合体（population）协议下的带路由/复制的追加操作。

Result: (1) 对每个固定k，无pop的RTMTk恰好实现确定性有限状态 transduction，聚合体协议下同样成立；(2) 引入pop后，k=1时实现DCFL，k≥2时实现RE（完全性）；(3) 单代理多通道与多代理单通道等价，故两个pop使能转录即可达通用性；(4) 给出了模拟代价、固定块大小和可见半径的不变性结论；(5) 放宽固定边界（如精确上下文增长、隐藏块访问、可写存储、无界Spawn）会增加状态复杂度。

Conclusion: 转录管理机制中pop操作的引入是计算能力跃迁的关键：从有限状态到DCFL再到RE。固定精度的限制下，双通道（或两个单通道代理）即足以实现通用计算，这为理解有限精度Transformer的表达能力提供了精确的层级刻画。

Abstract: We study transcript management for fixed, finite-precision causal Transformers. A transcript is partitioned into channels of bounded blocks. Each transition consults a fixed visible suffix and may append one block, leaving the model, weights, and token protocol unchanged. The operation $P_c:=\PopContext(c)$ deletes the newest block on channel $c$ and exposes its predecessor.
  We model the layer by the Transcript-Managed Transducer $\TMTn{k}$: one finite controller, $k$ channels, and per-round actions from stay, push, and pop under a caller-driven status map. Fixed visible windows encode as finite symbols. The pop-free Restricted Transcript-Managed Transducer $\RTMTn{k}$ is the standard append-only layer and, for every fixed $k$, realizes exactly the deterministic finite-state transductions. The same holds for every fixed finite agent population under a monotone protocol that appends, routes, and copies visible blocks.
  Admitting $\{P_c\}_{c=1}^k$ restores pop. Newest-first, a pop-enabled channel is a stack; compiling to the Hopcroft--Ullman presentation transfers the classical hierarchy: $\DCFL$ for $k=1$ and $\RE$ for every $k\ge2$. Orchestrated one-channel agents match one controller with $k$ channels, so two pop-enabled transcripts---in one agent or two---suffice for universality. Simulation costs and invariance to fixed block size and visible radius are stated. The bounds fix precision, alphabets, blocks, visibility, controller state, and population; growing exact context, hidden-block access, writable stores, and unbounded \textbf{Spawn} add further state.

</details>


### [112] [The Grokked Illusion: True Equilibrium Mitigates Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29503)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Lai Shun Chan,Yue Shang,Entao Yang,Ge Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过模算术中的grokking实验发现，高熵模型相比传统AdamW训练的模型具有更强的鲁棒性（抗灾难性遗忘），即使两者在泛化表现上完全相同。高熵模型的注意力层和MLP层具有更高的有效秩，丰富的特征表示可作为抵御灾难性遗忘的缓冲区。作者将这种"完美泛化但脆弱"的现象称为"grokked illusion"（迷恋错觉）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上评估神经网络仅关注训练和测试性能，但这些指标无法揭示学习表示的鲁棒性。已有研究表明高熵解（大参数空间体积）具有更好的泛化性，但作者质疑这种优势是否超越泛化范畴，即高熵是否同样带来对干扰的鲁棒性（如防止灾难性遗忘）。

Method: 以模算术中的grokking作为受控实验设置，对比两类性能饱和且完全相同的模型：(1) AdamW训练的transformer，(2) 从Wang-Landau分子动力学采样的高熵模型。通过设计噪声注入实验（强制模型学习随机标签的新数据）来评估两者在灾难性遗忘上的差异，并结合神经网络权重的奇异值分解（SVD）分析注意力层和MLP层的有效秩。

Result: AdamW训练模型遭受严重的灾难性遗忘（原始任务测试准确率从100%降至75%以下），而高熵模型保持约95%的测试准确率。SVD分析发现高熵模型在注意力层和MLP层中具有显著更高的有效秩，无论是噪声注入前还是后。

Conclusion: 完美泛化并不等同于同等鲁棒性。高熵神经网络的丰富特征表示能够作为抵御灾难性遗忘的缓冲区，这一发现为理解"什么使训练模型对干扰具有鲁棒性"提供了新视角，并揭示了传统训练方法中隐藏的脆弱性（grokked illusion）。

Abstract: While neural networks are typically evaluated by their training and test performance, these metrics do not reveal how robust a learned representation is. Recent studies have shown that solutions occupying larger volumes in parameter space, as quantified by Boltzmann entropy, often exhibit superior generalizability compared to those reached by conventional optimization, a phenomenon known as the high entropy advantage. Here we ask whether this advantage persists beyond generalization. Specifically, we investigate models' robustness, the ability to retain the learned knowledge when the model is subsequently trained to acquire new information. Using grokking in modular arithmetic as a controlled setting, we design a noise injection experiment to evaluate the robustness difference between AdamW-trained transformers and high-entropy model sampled from Wang-Landau Molecular Dynamics with identical saturated performance. By forcing both models to fully remember new data with random labels, we find that AdamW-trained models suffer from catastrophic forgetting, with original task test accuracy dropping from 100% to below 75%, whereas the high-entropy models maintain approximately 95% test accuracy. We term this hidden fragility behind apparent generalization the "grokked illusion." Through singular value decomposition of the neural network weights, we discover that high-entropy neural networks possess significantly higher effective rank in attention and MLP layers both before and after noise injection, indicating richer feature representations can serve as a buffer against catastrophic forgetting. Our findings demonstrate that perfect generalization does not imply equal robustness, offering a new perspective on what makes a trained model robust to interference.

</details>


### [113] [TerraNova: A Foundation Model for the Anthropocene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29527)
*Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TerraNova，一个在地球系统和人类社会数据的原生几何形态上训练的基础模型，通过跨模态Transformer融合512个网格化地球系统字段和512个国家指标，实现物理地球与社会系统的耦合建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类世的核心问题是将物理地球与人类社会建模为一个耦合系统，但物理地球以连续场（忽略政治边界）测量，而社会数据以行政单元报告，两者几何形态不匹配。现有地球系统基础模型仅支持前者，耦合后者需要有损的边界平均。

Method: TerraNova在1,024个物理和社会记录上训练：512个网格化地球系统字段和512个国家指标。使用专用编码器表示位置、国家、时间和任务，跨模态Transformer融合为共享时空状态，超网络生成按查询定制的解码器，其证据头返回预测分布。两个对比目标耦合表示：人口加权对齐（国家与其领土内坐标之间）和预训练地理空间嵌入（携带图像衍生语义）对齐。

Result: 解码器输出在专用地理空间编码器上具有竞争力，同时覆盖它们未表示的轴（时间、海洋和不确定性），并支持国家级能力。冻结骨干网络可以从稀疏观测重建密集场，并在消费级硬件上数分钟内适应未见变量。

Conclusion: TerraNova证明了可以通过保持数据原生几何形态并设计跨模态融合与对比学习目标，实现物理地球与社会系统的统一基础模型表示，克服了边界平均带来的信息损失。

Abstract: A defining problem of the Anthropocene is to model the physical Earth and human societies as one coupled system, yet no learned representation spans their observational breadth. We argue the obstacle is geometric: the physical Earth is measured as continuous fields that ignore political borders, whereas societies are reported for administrative units. Earth-system foundation models serve the first geometry; coupling it to the second has required lossy averaging over borders. We introduce TerraNova, a foundation model trained on 1,024 physical and societal records in their native geometries: 512 gridded Earth-system fields and 512 national indicators. Dedicated encoders represent location, country, time and task, cross-modal transformers fuse them into a shared spatiotemporal state, and a hypernetwork generates a per-query decoder whose evidential head returns a predictive distribution. Two contrastive objectives couple the representation: a population-weighted alignment between each country and coordinates in its territory, and one to pretrained geospatial embeddings carrying image-derived semantics. Read out through that decoder, the representation is competitive with purpose-built geospatial encoders while spanning axes they do not represent (time, oceans and uncertainty) and supporting country-level capabilities. The frozen backbone reconstructs dense fields from sparse observations and adapts to unseen variables in minutes on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [114] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Explainable Early Diagnosis of Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29530)
*Ranveer Singh,Pranuthi Tenali,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Vaishali Phatak,Karla Lynch,Daniel Murman,Matthew Rizzo,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化流程，直接从言语流畅性测试的音频录音中提取阿尔茨海默病（AD）进展的定性知识，利用预训练基础模型处理原始音频并构建贝叶斯网络，以推理AD进展标记及其定性关系。系统成功恢复了已知的临床知识，并发现了语言标记之间的新关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的AD标记识别需要人工转录和专家分析，耗时费力且难以规模化。作者希望自动化这一过程，直接从音频中提取AD进展相关的临床知识。

Method: 采用自动化流程：使用预训练基础模型处理言语流畅性测试的原始音频，提取临床相关的变量，构建贝叶斯网络（BN），并利用该网络推理AD进展标记及其定性关系。

Result: 该系统成功恢复了已知的AD临床知识，并发现了语言标记之间新的关联关系。

Conclusion: 自动化音频分析结合贝叶斯网络能够有效识别AD进展标记，不仅验证了已有临床知识，还能发现新关系，为AD研究提供了可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Identifying reliable Alzheimer's disease (AD) markers typically requires manual, labor-intensive transcription and expert analysis, limiting its scale. We introduce an automated pipeline that extracts qualitative knowledge about potential AD progression indicators directly from audio recordings of verbal fluency tests. Our method uses pretrained foundation models to process raw audio and extract clinically relevant variables to construct a Bayesian Network (BN); this BN is used to reason about the AD progression markers and infer their qualitative relationships. Our system successfully recovers known clinical knowledge and identifies novel relationships between linguistic markers.

</details>


### [115] [Pyramidal Width Can Increase Under Vertex Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29555)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi在2015年提出的猜想给出精确反例：在R^3中构造了六个整数点，使得当添加一个新顶点（原五个顶点保持为顶点）时，多面体的金字塔宽度不降反升，增加因子约为1.41。证明利用金字塔宽度与面距离的等价性，通过整数支撑超平面验证面格并采用有限有理计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi于2015年猜想：在保持所有旧点仍为顶点的条件下，向多面体添加顶点不会使金字塔宽度增加。该猜想若成立将对条件梯度方法（Frank-Wolfe类算法）的收敛性分析有重要意义，因此需要验证或反驳这一猜想。

Method: 作者在三维空间中给出六个整数点的精确反例。利用金字塔宽度与面距离的等价性，通过整数支撑超平面验证顶点插入前后多面体的面格（face lattices），并对每个面距离进行有限有理计算，从而精确比较加入新顶点前后的金字塔宽度。

Result: 构造出精确反例：五顶点多面体P添加一个顶点得到Q后，P的所有五个顶点仍为Q的顶点，但P的金字塔宽度平方为48/353，Q的为36/133，金字塔宽度增加因子√(1059/532)≈1.4109，推翻了该猜想。

Conclusion: Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi的猜想不成立，顶点插入可以使金字塔宽度增加。论文附带一个无依赖的精确验证器以确保证明无误。

Abstract: Lacoste-Julien and Jaggi conjectured in 2015 that the pyramidal width of a polytope cannot increase when a vertex is added, provided that every old point remains a vertex. We give an exact counterexample with six integer points in $\R^3$. For \[ P=\conv\{v_0,\ldots,v_4\},\qquad Q=\conv\{v_0,\ldots,v_5\}, \] where \[ \begin{aligned} v_0&=(-1,-3,-1), & v_1&=(3,2,-2), & v_2&=(0,2,1),\\ v_3&=(-1,-3,3), & v_4&=(-2,0,1), & v_5&=(-1,0,-2), \end{aligned} \] all five vertices of $P$ remain vertices of $Q$, but \[ \PWidth(P)^2=\frac{48}{353} \quad\text{and}\quad \PWidth(Q)^2=\frac{36}{133}. \] Thus vertex insertion increases pyramidal width by the factor $\sqrt{1059/532}\approx 1.410886779$. The proof uses the equivalence between pyramidal width and facial distance, certifies both face lattices by integer supporting hyperplanes, and evaluates every facial distance by a finite rational calculation. A dependency-free exact verifier accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [116] [MOT-SR: Multi-Objective Tool-Augmented Scientific Equation Discovery with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29561)
*Boxiao Wang,Runxiang Wang,Kai Li,Chongming Li,Zhiwei Chen,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多目标工具增强符号回归框架（MOT-SR），通过集成外部分析工具提取结构先验，并联合优化精度、复杂度和泛化性能，在40个标准任务及极端质量比旋进（EMRI）轨道建模中均取得最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based符号回归方法存在两个局限：缺乏数据依赖分析机制导致方程发现效率低；单一目标（仅拟合误差）评价忽视结构复杂度和泛化性，易陷入局部最优。

Method: MOT-SR包含两个协作的LLM模块：元策略生成器（Meta Strategy Generator）负责选择工具并基于Pareto最优方程综合结构优化策略；方程生成器（Equation Generator）据此生成新候选方程。系统采用闭环迭代方式，通过多目标评价模块维护动态Pareto前沿，同时优化精度、复杂度和泛化性。

Result: 在40个标准任务中，MOT-SR在精度、泛化性和效率上均优于现有符号回归方法；在EMRI轨道建模任务中，发现的解释性修正项在保留配置上取得最低的轨迹级积分误差。

Conclusion: MOT-SR展示了多目标优化与工具增强相结合的潜力，能够支持长期科学动力学任务的可靠建模。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) aims to discover analytical equations from observational data and plays a central role in scientific modeling. While recent Large Language Model (LLM) based approaches show promise, they face two limitations. First, they lack data analysis mechanisms for uncovering variable dependencies, which reduces the efficiency of equation discovery. Second, most methods rely on single-objective evaluation focused solely on fitting error. This neglect of structural complexity and generalization often causes models to converge prematurely to local optima, limiting their ability to explore the broader equation space. We propose Multi-Objective Tool-augmented Symbolic Regression (MOT-SR), a unified framework that integrates external analytical tools to extract structural priors and guide equation generation, while jointly optimizing for accuracy, complexity, and generalization via a multi-objective evaluation module that maintains a dynamic Pareto front. MOT-SR employs two collaborative LLM modules: a Meta Strategy Generator, which selects tools and synthesizes structural optimization strategies based on Pareto-optimal equations, and an Equation Generator, which produces new candidate equations accordingly. The system operates in a closed-loop manner, continuously refining both strategies and equation structures. Across 40 standard tasks, MOT-SR outperforms existing SR methods in accuracy, generalization, and efficiency. We further validate MOT-SR on extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) orbital modeling, an important problem in space-based gravitational-wave astronomy where small local errors can accumulate substantially over long-term evolution. The discovered interpretable correction achieves the lowest trajectory-level integration error on held-out configurations. These results demonstrate the potential of MOT-SR to enable reliable modeling of long-horizon scientific dynamics.

</details>


### [117] [Convergence and Regret of the Policy Gradient for Multi-Armed Bandits in Diffusion Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29593)
*Yanwei Jia,Du Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散环境（由随机微分方程描述）下多臂老虎机问题的策略梯度更新，证明在任意常数学习率下策略几乎必然收敛到最优臂，并给出了O(log T)阶的非渐近遗憾上界。通过构造新的Lyapunov函数，改进了已有分析，该方法同样适用于离散时间策略梯度算法的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续时间强化学习框架下，研究扩散环境中的多臂老虎机策略梯度算法的收敛性与遗憾界，并改进已有的理论分析。

Method: 采用logit参数化的随机策略，在连续时间SDE框架下分析策略梯度更新。通过构造一个新颖的Lyapunov函数来分析收敛性和遗憾上界，该Lyapunov函数同时适用于离散时间策略梯度算法的分析。

Result: 证明了策略梯度更新在任意常数学习率下几乎必然收敛到最优臂；当常数学习率低于一个时间不变的阈值时，得到了O(log T)阶的非渐近遗憾上界，优于或改进了已有工作（Lattimore 2026a）的分析结果。

Conclusion: 通过构造新颖的Lyapunov函数，展示了利用SDE工具分析策略梯度算法的透明性，并为连续时间和离散时间策略梯度算法提供了统一的理论分析框架。

Abstract: This paper studies the policy gradient update for a multi-arm bandit problem in diffusion environment that is described by a stochastic differential equation (SDE) under the continuous-time reinforcement learning framework by Wang et al. (2020), Jia and Zhou (2022b). With the logit parameterization for the stochastic policy, we show that it converges almost surely to the optimal arm under an arbitrary constant learning rate. Furthermore, we derive the non-asymptotic regret upper bound when the constant learning rate is below a time-invariant threshold; and the regret bound has order $O(\log T)$. We improve the analysis in Lattimore (2026a) for the same SDE by constructing a novel Lyapunov function and demonstrate the transparency of analyzing policy gradient using the tools in SDEs. In addition, the same Lyapunov function is also helpful in analyzing the discrete-time policy gradient algorithm.

</details>


### [118] [The Parts Are Greater Than the Sum: Automated Task Sequencing for Efficient Training of Multi-Policy LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29601)
*Jiajia Tang,Sizhe Yuen,Francisco Gomez Medina,Yali Du,Adam Sobey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种自动多策略PEFT架构，通过任务分组和排序，在固定参数预算下将兼容的适配路径组织为独立的量化LoRA（QLoRA），使异构任务在解耦的优化空间中训练，从而减少干扰和灾难性遗忘。在TRACE基准上达到44.78的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PEFT使用单一共享LoRA适配器，在异构任务序列上优化时容易产生干扰，导致迁移效果差和灾难性遗忘。现有方法通过增加参数容量或组合多个适配器来提升表达能力，但仍依赖共享的优化路径，未能从根本上解决问题。

Method: 提出一种优化路径组织框架，实现为自动多策略PEFT架构。在固定参数预算下，通过任务分组和任务排序自动组织优化兼容的适配路径，并将这些路径实现为独立的量化低秩适配器（QLoRA），使异构任务在解耦的适配空间中优化，同时保留兼容任务间的正迁移。

Result: 在TRACE基准上的实验表明，从传统的单策略PEFT到多策略PEFT性能持续提升，提出的自动多策略框架在相同可训练容量下达到了44.78的最佳性能。

Conclusion: 优化路径的组织比单纯增加适配器容量对异构参数高效微调更有效，为PEFT提供了新的设计方向。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) commonly adapts large language models using a single shared Low-Rank Adapter (LoRA). This shared optimization space often suffers from interference when adapting heterogeneous task sequences, leading to poor transfer and catastrophic forgetting. Existing approaches mainly improve adapter expressiveness by increasing parameter capacity or composing multiple adapters, yet they still rely on a shared optimization path. In this paper, we propose an optimization-path organization framework for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, implemented as an automatic multi-policy PEFT architecture. Specifically, optimization-compatible adaptation paths are automatically organized through task grouping and task sequencing under a fixed parameter budget. The organized optimization paths are implemented as independent Quantized Low-Rank Adapters (QLoRA), enabling heterogeneous tasks to be optimized in decoupled adaptation spaces while preserving positive transfer among compatible tasks. Experiments on the TRACE benchmark demonstrate that performance consistently improves from conventional single-policy PEFT to multi-policy PEFT, with the proposed automatic multi-policy framework achieving the best performance of 44.78 under the same trainable capacity. This suggests that optimization-path organization is more effective than simply increasing adapter capacity for heterogeneous parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [119] [A Human-Centered Validation of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29614)
*Christian Oliva,Luis F. Lago-Fernández*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的可解释性评估指标EPC分数，用于量化解释质量，通过平衡特征选择稀疏性与模型性能保持之间的权衡，并在表格、文本和图像多模态数据上验证其有效性，且与人类解释高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的深度学习应用需要可信的XAI，但客观评估解释保真度以及使XAI指标与人类中心理解对齐仍是关键挑战。

Method: 提出EPC分数的扩展版本，这是一种模型无关的指标，通过显式平衡特征选择稀疏性与模型性能保持之间的权衡来量化解释质量，并在表格、文本和图像三种模态上进行实证验证。

Result: EPC分数能有效揭示网络激活、数据维度和解释器性能之间的操作依赖关系；同时，更高的EPC分数与人类词汇情感判断和空间视觉注释强烈对齐。

Conclusion: EPC分数作为一种模型无关的评估指标，不仅能够客观量化解释质量，还能与人类中心的理解方式保持一致，为XAI的信任评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of deep learning models in high-risk domains has intensified the need for trustworthy Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). However, objectively evaluating explanation fidelity and aligning XAI metrics with human-centered understanding remain critical open challenges. In this work, we propose a model-agnostic metric, the EPC score, which is an extension of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient (EPC), that quantifies explanation quality by explicitly balancing the trade-off between feature selection sparsity and preserved model performance. Through an empirical validation across tabular, text, and image modalities, we show that the EPC score effectively uncovers operational dependencies among network activations, data dimensionality, and explainer performance. Furthermore, we validate the EPC score against independent human-based explanations, proving that higher EPC scores strongly align with human lexical sentiment judgments and spatial visual annotations.

</details>


### [120] [When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29617)
*Luca Viano,Antoine Moulin,Audrey Huang,Volkan Cevher,Philip Amortila,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OVI的交互式在线模仿学习算法，其核心发现是：通过与专家进行交互可以放宽学习者的表征需求——只需能够表达专家的价值函数，而无需直接表达专家的策略本身。论文还给出了一个否定性结果，证明在没有额外假设的情况下，离线模仿学习算法必须依赖于专家策略类的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习中的行为克隆（BC）存在复合错误和性能瓶颈，尤其是当学习者无法完美表征专家策略时（如蒸馏场景）。虽然已知两种干预措施（交互式查询和价值函数估计）可以提升性能，但对其背后的原理及两者间的交互关系缺乏深入理解。

Method: 提出OVI（交互式在线IL算法），利用专家交互来放松对学习者表征能力的要求——只需实现专家的价值函数而非策略本身。算法在统计上高效（只需价值函数可表达性），在计算上高效（只需线性最大化预言机）。同时通过理论分析给出离线IL的否定性结果。

Result: OVI在多个基准上优于离线策略方法（BC）、交互式策略方法（DAgger）和离线基于价值的IL方法，且当学习者网络表达能力显著弱于专家时，性能提升最为明显。

Conclusion: 专家交互能够将模仿学习的表征需求从"策略可实现性"放宽为"价值函数可实现性"，这使得在蒸馏等实际场景下（学习者表达能力受限）仍能实现统计高效的学习。同时证明了交互的必要性，离线方法在缺乏额外假设时无法摆脱专家策略类的复杂度瓶颈。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL)---training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations---underpins applications from robotics to language model training. Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert's policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). Two interventions are widely understood empirically to improve performance: querying the expert interactively along the learner's own trajectories, and using value function estimation en route to generating a policy rather than directly fitting the expert's full action distribution.
  We investigate the nature of these improvements and their potentially surprising interplay. Our main finding is that expert interaction relaxes the representational demands on the learner: one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert's value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert's policy itself. Concretely, we introduce OVI, an interactive on-policy IL algorithm that is statistically efficient whenever the learner can represent the expert's value function and computationally efficient given access to a linear maximization oracle. We complement this with a negative result showing that interaction is necessary. Namely, without stronger assumptions beyond expert-value realizability alone, any offline IL algorithm must scale with the complexity of the expert policy class. Our findings bear out empirically. OVI outperforms offline policy-based (BC), interactive policy-based (DAgger), and offline value-based IL methods, with the largest gains when the learner network is substantially less expressive than the expert's.

</details>


### [121] [CENDRe: Concept Extraction with Natural Domain Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29621)
*Antonia Holzapfel,Andres Felipe Posada Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CENDRe，一种面向CNN时间序列分类的概念提取方法，通过两阶段聚类自动确定概念数量，并利用梯度传播在时域和频域同时对概念进行定位，解决了现有方法仅限时域、预定义概念数和定位不准的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列概念提取方法存在三个局限：仅从时域角度分析而忽略频域特征、需要预定义概念数量、定位结果与模型实际使用的区域不一致。在关键领域（如故障诊断）中部署CNN需要更全面和准确的可解释性分析。

Method: CENDRe首先通过两阶段聚类（使用silhouette-guided聚合自动选择概念数量）对每个时间步的潜在表示进行概念发现；然后通过对比潜在表示与原型的presence score梯度进行时域定位；这些梯度通过可微的可逆映射（如傅里叶变换）传播，获得频域中的概念定位；最后为每个概念计算对各类别的相关性评分。

Result: 在合成基准上，CENDRe的表示正确性与最先进的概念提取方法相当，且重要性正确性显著更高。在真实轴承故障数据上，CENDRe成功提取出驱动模型预测的频带，这些频带位于故障诊断中常被检查的区域，提供了时域方法无法获得的模型评估证据。

Conclusion: CENDRe通过同时提供时域和频域的概念定位，克服了现有时间序列概念提取方法的局限，能够提取出与领域专业知识一致的频带特征，为关键领域中CNN模型的可靠性评估提供了更全面的支持。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are widely used for time-series classification, but their deployment in critical domains requires understanding the temporal and spectral patterns that drive their predictions. Concept extraction (CE) methods identify such patterns by analyzing representations within the models' latent space. However, existing time-series CE methods have three limitations: they operate only in the time domain and overlook frequency features, predefine the number of concepts, and produce localizations misaligned with the regions the model uses. We address these limitations by proposing CENDRe, a concept extraction method for CNNs. It first discovers concepts by clustering per-timestep latent representations in two stages, where silhouette-guided aggregation selects the number of concepts automatically. Then, it localizes each concept through gradients of a presence score that contrasts the latent representations with their prototypes, producing masks that concentrate on the regions driving the concept. These gradients, propagated through a differentiable invertible mapping of the input such as a Fourier transform, yield localizations for the same concepts in the frequency domain. Finally, each concept receives a relevance score that quantifies its contribution to each class. On synthetic benchmarks, CENDRe achieves representation correctness comparable to state-of-the-art CE methods and significantly higher importance correctness. On real bearing-fault data, CENDRe extracts the frequency bands driving the model's predictions, located in regions commonly inspected for fault diagnosis, producing evidence to assess the model that time-domain CE methods cannot.

</details>


### [122] [Freeze, Then Select: Structured Field Adapters and Stability-Validated Weak Selection for PDE Discovery from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29665)
*Juncheng Zhong,Chenghuang Shen,Jianfeng Liu,Zhengdong Xiao,Longjiu Luo,Qianrong Wang,Wenjun Xu,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"先冻结后选择"（freeze-then-select）的耦合方法，用于从稀疏观测中发掘偏微分方程（PDE），通过结构化场适配器与稳定性验证的弱选择（SVWS）将方程选择与神经优化解耦，在六个稀疏MDBench基准上取得了最高的精确支撑恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏观测中进行PDE发现需要同时重构连续场并选择正确的微分项，但耦合神经PDE发现中的优化路径表现出三种行为：精确支撑可能持续到训练结束、仅短暂出现或完全无法涌现。这暴露了方程选择与神经优化之间的耦合问题，需要解耦以提升PDE发现的可靠性和准确性。

Method: 方法包含两步：（1）训练一个无需PDE残差的结构化场适配器，将场分解为学习的空间特征和由三次样条表示的时间系数；（2）冻结场后，使用稳定性验证的弱选择（SVWS）通过独立弱形式系统识别重复项、重新拟合候选支撑，并在保留的弱形式系统上选择最终方程。此外，将相同原理扩展到遗传编程生成的表达式库，用于恢复未知非线性扩散函数的幂律形式。

Result: 在全部六个稀疏MDBench基准中，该方法取得了最高的精确支撑恢复率，尤其在具有挑战性的Kuramoto-Sivashinsky动力学上相比经典和神经基线优势最为明显。同时能从稀疏含噪观测中恢复未知非线性扩散函数的幂律形式。

Conclusion: 通过将场重构与方程选择解耦，"冻结后选择"方法有效解决了耦合神经PDE发现中优化路径不稳定导致支撑恢复失败的问题，在稀疏观测场景下显著提升了PDE发现的准确性和泛化能力，并具有超越固定函数库的扩展性。

Abstract: PDE discovery from sparse observations requires reconstructing a continuous field and selecting the correct differential terms. Our analysis of optimization paths in coupled neural PDE discovery reveals three behaviors: the exact support can persist to the end of training, appear only transiently, or fail to emerge. To decouple equation selection from neural optimization, we develop a freeze-then-select method combining a structured field adapter with Stability-Validated Weak Selection (SVWS). Trained from observations without a PDE residual, the adapter factorizes the field into learned spatial features and temporal coefficients represented by cubic splines. After freezing the field, SVWS identifies recurrent terms across independent weak-form systems, refits candidate supports, and selects the final equation on held-out weak-form systems. Beyond fixed libraries, we apply the same principle to expressions generated by genetic programming and recover the power-law form of an unknown nonlinear diffusion function from sparse, noisy observations. Across all six sparse MDBench regimes, our method attains the highest exact support recovery rate, with its clearest gains over classical and neural baselines on challenging Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [LayoutBench: Performance Benchmarking of Cloud Storage Layouts for Multimedia Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28880)
*Debopam Sanyal,Hongjie Chen,Alexey Tumanov,Joshua Kimball*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LayoutBench是首个专门评估多媒体数据存储布局在云对象存储中检索性能的基准测试工具，系统比较了三种布局策略（单独对象存储、tar归档打包、Parquet列式存储）在检索时间、数据传输量和成本方面的表现，发现tar归档在常规场景下延迟最低，而Parquet在超大检索中速度最快但成本和数据转移量显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多媒体机器学习工作负载将大规模数据集存储在云对象存储服务（如AWS S3）中，存储布局直接影响数据检索的速度和成本。然而现有基准测试主要针对数据库引擎和查询处理，缺乏对多媒体数据检索场景下不同存储布局的系统性评估。

Method: 提出LayoutBench基准测试框架，评估三种代表性存储布局策略：L1（每个样本单独存储为一个对象）、L2（将样本顺序打包为tar归档）、L3（将样本按列组织为Parquet文件）。使用11种不同结果集大小的查询，在ImageNet数据集上，跨越六个不同网络带宽和内存层的AWS EC2实例配置，测量检索时间、数据传输量和货币成本。

Result: L2通过连接复用实现比L1和L3更低的延迟，但在检索规模非常大时优势消失；L3在超大规模检索中速度最快，但由于行组粒度问题在所有查询规模下传输的数据量都显著更多，且需要更多内存；在所有布局中数据传输成本主导总支出，L3的成本比L1或L2高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 不同存储布局在多媒体数据检索中各有优劣：tar归档（L2）适合常规规模的检索场景，Parquet（L3）适合超大规模检索但对资源要求高且成本昂贵。研究填补了多媒体数据存储布局基准测试的空白，为云存储中的多媒体数据布局决策提供了系统性的评估依据。

Abstract: Modern multimedia machine learning workloads increasingly store large-scale datasets in cloud object storage services such as AWS S3. How these samples are physically organized in storage (i.e.,storage layout) directly affects how quickly and cheaply they can be retrieved. Yet the benchmarks used to guide storage decisions today focus on database engines and query processing, and none systematically evaluates how different storage layouts perform for multimedia data retrieval. We present LayoutBench, the first benchmark designed to fill this gap. It evaluates three representative layout strategies: storing each sample as an individual object (L1), sequentially packing samples into tar archives (L2), and organizing samples as columns in Parquet files (L3). We measure retrieval time, data transferred, and monetary cost using 11 queries of varying result-set sizes on ImageNet across six AWS EC2 instance configurations that span different network bandwidth and memory tiers. Our experiments reveal that L2 achieves lower latency than L1 and L3 through connection reuse, but loses this advantage as retrieval sizes become very large. L3 is the fastest for very large retrievals but transfers substantially more data across all query sizes due to row-group granularity, and requires significantly more memory. Across all layouts, data transfer cost dominates total expenditure, with L3 costing an order of magnitude more than L1 or L2.

</details>


### [124] [DeltaServe: Host-Agnostic Co-Serving of Inference and Fine-Tuning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28848)
*Jiaxuan Chen,Jianshu She,Ye Yuan,Rajat Ghosh,Karan Gupta,Qirong Ho,Xue Liu,Oana Balmau*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DeltaServe是一个与宿主机无关的共生设计方案，将LLM推理服务的空闲GPU算力转化为LoRA微调吞吐，同时保持推理服务级目标（SLO）。它通过紧凑的钩子接口集成现有推理引擎，利用推理预填充与LoRA微调前向传播的共享执行结构，并使用SLO感知调度器在推理有足够余量时才准入和执行微调任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务系统按峰值负载配置资源以满足严格的延迟目标，导致流量低于峰值时大量GPU算力闲置。现有系统未能有效利用这部分空闲算力进行其他有用的计算任务（如模型微调）。

Method: DeltaServe采用宿主无关的共设计方法：(1) 通过紧凑的钩子接口集成现有推理引擎（仅需多LoRA批处理支持）；(2) 利用推理预填充和LoRA微调前向传播的共享执行结构；(3) 使用SLO感知调度器，基于离线校准、在线精调的CUDA图感知延迟模型来准入和执行微调任务。

Result: 在Company X的生产轨迹上，DeltaServe在vLLM上比LLMStation提供2.9倍更高的微调吞吐量，且推理SLO合规率为100%（LLMStation仅为85%）；比vLLM+torchtune基线提高39%的微调吞吐量，无需额外硬件且保持完全SLO合规。

Conclusion: DeltaServe证明了可以在不牺牲推理服务质量的前提下，将闲置的推理算力高效转化为LoRA微调能力，且具有较好的通用性（已集成vLLM、SGLang和S-LoRA等主流推理引擎）。

Abstract: LLM serving systems are provisioned for peak load to meet strict latency targets, leaving substantial GPU compute idle whenever traffic falls below peak. We present DeltaServe, a host-agnostic co-serving design that converts this idle inference capacity into LoRA fine-tuning throughput while preserving inference service-level objectives (SLOs). DeltaServe integrates with existing inference engines through a compact hook interface that requires only multi-LoRA batching support. It exploits the shared execution structure of inference prefill and LoRA fine-tuning forward passes, and uses an SLO-aware scheduler to admit and execute fine-tuning only when sufficient inference headroom is available. The scheduler is driven by a CUDA-graph-aware latency model calibrated offline and refined online. We integrate DeltaServe with vLLM, SGLang, and S-LoRA. On a production trace from Company X, DeltaServe on vLLM delivers 2.9x higher fine-tuning throughput than LLMStation at 100% inference SLO compliance, versus 85% for LLMStation. It also achieves 39% higher fine-tuning throughput than a baseline running vLLM+torchtune, using no additional hardware and maintaining full SLO compliance.

</details>


### [125] [Rethinking AI Cloud Infrastructure for Agentic Serving Systems with the Aries Experimentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29069)
*Leonid Kondrashov,Hongrui Liu,JooYoung Park,Boxi Zhou,Zonghao Liu,Chengzhi Lu,Riccardo Mancini,Esha Choukse,Haris Javaid,German Sviridov,Tao Peng,Chen Zhao,Anastasia Avdeeva,Aleksei Gusev,Marios Kogias,Luo Mai,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Aries，一个全栈实验框架，用于研究自主智能体（agent）场景下的LLM服务系统。通过分离任务语义与执行配置、重构跨组件轨迹数据、提供统一的工具执行接口，作者在开源harness和生产环境trace上开展了可复现实验，揭示了token-centric指标、上下文保留和工具沙箱资源管理三方面的关键发现，并提出了面向agent原生服务系统的愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体改变了传统LLM服务的模式——它们需要反复推理、保持持久上下文并进行沙箱化的工具执行，这与经典的LLM服务假设（单次短请求、无状态）存在根本差异，因此需要专门的研究和实验基础设施。

Method: Aries框架包含三个核心组件：(1)将任务语义与执行配置解耦；(2)重建跨组件的agent执行轨迹，并关联系统遥测数据；(3)通过统一接口在不同沙箱子系统上暴露有状态的工具执行。实验结合了开源agent harness/benchmark的可复现实验，以及来自商业平台的生产trace验证。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1)以token为中心的性能指标会遗漏推理之外的性能瓶颈（如工具执行、上下文管理等）；(2)保留更多上下文在准确性上的收益递减，但会显著降低服务容量；(3)工具沙箱呈现"长空闲+短资源突发"的交替模式，而基于快照的状态管理使激进挂起策略代价高昂。安全分析还表明需要缩小沙箱攻击面。

Conclusion: 作者提出面向agent原生服务系统的愿景，核心方向包括：基于轨迹级别的度量体系、自适应上下文管理、弹性沙箱资源管理，以及最小化攻击面的沙箱设计。

Abstract: Autonomous agents challenge conventional LLM serving by coupling repeated inference with persistent context and sandboxed tool execution. We present Aries, a full-stack experimentation framework that separates task semantics from execution configurations, reconstructs cross-component agent trajectories with correlated system telemetry, and exposes stateful tool execution through a consistent interface across heterogeneous sandbox substrates. We use Aries to conduct reproducible experiments on open agent harnesses and benchmarks. We complement these experiments with production traces from a commercial platform, grounding low-level systems research in observed production behavior. Our results show that (1) token-centric metrics miss non-inference bottlenecks, (2) retaining additional context yields diminishing accuracy benefits while reducing serving capacity, and (3) tool sandboxes alternate between long idle periods and short resource bursts, while current snapshot-based state management makes aggressive suspension costly. A complementary security analysis further highlights the need to reduce the sandbox attack surface. We then discuss the vision for agent-native serving systems designed around trajectory-level metrics, adaptive context management, elastic sandbox resource management, and sandboxes with minimized attack surface.

</details>


### [126] [Knox: Fortifying Smart Spaces With Safety Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29198)
*Rishabh Menezes,Jadon T. Schuler,Kaimeng Zhu,Oliver Rogalski,Indranil Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Knox系统，这是首个能够在IoT智能空间中实现安全检测的系统，通过新的安全规范语言和两种静态安全检测算法，在NP-hard问题背景下提供了高效且可解释的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能空间和建筑中的IoT设备是一类新兴的分布式系统，具有关键的安全需求，但目前缺乏系统的安全检测机制。

Method: Knox提出了一种新的安全子句语言用于描述智能空间中的安全规范，并设计了两种静态验证算法来验证多个例程中的多个安全属性。由于该问题是NP-hard问题，作者提出了新颖且可解释的算法，并加入了运行时优化。

Result: 真实数据集上的分析和实验结果表明，与基线方法相比，Knox显著减少了检测时间，同时在捕捉安全违规方面具有高准确性。

Conclusion: Knox是首个实现智能空间IoT安全静态检测的系统，在效率和准确性之间取得了良好平衡，为该领域提供了可行的解决方案和算法基础。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) devices in smart spaces and buildings are an emerging class of distributed systems with critical safety requirements. This paper presents Knox, the first system to enable safety checking in IoT-enabled smart spaces. Knox's contributions include (i) safety specifications: a new language for safety clauses in such smart spaces, and (ii) static safety checking: two new algorithms for static verification of multiple safety properties across multiple routines running inside a smart space. Since the latter problem is NP-hard, we present and analyze novel and explainable algorithms for the static version of the problem. We also present optimizations that further reduce runtime. Our analysis and experimental results with real datasets show that Knox reduces checking time significantly compared to baselines, while providing high accuracy in catching safety violations.

</details>


### [127] [Allocation Tracking and Parameter Checking for Parallel Programming Models using Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29303)
*Yussur Mustafa Oraji,Christian Bischof*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoVer框架合同语言的扩展，引入通用参数检查和分配跟踪机制，在不牺牲通用性的前提下扩大可验证的错误类别范围，并证明其分析准确性在多种编程语言中保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能计算程序的正确性检查工具通常局限于特定并行编程模型（如MPI或OpenSHMEM）。CoVer框架虽已通过基于合同的通用方法解决了部分问题，但其有效性受限于底层合同语言的表达能力，无法覆盖更广泛的错误类型。

Method: 扩展CoVer的合同语言，引入通用参数检查和分配跟踪两种新特性，同时保持跨编程模型和跨语言的通用性。

Result: 评估表明分析准确性在多种语言间保持一致，验证了框架的通用适用性。虽然引入的运行时分析带来了一定的性能开销，但显著提升了CoVer的实用性和准确性。

Conclusion: 通过合同语言的扩展，CoVer能够在保持通用性的同时检查更广泛的错误类别，以可接受的性能开销换取显著的准确性提升，增强了框架的整体实用性。

Abstract: Correctness checking tools for High-Performance Computing programs are typically limited to specific parallel programming models such as MPI or OpenSHMEM. The CoVer framework previously addressed this by introducing a generic, contract-based approach that decoupled API requirements from the core tool. However, CoVer's effectiveness remains bounded by the expressiveness of its underlying contract language, restricting the types of errors it can verify. This paper presents an extension to the CoVer contract language designed to capture and check a broader range of error classes. Our extensions introduce generic parameter checking and allocation tracking, while keeping generality across both programming model and language. We evaluate these extensions and demonstrate that analysis accuracy remains consistent across multiple languages, reinforcing the framework's general applicability. While the additional runtime analyses naturally incur a performance overhead, these improvements greatly enhance CoVer's utility with a significant accuracy improvement.

</details>


### [128] [CARA: Exact Local Repair with Fresh One-Action Certification for Cloud Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29465)
*Xiyang Zhang,Yuanhe Tian,Hongzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Certificate-Aligned Recomposition (CARA)，一种基于驻留锚定的模拟器放置流水线，将自适应提议生成与一次性部署决策分离，在受限双主机邻域内通过可容许边界精确恢复top-P不同动作，并在新配对认证下以≤0.05的假阳性概率保证四向改进，128个合成环境中全面改善所有五项评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模拟器的放置流水线会检查大量修复方案，但只有在多个可靠性标准同时改善时才部署。复用搜索场景测试所选操作会使名义证据失效，而标量化可能牺牲最弱标准，需要一种既能精确审计提议恢复又对选择过程稳健的认证方案。

Method: CARA采用驻留锚定管道，将自适应提议生成与一次性部署决策分离。在受限双主机邻域中，利用打包特定的可容许边界在混合配对二值/固定赌注证书顺序下恢复精确的top-P不同动作。使用保留视图选择一个动作并在新配对认证之前提交其投注计划。在符号独立性和条件矩假设下控制假阳性概率。

Result: 在前瞻性冻结研究中，覆盖128个独立合成环境和四个重复上下文，完整的失败关闭终端策略在所有环境中对所有五个评估端点均改善了开发选择的同主机数量驻留。J降低了3.57个百分点，连续负担降低21-28%。匹配排序敏感性产生低于5.3×10⁻⁴的绝对平均差距，未建立排序优越性。

Conclusion: CARA将精确可审计的提议恢复与选择稳健的新鲜认证相结合，用于最后一公里的局部修复，在保证统计有效性的同时实现了端到端的全面性能改进。

Abstract: Simulator-based placement pipelines may inspect many repairs but deploy only when several reliability criteria improve together. Reusing search scenes to test the selected action invalidates nominal evidence, while scalarization can trade away the weakest criterion. We introduce Certificate-Aligned Recomposition (CARA), an incumbent-anchored pipeline that separates adaptive proposal generation from a one-use deployment decision. In a bounded two-host neighborhood, a packing-specific admissible bound recovers the exact top-$P$ distinct actions under a mixed paired-binary/fixed-bet certificate order. Held-out views then select one action and commit its betting plans before fresh paired Certification opens. Under the stated sign-independence and conditional-moment assumptions, the probability of falsely declaring four-way improvement is at most $.05$, irrespective of the size or complexity of upstream search. In a prospectively frozen study over 128 independent synthetic environments and four repeated contexts, the complete fail-closed terminal policy improved a development-selected same-host-count incumbent on all five Evaluation endpoints in every environment. $J$ fell by 3.57 percentage points and the continuous burdens by 21--28%. A matched ordering sensitivity produced absolute mean gaps below $5.3\times10^{-4}$ and does not establish order superiority. CARA thus couples exact auditable proposal recovery to selection-robust fresh certification for last-mile local repair.

</details>


### [129] [System-Wide Termination in Distributed Betweenness Centrality Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29474)
*Siamak Abdi,Lucia Cavallaro,Giuseppe Di Fatta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分布式介数中心性计算的轻量级全局终止检测算法，使各顶点能够在局部判断整个系统是否已收敛，避免由于各顶点独立提前停止导致的错误结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模网络上计算介数中心性代价高昂，现有分布式实现在缺乏轻量级的系统级终止检测机制，导致本地收敛后产生额外消息开销，或配置不当导致过早停止，影响计算准确性。

Method: 提出一种轻量级的系统级全局终止检测算法，使各顶点能够基于局部信息判断全局收敛状态。通过自定义Python模拟器，在合成网络（Erdos-Renyi和几何网络）及真实网络（Email和Road）上，将全局终止检测与局部停止策略进行对比实验。

Result: 全局终止检测方法在测试网络上实现了零最终误差，使顶点能够安全停止；而局部停止策略在异构网络上会导致过早终止并产生一定误差。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了在分布式中心性计算中协调停止（全局终止检测）的必要性，证明了系统级终止检测相比局部独立停止策略更为可靠和准确。

Abstract: Computing betweenness centrality on large networks is inherently expensive, as it requires aggregating shortest-path dependencies across all pairs of vertices and becomes increasingly difficult to scale as network size grows. Scalable distributed algorithms can facilitate such computations, particularly when centralised processing is not feasible, and message exchanges must be carefully controlled, for example, in bandwidth-limited or very large-scale networks. However, existing distributed betweenness centrality implementations do not integrate a lightweight, system-wide termination detector. As a consequence, this can lead to extra messaging after local convergence or, if misconfigured, premature stops. In this work, a lightweight, system-wide global termination detection algorithm for this task is presented. The proposed method enables vertices to decide locally when the overall system has converged. The method is evaluated against a local stopping strategy in which vertices terminate individually once their own estimates stabilise. To compare these two approaches, namely global termination detection and local stopping, a custom Python simulator is implemented, and both approaches are tested on synthetic (Erdos-Renyi and geometric) and real (Email and Road) network topologies. Our results show that system-wide termination detection lets vertices stop safely after detecting global convergence, as indicated by zero final error in the evaluated networks, rather than stopping independently based only on local convergence. The local stopping approach, on the other hand, results in premature termination and some errors on heterogeneous networks. This work emphasises the need for coordinated halting in distributed centrality computation.

</details>


### [130] [SLIM: Saturation-Aware Lightweight Performance Modeling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29575)
*Pol G. Recasens,Ferran Agullo,Yue Zhu,Chen Wang,Jordi Torres,Josep Ll. Berral*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过GPU硬件剖析揭示了LLM解码阶段吞吐饱和的根本原因在于注意力核的DRAM带宽限制（而非单纯批大小增加），并提出了BCA（批处理配置顾问）和SLIM（饱和感知轻量性能模型）来优化批处理配置、预测吞吐和延迟，在满足延迟约束的同时可避免高达55GB的GPU内存分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中增大批大小虽然能提升吞吐量，但性能最终会达到平台期，继续增大批大小只会增加延迟和GPU内存消耗。以往研究将这一现象归因于HBM/DRAM带宽限制，但缺乏基于详细硬件剖析的实证证据。作者希望从底层硬件层面揭示吞吐饱和的真正机制，并为实际部署提供可操作的优化建议。

Method: 本文首先使用硬件性能剖析技术对GPU进行详细表征，精确定位吞吐饱和来源于解码阶段的注意力核，发现其算术强度几乎恒定导致了DRAM带宽饱和。在此基础上，提出了BCA（Batching Configuration Advisor）用于选择满足延迟约束的最高吞吐批处理配置，以及SLIM（Saturation-Aware Lightweight Performance Model）半解析性能模型，基于Transformer计算量和内存流量的解析公式来预测推理吞吐和延迟。

Result: BCA能够识别出在评估的OPT模型中最多可避免55GB的GPU内存分配，同时吞吐损失极小。SLIM在评估的多种场景下优于代表性的性能建模基线方法，并能成功泛化到未见过的运行条件。

Conclusion: 本文通过详细的硬件剖析首次实证证明了LLM解码阶段吞吐饱和的根本原因是注意力核的DRAM带宽限制，而非简单的批大小增大。提出的BCA和SLIM工具为LLM推理服务提供了实用的性能优化和预测手段，能够在不牺牲吞吐的前提下显著节省GPU内存资源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving commonly increases batch size to improve throughput, but performance eventually reaches a deployment-dependent plateau beyond which larger batches provide marginal gains while increasing latency and GPU memory consumption. Previous studies have attributed this behavior to HBM/DRAM bandwidth limitations, but the underlying causes have primarily been supported by conceptual arguments or high-level performance observations. As our first contribution, we present a detailed GPU characterization using hardware profiling techniques, demonstrating that throughput saturation originates in the attention kernels during the decode phase. Specifically, we show that their nearly constant arithmetic intensity as active-context lengths increases -not merely larger batch sizes- drives DRAM-bandwidth saturation, while the achieved compute throughput remains far below the hardware limit. Building on this analysis, we present the Batching Configuration Advisor (BCA), which selects the highest-throughput batching configuration satisfying a target latency constraint and identifies up to 55 GB of GPU memory allocation that can be avoided for the evaluated OPT models with minimal throughput loss. To enable these recommendations, we introduce SLIM (Saturation-Aware Lightweight Performance Model), a semi-analytical model that predicts LLM inference throughput and latency from analytical formulations of Transformer computation and memory traffic. Across the evaluated scenarios, SLIM outperforms representative performance-modeling baselines while successfully generalizing to previously unseen operating conditions.

</details>
